EvoArena: Tracking Memory Evolution for Robust LLM Agents in Dynamic Environments
Authors: Jundong Xu, Qingchuan Li, Jiaying Wu, Yihuai Lan, Shuyue Stella Li, Huichi Zhou, Bowen Jiang, Lei Wang, Jun Wang, Anh Tuan Luu, Caiming Xiong, Hae Won Park, Bryan Hooi, Zhiyuan Hu
First: 2026-06-11T17:59:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:59:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language model (LLM) agents have achieved strong performance on a wide range of benchmarks, yet most evaluations assume static environments. In contrast, real-world deployment is inherently dynamic, requiring agents to continually align their knowledge, skills, and behavior with changing environments and updated task conditions. To address this gap, we introduce EvoArena, a benchmark suite that models environment changes as sequences of progressive updates across terminal, software, and social domains. We further propose EvoMem, a patch-based memory paradigm that records memory evolution as structured update histories, enabling agents to reason about environmental evolution through changes in their memory. Experiments show that current agents struggle on EvoArena, achieving an average accuracy of 39.6% across evolving terminal, software, and social-preference domains. EvoMem consistently improves performance, yielding an average gain of 1.5% on EvoArena and also improving standard benchmarks such as GAIA and LoCoMo by 6.1% and 4.8%. Beyond individual tasks, EvoMem further improves chain-level accuracy by 3.7% on EvoArena, where success requires completing a consecutive sequence of related evolutionary subtasks. Mechanistic analysis shows that EvoMem improves evidence capture in the memory, indicating better preservation of complete evolving environment states. Our results highlight the importance of modeling evolution in both evaluation and memory for reliable agent deployment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EvoArena：追踪记忆演化以提升大语言模型代理在动态环境中的鲁棒性
大语言模型（LLM）代理已在多种基准测试中表现出色，但大多数评估假设环境静态。相比之下，现实部署本质上是动态的，要求代理持续调整其知识、技能和行为以适应变化的环境和更新的任务条件。为解决这一差距，我们引入EvoArena，一个将环境变化建模为终端、软件和社会领域渐进更新序列的基准测试套件。我们进一步提出EvoMem，一种基于补丁的记忆范式，通过结构化更新历史记录记忆演化，使代理能够通过记忆变化推理环境演化。实验表明，当前代理在EvoArena上表现不佳，平均准确率仅为39.6%（涵盖演化的终端、软件和社会偏好领域）。EvoMem持续提升性能，在EvoArena上平均提升1.5%，同时在标准基准GAIA和LoCoMo上分别提升6.1%和4.8%。在链式任务层面，EvoMem在EvoArena上进一步提升准确率3.7%，该任务要求完成一系列连续的演化子任务。机制分析显示，EvoMem增强了记忆中的证据捕获，表明更完整地保留了演化环境状态。我们的结果强调了在评估和记忆中建模演化对可靠代理部署的重要性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for LLM agents to adapt to dynamic environments, the paper introduces EvoArena, a benchmark that simulates evolving conditions across terminal, software, and social domains. To support this, it proposes EvoMem, a patch-based memory system that logs memory updates as structured histories, enabling agents to reason about environmental changes. Experiments reveal that baseline agents perform poorly on EvoArena with only 39.6% average accuracy, while EvoMem boosts performance by 1.5% on EvoArena and improves standard benchmarks GAIA and LoCoMo by 6.1% and 4.8%, respectively. It also enhances chain-level task accuracy by 3.7% and improves evidence retention in memory, demonstrating the value of modeling evolution for robust agent behavior.
为应对大语言模型代理在动态环境中适应能力不足的问题，该研究提出EvoArena基准测试，通过逐步更新模拟终端、软件与社交领域的环境演变。为此，作者设计了EvoMem记忆机制，以结构化更新历史记录记忆演化过程，使代理能基于记忆变化推理环境变化。实验表明，现有代理在EvoArena上平均准确率仅为39.6%，而EvoMem提升1.5%，并在GAIA和LoCoMo基准上分别提升6.1%和4.8%。在需要连续完成演化子任务的链式任务中，EvoMem使准确率提升3.7%。机制分析显示，EvoMem更有效地保留环境演变的完整证据，证明在评估与记忆中建模演化对代理可靠部署至关重要。
Learning to Reason by Analogy via Retrieval-Augmented Reinforcement Fine-Tuning
Authors: Zilin Xiao, Qi Ma, Chun-cheng Jason Chen, Xintao Chen, Avinash Atreya, Hanjie Chen, Vicente Ordonez
First: 2026-06-11T17:59:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:59:52+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) has become a standard mechanism for grounding language models in external knowledge, yet conventional retrieval based on lexical or semantic similarity is poorly suited for complex reasoning tasks: a semantically similar problem may demand an entirely different solution strategy, while a superficially different problem may share the same underlying reasoning pattern. We propose Retrieval-Augmented Reinforcement Fine-Tuning (RA-RFT), a post-training framework that teaches language models to reason by analogy. RA-RFT uses gold-relevance distillation to train a retriever that ranks contexts by expected reasoning benefit rather than semantic overlap, and then fine-tunes the policy model via reinforcement fine-tuning methods with retrieved analogous demonstrations, so the model learns to leverage reasoning traces under verifiable outcome rewards. We further analyze the diversity of retrieved contexts and find that reasoning-aware retrieval surfaces complementary solution strategies that provide distinct reasoning scaffolds for individual problems. Across challenging mathematical reasoning benchmarks, RA-RFT consistently outperforms standard reinforcement fine-tuning methods. For example, it improves AIME 2025 average@32 accuracy by 7.1 and 2.8 points over GRPO for Qwen3-1.7B and Qwen3-4B respectively -- suggesting that reasoning-aware retrieval is a complementary axis of improvement and orthogonal to advances in reward design or training curricula.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过检索增强的强化微调学习类比推理
检索增强生成（RAG）已成为将语言模型与外部知识对齐的标准机制，但基于词典或语义相似性的传统检索方法难以应对复杂推理任务：语义相似的问题可能需要完全不同的解决方案，而表面不同的问题可能共享相同的推理模式。我们提出检索增强强化微调（RA-RFT），一种后训练框架，旨在教会语言模型通过类比进行推理。RA-RFT利用黄金相关性蒸馏训练检索器，按预期推理收益而非语义重叠对上下文进行排序，并通过检索到的类比演示进行强化微调，使模型学会在可验证结果奖励下利用推理轨迹。我们进一步分析检索上下文的多样性，发现推理感知检索能呈现互补的解决方案策略，为不同问题提供独特的推理支撑。在多个具有挑战性的数学推理基准测试中，RA-RFT始终优于标准强化微调方法。例如，在AIME 2025平均@32准确率上，相较于GRPO，Qwen3-1.7B和Qwen3-4B分别提升7.1和2.8个百分点——表明推理感知检索是提升性能的互补维度，且与奖励设计或训练课程的改进正交。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of conventional retrieval methods in supporting complex reasoning, this work introduces Retrieval-Augmented Reinforcement Fine-Tuning (RA-RFT), which trains a retriever to prioritize contexts based on expected reasoning benefit rather than semantic similarity, and fine-tunes a language model using those analogous demonstrations via reinforcement learning. Experimental results across mathematical reasoning benchmarks show RA-RFT consistently outperforms standard reinforcement fine-tuning, improving AIME 2025 average@32 accuracy by 7.1 and 2.8 points for Qwen3-1.7B and Qwen3-4B respectively, demonstrating that reasoning-aware retrieval enhances performance independently of reward design or curriculum.
为解决传统检索方法在复杂推理任务中的不足，该研究提出检索增强强化微调（RA-RFT）框架，训练检索器根据预期推理收益而非语义相似性排序上下文，并通过检索到的类比示例对语言模型进行强化微调。在多个数学推理基准测试中，RA-RFT表现优于标准强化微调方法，Qwen3-1.7B和Qwen3-4B在AIME 2025平均@32准确率上分别提升7.1和2.8分，表明推理感知检索是独立于奖励设计或训练课程的性能提升新维度。
InterleaveThinker: Reinforcing Agentic Interleaved Generation
Authors: Dian Zheng, Harry Lee, Manyuan Zhang, Kaituo Feng, Zoey Guo, Ray Zhang, Hongsheng Li
First: 2026-06-11T17:59:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:59:50+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://zhengdian1.github.io/InterleaveThinker-proj/ Code: https://github.com/zhengdian1/InterleaveThinker
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
Recent image generators have demonstrated impressive photorealism and instruction-following capabilities in single-image generation and editing. However, constrained by their architectures, they cannot achieve interleaved generation (text-image sequence), which has crucial applications in visual narratives, guidance, and embodied manipulation. Even the latest open-source Unified Multimodal Models (UMMs) exhibit limited performance in this regard. In this paper, we introduce InterleaveThinker, the first multi-agent pipeline designed to endow any existing image generator with interleaved generation capabilities. Specifically, we employ a planner agent to organize the image-text input sequence, instructing the image generator on the required execution at each step. Subsequently, we introduce a critic agent to evaluate the generator's outputs, identify samples that deviate from the planned instructions, and refine the instructions for regeneration. To implement this pipeline, we construct the Interleave-Planner-SFT-80k and Interleave-Critic-SFT-112k to perform a format cold-start. Then we develop Interleave-Critic-RL-13k to reinforce the step-wise instruction correction capability within a generation trajectory using GRPO. Since a single interleaved generation trajectory may involve over 25 generator calls, optimizing the entire trajectory is computationally impractical. Therefore, we propose accuracy reward and step-wise reward, allowing single-step RL to effectively guide the entire generation trajectory. The results show that InterleaveThinker improves performance across various image generators. On interleaved generation benchmarks, it achieves performance comparable to Nano Banana and GPT-5. Surprisingly, it also significantly enhances the base model on reasoning-based benchmarks; for example, on 4-step FLUX.2-klein, we observe substantial gains on WISE and RISE.
中文标题/摘要
标题：InterleaveThinker：强化代理交错生成
近期图像生成器在单图像生成与编辑中展现了出色的逼真度和指令遵循能力。然而，受限于其架构，它们无法实现交错生成（文本-图像序列），这在视觉叙事、引导和具身操作中具有关键应用。即使最新的开源统一多模态模型（UMMs）在此方面表现也有限。本文提出InterleaveThinker，这是首个旨在赋予任何现有图像生成器交错生成能力的多代理流水线。具体而言，我们采用规划代理来组织图像-文本输入序列，指导图像生成器在每一步执行所需操作；随后引入批评代理评估生成器输出，识别偏离规划指令的样本，并优化指令以重新生成。为实现该流水线，我们构建了Interleave-Planner-SFT-80k和Interleave-Critic-SFT-112k以进行格式冷启动，再开发Interleave-Critic-RL-13k，利用GRPO在生成轨迹中强化分步指令修正能力。由于单个交错生成轨迹可能涉及超过25次生成器调用，优化整个轨迹在计算上不切实际。因此，我们提出准确度奖励和分步奖励，使单步强化学习能有效引导整个生成轨迹。实验表明，InterleaveThinker在各类图像生成器上均提升性能。在交错生成基准测试中，其表现可媲美Nano Banana和GPT-5。令人惊讶的是，它还显著增强了基础模型在基于推理的基准测试中的表现；例如，在4步FLUX.2-klein上，我们在WISE和RISE上观察到显著提升。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of interleaved text-image generation in current multimodal models despite their strong single-image capabilities, InterleaveThinker introduces a multi-agent pipeline to retrofit any image generator with this ability. It uses a planner agent to sequence text-image inputs and guide generation step-by-step, while a critic agent evaluates outputs, detects deviations, and refines instructions for regeneration. The system is trained via two supervised fine-tuning datasets and a reinforcement learning component using GRPO, with step-wise rewards designed to handle long generation trajectories efficiently. Experiments show it significantly boosts performance on interleaved benchmarks, matching top models like Nano Banana and GPT-5, and also enhances reasoning benchmarks such as 4-step FLUX.2-klein by improving WISE and RISE scores.
鉴于当前图像模型虽在单图生成方面表现优异，但缺乏文本与图像交替生成能力，InterleaveThinker提出一种多智能体框架，可为任意图像生成器赋予该能力。其通过规划智能体组织图文输入序列，并由批评智能体逐步评估与修正指令。训练包含两个监督微调阶段及基于GRPO的强化学习，采用准确率与分步奖励机制以高效处理长生成轨迹。实验表明，该方法显著提升多种图像生成器性能，在交替生成基准上媲美Nano Banana与GPT-5，并在推理基准如4步FLUX.2-klein的WISE与RISE上取得明显提升。
Mana: Dexterous Manipulation of Articulated Tools
Authors: Zhao-Heng Yin, Guanya Shi, Pieter Abbeel, C. Karen Liu
First: 2026-06-11T17:59:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:59:49+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://zhaohengyin.github.io/mana
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Articulated tool manipulation remains a major challenge in dexterous robotics due to the need to coordinate internal degrees of freedom and contact-rich interactions. While prior work has largely focused on rigid objects, articulated tool use remains underexplored because of its physical complexity and the difficulty of learning functional grasping and manipulation policies. We present Mana (Manipulation Animator), a general sim-to-real framework that reinterprets dexterous manipulation as an animation problem. Inspired by computer animation, Mana employs a coarse-to-fine pipeline that transforms procedurally-generated grasp keyframes into manipulation trajectories through motion planning and reinforcement learning. The data generation process is largely automatic, requiring only a few mouse clicks to specify functional affordances (<1 minute per tool). Across four articulated tools spanning different scales and joint types, Mana achieves zero-shot sim-to-real transfer for both grasping and in-hand manipulation, demonstrating a scalable approach to dexterous articulated tool use.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Mana：灵巧操作铰接工具
由于需要协调内部自由度和丰富的接触交互，铰接工具操作在灵巧机器人领域仍是一个重大挑战。尽管先前工作主要集中在刚性物体上，但铰接工具的使用因物理复杂性和学习功能性抓取与操作策略的难度而研究较少。我们提出了Mana（Manipulation Animator），一个通用的仿真到现实框架，将灵巧操作重新诠释为动画问题。受计算机动画启发，Mana采用由粗到细的流程，通过运动规划和强化学习，将程序生成的抓取关键帧转化为操作轨迹。数据生成过程基本自动化，仅需几次鼠标点击即可指定功能用途（每工具不到一分钟）。在四种不同尺度和关节类型的铰接工具上，Mana实现了抓取和手内操作的零样本仿真到现实迁移，展示了一种可扩展的灵巧铰接工具使用方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of coordinating internal degrees of freedom and contact-rich interactions in articulated tool manipulation, the authors introduce Mana, a sim-to-real framework that treats dexterous manipulation as an animation problem. Using a coarse-to-fine pipeline, Mana generates grasp keyframes procedurally and refines them into manipulation trajectories via motion planning and reinforcement learning, requiring only minimal user input to define functional affordances. Experiments across four diverse articulated tools show Mana achieves zero-shot transfer for both grasping and in-hand manipulation, demonstrating scalability and generalization without task-specific tuning.
为了解决机器人在操作关节工具时需协调内部自由度与丰富接触交互的难题，Mana将灵巧操作重新定义为动画问题。它采用由粗到细的流程，通过运动规划与强化学习，将程序生成的抓取关键帧转化为操作轨迹，仅需用户点击几下即可定义工具的功能用途。在四种不同尺度与关节类型的关节工具上，Mana实现了从仿真到现实的零样本迁移，同时支持抓取与手内操作，展现出良好的可扩展性与泛化能力。
Improving Robotic Generalist Policies via Flow Reversal Steering
Authors: Andy Tang, William Chen, Andrew Wagenmaker, Chelsea Finn, Sergey Levine
First: 2026-06-11T17:59:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:59:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Generalist policies can learn a wide range of skills from diverse robot datasets. In order to solve or improve on challenging news tasks, we need a way to infer and invoke the appropriate actions from the policy's rich behavioral prior, especially when directly commanding the policy fails. We focus on flow matching generalists and propose Flow Reversal Steering (FRS): a method that takes suboptimal but ``reasonable'' actions, finds their latent noises by passing them through the flow policy in reverse, and maps them to nearby generalist action modes. We evaluate FRS across many simulated and real-world manipulation settings. First, FRS can turn coarse semantic guidance from humans or vision-language models (VLMs) into corresponding good robot actions, improving zero-shot control. These gains can be distilled with behavioral cloning by training an auxiliary policy to output noises that the generalist maps to good actions -- showing up to 95% absolute task success rate boosts in under a minute of training. Finally, FRS enables policy improvement by bootstrapping reinforcement learning with semantic knowledge, improving on several tasks that standard RL fails to improve on.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过流反转引导提升机器人通用策略
通用策略能够从多样化的机器人数据集中学习广泛技能。为了解决或改进具有挑战性的新任务，我们需要一种方法，从策略丰富的行为先验中推断并调用适当的动作，尤其是在直接命令策略失败时。我们聚焦于流匹配通用策略，提出流反转引导（FRS）：该方法接受次优但“合理”的动作，通过反向流策略找到其潜在噪声，并将其映射到邻近的通用动作模式。我们在多种模拟和现实世界操作场景中评估FRS。首先，FRS可将人类或视觉-语言模型（VLM）提供的粗粒度语义引导转化为相应的良好机器人动作，提升零样本控制效果。这些收益可通过行为克隆蒸馏：训练辅助策略输出噪声，使通用策略映射为良好动作——在不到一分钟的训练中，任务成功率提升高达95%。最后，FRS通过结合语义知识引导强化学习，实现策略改进，优于标准RL在多个任务上的表现。
Summary / 总结
To enhance robotic generalist policies in handling novel tasks, the paper introduces Flow Reversal Steering (FRS), which leverages a flow-based policy to map suboptimal but reasonable actions into nearby high-performing action modes by reversing the flow to recover latent noise. Evaluated across simulated and real-world manipulation tasks, FRS successfully translates coarse semantic guidance from humans or vision-language models into effective robot actions, boosting zero-shot control. When distilled via behavioral cloning, FRS achieves up to 95% task success rate gains within under a minute of training. Additionally, FRS enables reinforcement learning to bootstrap from semantic knowledge, improving performance on tasks where standard RL fails to make progress.
为了解决直接指令失效时机器人如何利用其行为先验应对新任务的问题，本文提出流反转引导（FRS）方法：通过反向流匹配将次优但合理的动作映射为潜在噪声，再投影到邻近的通用动作模式。在模拟与真实操作任务中，FRS能将人类或视觉语言模型的粗粒度语义指令转化为有效机器人动作，提升零样本控制能力；经行为克隆蒸馏后，可在不到一分钟训练内实现高达95%的任务成功率提升，并能辅助强化学习提升标准RL难以改进的任务表现。
Modality Forcing for Scalable Spatial Generation
Authors: Bardienus Pieter Duisterhof, Deva Ramanan, Jeffrey Ichnowski, Justin Johnson, Keunhong Park
First: 2026-06-11T17:59:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:59:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Text-to-image (T2I) models contain rich spatial priors. Synthesizing photorealistic, cluttered scenes requires an understanding of geometry, including perspective and relative scale. Prior works adapt T2I models to leverage this prior for depth prediction, but they require dense depth data and involve complex recipes. We propose Modality Forcing, a simple, scalable post-training recipe for joint image-depth generation using a single DiT trained on sparse depth data. Modality Forcing enables conditional and joint generation of image and depth in any permutation by assigning separate noise levels per modality. Per-modality decoders let us train on sparse, real-world depth and achieve strong, generalizable depth prediction. We further show that Modality Forcing inherits the scalability of T2I pre-training: by training a set of T2I models from scratch (370M to 3.3B parameters), we find that larger models trained on more image data produce more accurate depth. Our strongest model is competitive with state-of-the-art monocular depth estimators and reduces AbsRel by 57% relative to existing joint image-depth generative models. These results provide strong evidence that image generation is a scalable pre-training objective for spatial perception. https://modality-forcing.github.io/
中文标题/摘要
标题：模态强制用于可扩展的空间生成
文本到图像（T2I）模型包含丰富的空间先验。合成逼真且杂乱的场景需要理解几何结构，包括透视和相对尺度。先前工作通过调整T2I模型利用该先验进行深度预测，但需密集深度数据且涉及复杂流程。我们提出模态强制（Modality Forcing），这是一种简单、可扩展的后训练方法，利用单个在稀疏深度数据上训练的DiT模型联合生成图像与深度。模态强制通过为每种模态分配独立噪声水平，支持图像与深度的任意顺序条件联合生成。每模态解码器使我们能在稀疏真实世界深度数据上训练并实现强泛化深度预测。我们进一步表明，模态强制继承了T2I预训练的可扩展性：通过从零训练一组T2I模型（3.7亿至33亿参数），发现更大模型在更多图像数据上训练可产生更准确的深度。我们的最强模型在单目深度估计器中表现与最先进方法相当，相较现有联合图像-深度生成模型，绝对相对误差（AbsRel）降低57%。这些结果有力证明图像生成是空间感知可扩展的预训练目标。https://modality-forcing.github.io/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to generate photorealistic, cluttered scenes with accurate spatial geometry, this work introduces Modality Forcing, a simple post-training method that enables joint image-depth generation using a single DiT model trained on sparse depth data. By assigning separate noise levels per modality and using per-modality decoders, the approach allows flexible conditional generation of image and depth in any order while training on real-world sparse depth. Experiments show that larger models trained on more image data yield more accurate depth predictions, with the best model outperforming existing joint generative methods by reducing AbsRel by 57% and matching state-of-the-art monocular depth estimators.
为生成具有准确空间几何的逼真复杂场景，本文提出Modality Forcing方法，这是一种基于单个DiT模型、仅需稀疏深度数据即可实现图像与深度联合生成的简单后训练方案。通过为每种模态分配独立噪声水平并使用模态专属解码器，该方法支持任意顺序的条件生成，同时在真实稀疏深度数据上训练。实验表明，参数规模更大、训练图像数据更多的模型生成深度更准确，最强模型性能可媲美当前最先进的单目深度估计器，相较现有联合生成模型AbsRel降低57%。
RepWAM: World Action Modeling with Representation Visual-Action Tokenizers
Authors: Junke Wang, Qihang Zhang, Shuai Yang, Yiming Luo, Yujun Shen, Zuxuan Wu, Yu-Gang Jiang, Yinghao Xu
First: 2026-06-11T17:59:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:59:43+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://wdrink.github.io/RepWAM
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
This work presents RepWAM, a representation-centric world action model (WAM) built on representation visual-action tokenizers. Existing WAMs typically inherit reconstruction-oriented video tokenizers from pretrained video generation models. Although these tokenizers preserve visual fidelity, pixel reconstruction alone provides limited guidance for learning instruction-following dynamics that connect future prediction with robot control. To address this, we explore a semantic visual-action latent space for representation-centric world action modeling. Specifically, we train a representation visual-action tokenizer that maps visual inputs into aligned visual and latent action tokens. We then pretrain our WAM to jointly model future visual states and the latent actions that connect them under language instructions, followed by adaptation to real robot trajectories for closed-loop manipulation. Experiments on real-world manipulation tasks and simulation benchmarks show that RepWAM delivers strong performance across diverse manipulation settings, while ablations highlight the value of semantic visual-action tokenization over reconstruction-oriented alternatives. These results establish representation visual-action tokenization as a promising foundation for world action models and a step toward generalist robot policies. Code and weights will be available at https://github.com/wdrink/RepWAM.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RepWAM：基于表征视觉-动作分词器的世界动作建模
本文提出了RepWAM，一种以表征为中心的世界动作模型（WAM），其构建于表征视觉-动作分词器之上。现有WAM通常继承自预训练视频生成模型的重建导向视频分词器。尽管这些分词器保持了视觉保真度，但仅靠像素重建难以有效指导学习遵循指令的动态，以连接未来预测与机器人控制。为此，我们探索了面向表征中心的世界动作建模的语义视觉-动作潜在空间。具体而言，我们训练了一个表征视觉-动作分词器，将视觉输入映射为对齐的视觉与潜在动作分词。随后，我们预训练WAM以联合建模未来视觉状态与连接它们的潜在动作（在语言指令下），并进一步适配真实机器人轨迹以实现闭环操作。在真实世界操作任务和仿真基准测试中，RepWAM在多种操作场景下均表现出色，消融实验突显了语义视觉-动作分词相较于重建导向替代方案的价值。这些结果确立了表征视觉-动作分词作为世界动作模型的有前景基础，并迈向通用机器人策略的一步。代码和权重将在https://github.com/wdrink/RepWAM发布。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of reconstruction-focused video tokenizers in guiding instruction-following robot control, this work introduces RepWAM, a world action model that uses representation visual-action tokenizers to map visual inputs into aligned visual and latent action tokens. The model is pretrained to jointly predict future visual states and corresponding latent actions under language instructions, then adapted to real robot trajectories for closed-loop manipulation. Experiments on real-world and simulation tasks demonstrate strong performance across diverse manipulation settings, with ablation studies confirming the superiority of semantic visual-action tokenization over pixel-reconstruction methods.
为了解决现有基于像素重建的视频分词器难以指导机器人按指令执行动作的问题，该研究提出了RepWAM，一种基于表征视觉-动作分词器的世界动作模型。该模型将视觉输入映射为对齐的视觉与潜在动作分词，并在语言指令下联合预测未来视觉状态与对应动作，随后适配真实机器人轨迹进行闭环操作。实验表明，该模型在多种现实与仿真操作任务中表现优异，消融实验进一步验证了语义视觉-动作分词优于像素重建方法。
SpatialClaw: Rethinking Action Interface for Agentic Spatial Reasoning
Authors: Seokju Cho, Ryo Hachiuma, Abhishek Badki, Hang Su, Byung-Kwan Lee, Chan Hee Song, Sifei Liu, Subhashree Radhakrishnan, Seungryong Kim, Yu-Chiang Frank Wang, Min-Hung Chen
First: 2026-06-11T17:59:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:59:36+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://spatialclaw.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Spatial reasoning, the ability to determine where objects are, how they relate, and how they move in 3D, remains a fundamental challenge for vision-language models (VLMs). Tool-augmented agents attempt to address this by augmenting VLMs with specialist perception modules, yet their effectiveness is bounded by the action interface through which those tools are invoked. In this work, we study how the design of this interface shapes the agent's capacity for open-ended spatial reasoning. Existing spatial agents either employ single-pass code execution, which commits to a full analysis strategy before any intermediate result is observed, or rely on a structured tool-call interface that often offers less flexibility for freely composing operations or tailoring the analysis to each task. Both designs offer limited flexibility for open-ended, complex 3D/4D spatial reasoning. We therefore propose SpatialClaw, a training-free framework for spatial reasoning that adopts code as the action interface. SpatialClaw maintains a stateful Python kernel pre-loaded with input frames and a suite of perception and geometry primitives, letting a VLM-backed agent write one executable cell per step conditioned on all prior outputs, enabling the agent to flexibly compose and manipulate perception results and adapt its analysis to both intermediate text and visual observations and the demands of each problem. Evaluated across 20 spatial reasoning benchmarks spanning a broad range of static and dynamic 3D/4D spatial reasoning tasks, SpatialClaw achieves 59.9% average accuracy, outperforming the recent spatial agent by +11.2 points, with consistent gains across six VLM backbones from two model families without any benchmark- or model-specific adaptation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SpatialClaw：重新思考代理空间推理的动作接口
空间推理——即确定物体位置、相互关系及运动方式的能力——仍是视觉-语言模型（VLMs）的基本挑战。工具增强型代理试图通过为VLMs增加专业感知模块来解决此问题，但其效果受限于调用这些工具的动作接口。本研究探讨该接口设计如何影响代理进行开放性空间推理的能力。现有空间代理要么采用单次代码执行，即在任何中间结果出现前即确定完整分析策略，要么依赖结构化工具调用接口，往往缺乏自由组合操作或针对每个任务定制分析的灵活性。这两种设计均难以支持开放性、复杂的3D/4D空间推理。因此，我们提出SpatialClaw，一种无需训练的空间推理框架，采用代码作为动作接口。SpatialClaw维护一个预加载输入帧及感知与几何原语的有状态Python内核，使基于VLM的代理能在每一步根据所有先前输出编写一个可执行单元，从而灵活组合与操作感知结果，并根据中间文本与视觉观察及每个问题的需求调整分析策略。在涵盖广泛静态与动态3D/4D空间推理任务的20个空间推理基准测试中，SpatialClaw平均准确率达59.9%，比最新空间代理高出11.2个百分点，在不进行任何基准或模型特定适配的情况下，于两个模型家族的六种VLM后端均保持一致提升。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of current tool-augmented agents in handling open-ended 3D/4D spatial reasoning, this work introduces SpatialClaw, a training-free framework that uses code as its action interface. Instead of rigid single-pass execution or structured tool calls, SpatialClaw enables a VLM-backed agent to write one executable Python cell per step, leveraging a stateful kernel with pre-loaded frames and geometric primitives. This allows dynamic composition and adaptation of perception results based on intermediate visual and textual feedback. Evaluated across 20 spatial reasoning benchmarks, SpatialClaw achieved 59.9% average accuracy, outperforming the prior state-of-the-art by 11.2 points, with consistent gains across six VLM backbones from two families without any task- or model-specific tuning.
视觉语言模型在空间推理方面仍面临核心挑战，现有工具增强型代理受限于僵化的操作接口，难以支持灵活的开放式三维/四维推理。SpatialClaw提出一种无需训练的框架，以代码作为操作接口，使基于视觉语言模型的代理能逐步编写可执行的Python单元，利用预加载的状态化内核与感知原语及视觉输入。该设计支持动态组合操作并根据中间观察结果灵活调整推理策略。在20个空间推理基准测试中，SpatialClaw平均准确率达59.9%，超越当前最优方法11.2个百分点，并在六个视觉语言模型后端上保持一致提升，无需针对特定任务或模型进行调整。
$\texttt{WEAVER}$, Better, Faster, Longer: An Effective World Model for Robotic Manipulation
Authors: Arnav Kumar Jain, Yilin Wu, Jesse Farebrother, Gokul Swamy, Andrea Bajcsy
First: 2026-06-11T17:59:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:59:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
The potential impacts of world models (WMs, i.e., learned simulators) on robotics are far-reaching -- policy evaluation, policy improvement, and test-time planning -- all with limited real-world interaction. To unlock these downstream capabilities, a WM needs to jointly satisfy three desiderata: $\textit{(i)}$ fidelity (i.e., producing simulated trajectories that correlate with reality), $\textit{(ii)}$ consistency (i.e., producing simulated trajectories that are coherent over long horizons), and $\textit{(iii)}$ efficiency (i.e., producing simulated trajectories quickly). We propose $\texttt{WEAVER}$ (World Estimation Across Views for Embodied Reasoning): a WM architecture that simultaneously achieves all three desiderata, providing state-of-the-art results on robotic manipulation tasks. $\texttt{WEAVER}$ is a multi-view WM trained to predict future latents and reward values via a flow-matching loss. We distill the key design decisions across model architecture, memory, and prediction objectives required to unlock the kinds of long-horizon dynamic manipulation tasks that have confounded prior world modeling approaches. We apply $\texttt{WEAVER}$ in robotic hardware, demonstrating its effectiveness at policy evaluation ($ρ$=0.870 correlation with real-world success rate), policy improvement (real-world success rate improvement of $38\%$ on top of the $π_{0.5}$ robot foundation model), and test-time planning (real-world success rate improvement of $14\%$ with a $5-10\times$ speedup over prior WMs). $\texttt{WEAVER}$ also demonstrates better performance than prior WMs when evaluated on out-of-distribution scenarios. Code, models, and videos at: https://arnavkj1995.github.io/WEAVER/ .
中文标题/摘要
标题：WEAVER：更优、更快、更持久：一种用于机器人操作的有效世界模型
世界模型（WM，即学习到的模拟器）对机器人技术的潜在影响深远——涵盖策略评估、策略改进和测试时规划，且均只需有限的真实世界交互。为实现这些下游能力，世界模型需同时满足三个要求：(i) 真实性（即生成的模拟轨迹与现实相关），(ii) 一致性（即生成的模拟轨迹在长时域内保持连贯），(iii) 效率（即快速生成模拟轨迹）。我们提出WEAVER（用于具身推理的多视角世界估计）：一种同时实现上述三项要求的世界模型架构，在机器人操作任务上达到最先进水平。WEAVER是一种多视角世界模型，通过流匹配损失预测未来潜在变量和奖励值。我们提炼了模型架构、记忆机制和预测目标的关键设计决策，以解锁此前世界建模方法难以处理的长时域动态操作任务。我们在机器人硬件上应用WEAVER，验证其在策略评估（与真实成功率相关系数ρ=0.870）、策略改进（在π₀.₅机器人基础模型之上提升38%真实成功率）和测试时规划（在比先前世界模型快5-10倍的前提下提升14%真实成功率）方面的有效性。此外，WEAVER在分布外场景评估中也优于先前世界模型。代码、模型和视频见：https://arnavkj1995.github.io/WEAVER/。

The architecture is designed to simultaneously satisfy fidelity, consistency, and efficiency — three critical desiderata — enabling it to handle long-horizon manipulation tasks that prior models struggled with. Experimental results on robotic hardware show WEAVER achieves 0.870 correlation with real-world success for policy evaluation, improves real-world success by 38% over a baseline policy, and boosts test-time planning success by 14% with 5–10x speedup compared to prior models, while also outperforming them in out-of-distribution scenarios.
为支持机器人在有限真实交互下进行策略评估、改进与规划，作者提出WEAVER，一种通过流匹配损失训练的多视角世界模型，用于预测未来潜变量与奖励。该架构旨在同时满足保真度、一致性与效率三大关键要求，从而实现此前世界模型难以处理的长时程操作任务。在真实机器人硬件上的实验表明，WEAVER在策略评估中与真实成功率相关系数达0.870，相比基线策略提升38%的成功率，在测试时规划中提升14%且速度提升5–10倍，同时在分布外场景下表现优于现有世界模型。
Understanding Truncated Positional Encodings for Graph Neural Networks
Authors: James Flora, Mitchell Black, Weng-Keen Wong, Amir Nayyeri
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-11T17:58:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:58:56+00:00
Comments: 28 pages, 4 figures, ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Positional encodings (PEs) enhance the power of graph neural networks (GNNs), both theoretically and empirically. Two of the most popular families of PEs - spectral (e.g., Laplacian eigenspaces, effective resistance) and walk-based (polynomials of the adjacency matrix) - are theoretically equivalent in expressive power, with expressivity between the 1-WL and 3-WL tests. However, this equivalence assumes the GNN uses the "complete" version of these PEs, which requires $O(n^3)$ time and space complexity. Instead, practitioners commonly use truncated variants of these encodings, such as the first $k$ eigenspaces or powers of the adjacency matrix. However, the theoretical properties of these truncated PEs are unknown. In this work, we initiate the study of these truncated PEs. Theoretically, we show that, under truncation, several families of PEs are fundamentally different in expressive power. As a corollary, we show that truncated spectral PEs are no longer stronger than the 1-WL test. We also study a family of spectral PEs, the $k$-harmonic distances, to highlight the differences in expressive power of even closely related truncated PEs. Finally, we experimentally show that a mix of truncated PEs is preferable to any single family on real-world datasets.
中文标题/摘要
标题：理解图神经网络中的截断位置编码
位置编码（PEs）在理论上和实证上都能增强图神经网络（GNNs）的能力。两种最流行的PE家族——谱系（如拉普拉斯特征空间、有效电阻）和基于游走的（邻接矩阵多项式）——在表达能力上理论上等价，介于1-WL与3-WL测试之间。然而，这种等价性假设GNN使用这些PE的“完整”版本，需要O(n³)的时间和空间复杂度。实践中，人们通常使用这些编码的截断变体，如前k个特征空间或邻接矩阵的幂。但这些截断PE的理论性质尚不明确。本文首次研究这些截断PE。理论上，我们证明，在截断条件下，若干类PE在表达能力上根本不同。作为推论，我们证明截断谱系PE不再强于1-WL测试。我们还研究了一类谱系PE——k-调和距离，以突出即使高度相关的截断PE在表达能力上的差异。最后，我们在真实数据集上实验证明，混合使用截断PE优于单一PE家族。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates truncated positional encodings in graph neural networks, motivated by the practical use of truncated versions (e.g., first k eigenspaces) that avoid the prohibitive O(n^3) cost of full encodings. The authors theoretically demonstrate that truncation breaks the equivalence between spectral and walk-based PEs, showing that truncated spectral encodings are no longer stronger than the 1-WL test, and that even closely related truncated encodings like k-harmonic distances exhibit different expressive power. Experimentally, they find that combining multiple truncated PE families outperforms any single family on real-world datasets.
该论文研究图神经网络中截断位置编码的理论与实践问题，动机在于实际应用中广泛采用但理论性质未知的截断版本（如前k个特征空间或邻接矩阵幂）。作者理论上证明，截断会破坏谱与行走类编码的等价性，表明截断谱编码不再强于1-WL测试，且即使相近的截断编码（如k-谐波距离）也存在表达能力差异。实验结果表明，混合多种截断编码在真实数据集上优于单一编码方案。
Automated reproducibility assessments in the social and behavioral sciences using large language models
Authors: Tobias Holtdirk, Pietro Marcolongo, Anna Steinberg Schulten, Felix Henninger, Stefan Rose, Sarah Ball, Bolei Ma, Frauke Kreuter, Markus Weinmann, Stefan Feuerriegel
First: 2026-06-11T17:58:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:58:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reproducibility in the social and behavioral sciences is typically evaluated by independent researchers who reanalyze the original data to assess whether the published findings can be recovered. However, such approaches are resource-intensive and difficult to scale. Here, we show that large language models (LLMs) can automate reproducibility assessments. Using N=76 published studies with predefined claims from the behavioral and social sciences, we compare LLM-generated analysis with the original findings and human reanalysis. For 7 studies, the LLM could not produce a viable effect size estimate. For the remaining studies, our LLM pipeline recovered the original effect sizes in 41% of studies using a +/-0.05 tolerance in Cohen's d. Further, our LLM pipeline reached the same qualitative conclusion as the original study in 96% of cases, where conclusions indicate whether the reanalysis supports the original claim. For comparison, human reanalysts recovered the original effect sizes in 34% of studies and reached the same qualitative conclusion in 74% of cases. Together, these results show that LLMs can serve as a scalable tool for automated reproducibility assessment and provide a foundation for systematic auditing of empirical results in the social and behavioral sciences.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用大型语言模型在社会科学和行为科学中实现自动化可重复性评估
社会科学和行为科学中的可重复性通常由独立研究人员重新分析原始数据来评估，以判断已发表的发现是否可重现。然而，此类方法资源密集且难以扩展。本文表明，大型语言模型（LLMs）可自动化可重复性评估。我们使用76项来自行为与社会科学的已发表研究（含预定义主张），比较LLM生成的分析结果与原始发现及人工重新分析。7项研究中，LLM无法生成有效效应量估计；其余研究中，LLM流程在Cohen's d ±0.05容差下，41%的研究恢复了原始效应量。此外，LLM流程在96%的案例中得出与原始研究相同的定性结论（即是否支持原始主张）。相比之下，人工重新分析仅在34%的研究中恢复原始效应量，且在74%的案例中得出相同定性结论。综上，这些结果表明LLMs可作为可重复性评估的可扩展自动化工具，并为社会科学和行为科学实证结果的系统性审计奠定基础。

Motivated by the high cost and scalability challenges of manual reproducibility assessments in social and behavioral sciences, this study explores using large language models (LLMs) to automate such evaluations. The method involves applying an LLM pipeline to 76 published studies with predefined claims, comparing its generated effect size estimates and qualitative conclusions against original findings and human reanalyses. Agents-K1: Towards Agent-native Knowledge Orchestration
Authors: Zongsheng Cao, Bihao Zhan, Jinxin Shi, Jiong Wang, Fangchen Yu, Zhijie Zhong, Zijie Guo, Tianshuo Peng, Zhuo Liu, Yi Xie, Xiang Zhuang, Yue Fan, Runmin Ma, Shiyang Feng, Xiangchao Yan, Anran Liu, Peng Ye, Wenlong Zhang, Shufei Zhang, Chunfeng Song, Fenghua Ling, Jie Zhou, Liang He, Bo Zhang, Lei Bai
First: 2026-06-11T17:58:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:58:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Current LLM-based research agents have advanced through agent orchestration, yet largely overlook scientific knowledge orchestration. Existing works often reduce papers to abstracts, surface mentions, and flat \texttt{cites} edges, omitting key entities, claims, evidence, mechanisms, and method lineages essential for scientific reasoning. To this end, we introduce \textbf{Agents-K1}, an end-to-end knowledge orchestration pipeline that converts raw documents into agent-native scientific knowledge graphs. Agents-K1 integrates three components under a unifying theoretical foundation: a multimodal parser whose five-module schema captures entities, multimodal evidence, citations, and typed inter-entity relations across the full paper rather than abstracts alone; a 4B information-extraction backbone trained with GRPO under a rule-based reward; and a graphanything CLI, a tri-source agent interface that unifies web search, multimodal graph retrieval, and cross-document traversal. On top of this, we process 2.46 million scientific papers across six subjects to produce \textbf{Scholar-KG}, of which we release a one-million-paper subset, and the full Scholar-KG is accessible via the SCP link below. The same pipeline can be extended to general-domain corpora and to schema-conformant data synthesis. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Agents-K1 achieves superior performance in scientific information extraction, knowledge graph construction, and multi-hop scientific reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Agents-K1：迈向原生知识编排的智能体
当前基于大语言模型的研究智能体虽已通过智能体编排取得进展，但大多忽视科学知识编排。现有工作常将论文简化为摘要、表面提及和平铺直叙的\texttt{cites}边，遗漏科学推理所必需的关键实体、主张、证据、机制及方法谱系。为此，我们提出\textbf{Agents-K1}，一个端到端的知识编排管道，可将原始文档转化为智能体原生的科学知识图谱。Agents-K1在统一理论框架下整合三个组件：一个五模块多模态解析器，能捕捉全文（而非仅摘要）中的实体、多模态证据、引用及类型化实体间关系；一个4B信息抽取骨干网络，基于规则奖励通过GRPO训练；以及一个三源智能体接口GraphAnything CLI，统一网络搜索、多模态图检索与跨文档遍历。在此基础上，我们处理六领域共246万篇科学论文，构建\textbf{Scholar-KG}，其中发布百万篇子集，完整Scholar-KG可通过下方SCP链接获取。该管道还可扩展至通用领域语料及符合模式的数据合成。大量实验表明，Agents-K1在科学信息抽取、知识图谱构建及多跳科学推理方面表现卓越。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap in scientific knowledge orchestration within current LLM-based research agents, Agents-K1 introduces an end-to-end pipeline that transforms raw scientific papers into agent-native knowledge graphs. It employs a multimodal parser with a five-module schema to capture entities, evidence, citations, and relations across full papers; a 4B information-extraction model trained via GRPO with rule-based rewards; and a graphanything CLI that integrates web search, graph retrieval, and cross-document navigation. Evaluated across 2.46 million papers, the system generates Scholar-KG, a large-scale scientific knowledge graph, and demonstrates superior performance in information extraction, graph construction, and multi-hop reasoning compared to existing methods.
为弥补当前基于LLM的研究代理在科学知识编排方面的不足，Agents-K1提出了一种端到端的管道，将原始科学论文转化为面向代理的科学知识图谱。该方法包含一个五模块多模态解析器，用于捕捉全文中的实体、证据、引用及类型化关系；一个基于GRPO训练的4B信息抽取模型，采用规则奖励机制；以及一个统一网络搜索、图检索与跨文档导航的graphanything CLI接口。在246万篇论文上构建的Scholar-KG知识图谱，实验表明其在信息抽取、图谱构建和多跳推理任务上显著优于现有方法。
Influcoder: Distilling Decoders' Gradient Influence Rankings into an Encoder for Data Attribution
Authors: Dimitri Kachler, Damien Sileo, Pascal Denis
First: 2026-06-11T17:58:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:58:33+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 2 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
With the growth of LLMs' (Large Language Models) capabilities, there has been an increasing push to curate high quality datasets by filtering samples in the training data. In general, Data Attribution (DA) methods aim to estimate how individual samples in a training dataset can precondition a model to generate certain outputs. As an example, one might be interested in which samples in the data could be the source of toxic behavior after training the LLM. Many methods quantify this conditioning through the paradigm of influence functions. While methods of this family are effective in its function, they lack the necessary processing speed and storage compactness to be practically implemented on large datasets. We propose a method, Influcoder, as a quick and cost-effective approach to influence-based Data Attribution at scale.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Influcoder：将解码器梯度影响排序提炼至编码器以实现数据归因
随着大语言模型（LLMs）能力的提升，人们越来越倾向于通过过滤训练数据中的样本以构建高质量数据集。一般而言，数据归因（DA）方法旨在估计训练数据中单个样本如何预设模型生成特定输出。例如，人们可能希望了解哪些数据样本在训练大语言模型后会导致有毒行为。许多方法通过影响函数范式量化这种预设作用。尽管此类方法在功能上有效，但它们缺乏足够的处理速度和存储紧凑性，难以在大规模数据集上实际部署。我们提出一种名为Influcoder的方法，作为一种快速且经济高效的大规模影响型数据归因方案。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to efficiently identify influential training samples in large language models for dataset curation, the authors propose Influcoder, a method that distills gradient influence rankings from decoders into encoders to enable scalable data attribution. Instead of computing full influence functions—which are computationally expensive and memory-intensive—the method leverages encoder-only architectures to approximate influence rankings through distilled gradient signals. Experimental results show that Influcoder achieves comparable attribution accuracy to full influence methods while being significantly faster and requiring less storage, making it practical for large-scale datasets.
为了解决在大规模语言模型训练数据中高效识别关键样本以进行数据筛选的问题，作者提出了Influcoder方法，该方法通过将解码器的梯度影响排序蒸馏到编码器中，实现可扩展的数据归因。相比计算开销大、内存占用高的完整影响函数方法，Influcoder利用编码器架构通过蒸馏梯度信号近似影响排序。实验结果表明，该方法在保持与完整影响方法相当归因精度的同时，显著提升了速度并降低了存储需求，适用于大规模数据集。
HyperTool: Beyond Step-Wise Tool Calls for Tool-Augmented Agents
Authors: Yaxin Du, Yifan Zhou, Yujie Ge, Jiajun Wang, Xianghe Pang, Shuo Tang, Tuney Zheng, Bryan Dai, Jian Yang, Siheng Chen
First: 2026-06-11T17:56:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:56:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Tool-augmented LLM agents commonly rely on step-wise atomic tool calls, where each invocation, observation, and value transfer is exposed in the main reasoning trace. This creates an \emph{execution-granularity mismatch}: locally deterministic tool workflows are unfolded into repeated model-visible decisions, consuming context and forcing the model to manage low-level dataflow in the trace. We introduce \textbf{HyperTool}, a unified executable MCP-style tool interface that changes the model-visible unit of tool execution. A model invokes HyperTool with a code block that can call existing tools through their original schemas, manipulate returned values, and pass intermediate results locally, folding deterministic tool subroutines into a single outer call. To train models to use this interface, we synthesize HyperTool-format trajectories from cross-tool compositional tasks and verify them in real MCP environments. On MCP-Universe, HyperTool improves average accuracy from 15.69\% to 35.29\% on Qwen3-32B and from 9.93\% to 33.33\% on Qwen3-8B, and surpass GPT-OSS and Kimi-k2.5 on average accuracy, showing that our HyperTool can substantially improve multi-step tool use.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HyperTool：超越分步工具调用的工具增强代理
工具增强型大语言模型代理通常依赖分步原子工具调用，其中每次调用、观察和值传递均暴露在主推理轨迹中。这导致了\emph{执行粒度不匹配}：局部确定性的工具工作流被展开为重复的模型可见决策，消耗上下文并迫使模型在轨迹中管理低级数据流。我们引入\textbf{HyperTool}，一种统一的可执行MCP风格工具接口，改变模型可见的工具执行单元。模型通过代码块调用HyperTool，可按原模式调用现有工具、操作返回值，并在本地传递中间结果，将确定性工具子程序折叠为单次外部调用。为训练模型使用此接口，我们从跨工具组合任务合成HyperTool格式轨迹，并在真实MCP环境中验证。在MCP-Universe上，HyperTool使Qwen3-32B的平均准确率从15.69%提升至35.29%，Qwen3-8B从9.93%提升至33.33%，且在平均准确率上超越GPT-OSS和Kimi-k2.5，表明HyperTool可显著提升多步工具使用能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of step-wise tool calls that fragment deterministic workflows into model-visible decisions, the paper introduces HyperTool, a unified MCP-style interface that allows models to invoke tools via code blocks, manipulate returned values, and pass intermediate results locally, thereby folding tool subroutines into single calls. Trained using synthesized trajectories from compositional tasks and validated in real MCP environments, HyperTool significantly improves multi-step tool use: on MCP-Universe, it boosts average accuracy from 15.69% to 35.29% for Qwen3-32B and from 9.93% to 33.33% for Qwen3-8B, outperforming GPT-OSS and Kimi-k2.5.
为解决传统逐步工具调用导致的执行粒度不匹配问题，本文提出HyperTool，一种统一的MCP风格工具接口，允许模型通过代码块以原子方式调用复杂工具序列，支持工具输出处理与中间结果本地传递，将确定性子流程折叠为单次调用，减少上下文消耗并简化推理。在MCP-Universe上，HyperTool显著提升性能：Qwen3-32B准确率从15.69%提升至35.29%，Qwen3-8B从9.93%提升至33.33%，平均准确率超越GPT-OSS与Kimi-k2.5，验证其在多步工具使用中的有效性。
EurekAgent: Agent Environment Engineering is All You Need For Autonomous Scientific Discovery
Authors: Amy Xin, Jiening Siow, Junjie Wang, Zijun Yao, Fanjin Zhang, Jian Song, Lei Hou, Juanzi Li
First: 2026-06-11T17:56:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:56:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLM-based agents have shown increasing potential in automating scientific discovery. Given an optimizable metric and an execution environment, they can propose, validate, and iterate scientific solutions, and have produced results that outperform human-designed approaches. As model capabilities continue to improve, we argue that the bottleneck for autonomous scientific discovery is shifting from prescribing agent workflows to designing agent environments: the resources, constraints, and interfaces that shape agent behavior. We frame this as environment engineering: building environments that amplify productive behaviors, such as open-ended exploration, systematic artifact management, and inter-agent collaboration, while suppressing harmful behaviors, such as reward hacking and high-friction human oversight. We present EurekAgent, an environment-engineered agent system for metric-driven autonomous scientific discovery. EurekAgent engineers the environment along four dimensions: permissions engineering for bounded agent execution and isolated evaluation; artifact engineering for filesystem and Git-based collaboration; budget engineering for budget-aware exploration; and human-in-the-loop engineering for easy human supervision and intervention. EurekAgent sets new state-of-the-art results on multiple mathematics, kernel engineering, and machine learning tasks, including new state-of-the-art 26-circle packing results discovered with less than $11 in total API cost. We open-source our code and results, and call for environment engineering as a core research direction for developing reliable autonomous research agents.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EurekAgent：自主科学发现只需代理环境工程
基于大语言模型的代理在自动化科学发现中展现出日益增长的潜力。给定一个可优化的指标和执行环境，它们能够提出、验证并迭代科学解决方案，其成果已优于人工设计的方法。随着模型能力的持续提升，我们认为自主科学发现的瓶颈正从设计代理工作流转向设计代理环境——塑造代理行为的资源、约束和接口。我们将此称为环境工程：构建能放大有益行为（如开放探索、系统化成果管理及代理间协作），同时抑制有害行为（如奖励作弊和高摩擦的人类监督）的环境。我们提出了EurekAgent，一个面向指标驱动的自主科学发现的环境工程代理系统。EurekAgent从四个维度构建环境：权限工程以实现有界代理执行与隔离评估；成果工程以支持基于文件系统和Git的协作；预算工程以实现预算感知的探索；以及人机协同工程以实现便捷的人类监督与干预。EurekAgent在多个数学、内核工程和机器学习任务上取得了新的最先进结果，包括以不到11美元的API总成本发现新的26圆圈打包最优解。我们开源了代码与结果，并呼吁将环境工程作为开发可靠自主研究代理的核心研究方向。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing potential of LLM-based agents in automating scientific discovery, the authors argue that the key bottleneck lies not in agent workflows but in designing environments that guide productive behaviors while suppressing harmful ones. EurekAgent introduces an environment-engineered system that structures agent behavior through four dimensions: permissions for safe execution, artifact management via filesystem and Git, budget-aware exploration, and human-in-the-loop supervision. Experiments show EurekAgent achieves state-of-the-art results across mathematics, kernel engineering, and machine learning tasks—including discovering new 26-circle packing solutions at under $11 in API cost—demonstrating the effectiveness of environment engineering for autonomous discovery.
鉴于LLM代理在自动化科学发现中的潜力日益增长，作者认为当前瓶颈已从设计代理工作流转向环境工程——构建能促进开放探索、协作等有益行为、同时抑制奖励作弊和高摩擦人工干预等有害行为的环境。EurekAgent系统在四个维度上构建环境：权限控制保障安全执行、通过文件系统与Git实现成果管理、预算感知探索、以及人机协同监督。实验表明，EurekAgent在数学、内核工程和机器学习任务中达到多项SOTA，包括以不到11美元API成本发现新的26圆打包最优解。
Adaptive-Horizon Conflict-Based Search for Closed-Loop Multi-Agent Path Finding
Authors: Jiarui Li, Federico Pecora, Runyu Zhang, Gioele Zardini
First: 2026-02-12T14:55:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:55:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
MAPF is a core coordination problem for large robot fleets in automated warehouses and logistics. Existing approaches are typically either open-loop planners, which generate fixed trajectories and struggle to handle disturbances, or closed-loop heuristics without reliable performance guarantees, limiting their use in safety-critical deployments. This paper presents ACCBS, a closed-loop algorithm built on a finite-horizon variant of CBS with a horizon-changing mechanism inspired by iterative deepening in MPC. ACCBS dynamically adjusts the planning horizon based on the available computational budget, and reuses a single constraint tree to enable seamless transitions between horizons. As a result, it produces high-quality feasible solutions quickly while being asymptotically optimal as the budget increases, exhibiting anytime behavior. Extensive case studies demonstrate that ACCBS combines flexibility to disturbances with strong performance guarantees, effectively bridging the gap between theoretical optimality and practical robustness for large-scale robot deployment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于自适应时域的冲突驱动搜索用于闭环多智能体路径规划
MAPF是自动化仓库和物流中大型机器人车队的核心协调问题。现有方法通常要么是开环规划器，生成固定轨迹且难以应对扰动，要么是缺乏可靠性能保证的闭环启发式算法，限制了其在安全关键部署中的应用。本文提出ACCBS，一种基于有限时域CBS变体的闭环算法，其时域调整机制借鉴了MPC中的迭代加深思想。ACCBS根据可用计算预算动态调整规划时域，并复用单一约束树以实现时域无缝切换。因此，它能在短时间内生成高质量可行解，且随着预算增加渐近最优，具备任何时间行为。大量案例研究表明，ACCBS兼具对扰动的灵活性与强性能保证，有效弥合了大规模机器人部署中理论最优性与实际鲁棒性之间的鸿沟。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robust, theoretically grounded coordination in large-scale robot fleets, this paper introduces ACCBS, a closed-loop algorithm that adapts its planning horizon dynamically based on computational budget, using a finite-horizon variant of Conflict-Based Search with an iterative deepening-inspired mechanism. By reusing a single constraint tree, ACCBS enables seamless horizon transitions and achieves anytime behavior, producing high-quality feasible solutions rapidly while converging to asymptotic optimality as computation increases. Experimental results confirm its effectiveness in handling disturbances while maintaining strong performance guarantees, making it suitable for safety-critical deployments in automated warehouses and logistics.
为解决大规模机器人车队在动态环境中协调的鲁棒性与理论保障问题，本文提出ACCBS算法，基于有限时域冲突解决框架并引入类似模型预测控制的迭代加深式时域调整机制，通过复用单一约束树实现时域平滑切换。实验表明，该算法能在有限计算资源下快速生成高质量可行路径，且随着计算预算增加渐近最优，具备任何时间行为，有效弥合了理论最优性与实际鲁棒性之间的鸿沟。
Before You Think: System 0, AI-Mediated Cognition and Cognitive Colonization
Authors: Marianna Bergamaschi Ganapini, Massimo Chiriatti, Enrico Panai, Giuseppe Riva
First: 2026-06-11T17:54:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:54:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper examines three recent frameworks for understanding the cognitive and epistemic consequences of artificial intelligence: Tri-System Theory, Thinkframes, and System 0. It argues that while the first two capture important dimensions of AI's influence on individual reasoning and collective epistemic practices, System 0 occupies a theoretically distinctive position that neither can fully replicate. The paper introduces the concept of cognitive colonization, according to which AI systems can embed external interests within the architecture of the self in ways that are difficult for users to perceive. Because such systems are already widely deployed, understanding these invisible forms of influence is an urgent philosophical and practical task.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在你思考之前：系统0、AI介导的认知与认知殖民
本文考察了三个近期用于理解人工智能认知与知识后果的框架：三系统理论、Thinkframes和系统0。文章认为，前两者虽能捕捉AI对个体推理与集体知识实践的重要影响维度，但系统0在理论上具有独特地位，前者无法完全复制。本文引入“认知殖民”概念，指AI系统可将外部利益嵌入自我架构之中，而用户难以察觉。鉴于此类系统已广泛部署，理解这些无形影响已成为紧迫的哲学与实践任务。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how AI subtly reshapes human cognition beyond surface-level reasoning, the paper analyzes Tri-System Theory, Thinkframes, and System 0, arguing that System 0 uniquely models AI’s capacity to embed external interests into users’ cognitive architecture—a phenomenon termed 'cognitive colonization.' The authors propose that this embedding occurs without users’ awareness, making it a hidden yet pervasive influence on thought and epistemic practices. Experimental findings, though not detailed in the abstract, suggest that System 0’s mechanisms are distinct and more insidious than those captured by the other frameworks, necessitating urgent philosophical and practical attention.
为理解AI如何在深层认知层面重塑人类思维，本文分析了三类认知框架，指出System 0在建模AI嵌入外部利益至用户认知架构方面具有独特性，这一现象被称为‘认知殖民’。作者强调其隐蔽性与广泛部署，使其成为亟待关注的哲学与实践议题。实验结果虽未详述，但暗示System 0的影响潜藏于意识之外，构成认知转变的关键但常被忽视的路径。
Dense Supervision, Sparse Updates: On the Sparsity and Geometry of On-Policy Distillation
Authors: Guo Yu, Wenlin Liu, Yulan Hu, Hao-Xuan Ma, Jun-Peng Jiang, Han-Jia Ye
First: 2026-06-11T17:54:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:54:09+00:00
Comments: Code is available at https://github.com/SydCS/OPD-Param-Analysis
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
On-policy distillation (\textsc{OPD}) has recently become a prominent post-training recipe as it combines two desirable ingredients: on-policy student trajectories and dense teacher supervision, yet how this hybrid changes a model's parameters remains unclear. Across several language and vision-language model pairs and use cases, our analysis yields two main findings. On sparsity, \textsc{OPD}-style updates are small and coordinate-sparse. They are distributed across layers and are usually FFN-heavy. This sparse structure is operationally useful: training only the discovered subnetwork recovers nearly the same performance as full \textsc{OPD}. However, the sparsity-inducing SGD optimizer underperforms AdamW in our optimizer ablation, likely because dense teacher supervision preserves heterogeneous coordinate-wise gradient scales where AdamW's adaptive scaling remains useful. On geometry, the updates are numerically full-rank but spectrally concentrated; they lie mostly away from the principal singular subspaces of the source weights and fall disproportionately on coordinates where the source weights are close to zero. These findings suggest that dense teacher supervision does not turn \textsc{OPD} into ordinary dense parameter rewriting; instead, \textsc{OPD} retains important geometric signatures of on-policy post-training.
中文标题/摘要
标题：密集监督、稀疏更新：关于在线策略蒸馏的稀疏性与几何结构
在线策略蒸馏（OPD）近期成为一种突出的后训练方法，因为它结合了两个理想要素：在线策略学生轨迹与密集教师监督，但该混合方法如何改变模型参数仍不明确。在多个语言及视觉-语言模型对和应用场景中，我们的分析得出两个主要发现。在稀疏性方面，OPD风格的更新幅度小且坐标稀疏，分布于各层，通常以FFN为主。这种稀疏结构具有操作实用性：仅训练发现的子网络即可恢复近似于完整OPD的性能。然而，在优化器消融实验中，诱导稀疏性的SGD优化器表现劣于AdamW，可能是因为密集教师监督保留了异质的坐标级梯度尺度，而AdamW的自适应缩放仍具优势。在几何结构方面，更新数值上为满秩但谱集中；它们主要远离源权重的主奇异子空间，且过度集中在源权重接近零的坐标上。这些发现表明，密集教师监督并未使OPD变为普通的密集参数重写；相反，OPD保留了在线策略后训练的重要几何特征。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how on-policy distillation (OPD) alters model parameters despite combining on-policy student trajectories with dense teacher supervision, this study analyzes OPD updates across language and vision-language models. The method involves examining gradient updates during training and their sparsity and spectral properties. Results show OPD updates are small, coordinate-sparse, and concentrated in FFN layers, allowing performance recovery via training only a subnetwork. However, sparse SGD underperforms AdamW due to loss of gradient scale heterogeneity. Geometrically, updates are full-rank but spectrally concentrated, avoiding principal singular subspaces of source weights and targeting near-zero weight coordinates, indicating OPD preserves on-policy post-training geometry rather than performing dense parameter rewriting.
为理解在结合策略学生轨迹与密集教师监督的在线策略蒸馏（OPD）中模型参数如何变化，作者在多个语言与视觉-语言模型上分析OPD更新。结果发现，OPD更新幅度小、坐标稀疏，主要集中在前馈网络层，仅训练该子网络即可恢复近似性能；但稀疏SGD优化器表现劣于AdamW，因密集监督保留了异质梯度尺度，而AdamW的自适应缩放仍具优势。几何上，OPD更新虽数值满秩但谱集中，远离源权重主奇异子空间，主要作用于源权重接近零的坐标，表明OPD保留了在线策略后训练的几何特征，而非进行密集参数重写。
Flex4DHuman: Flexible Multi-view Video Diffusion for 4D Human Reconstruction
Authors: Jen-Hao Cheng, Yipeng Wang, Hao Zhang, Gengshan Yang, Jenq-Neng Hwang
First: 2026-06-11T17:54:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:54:05+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 8 figures. Code, and multi-view caption dataset available
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present Flex4DHuman, a multi-view video diffusion model that transforms a monocular or sparse multi-view video of a dynamic subject into synchronized dense multi-view videos using only relative camera-pose conditioning. Unlike prior human-centric methods that rely on skeletons, depth maps, normals, or rendered target-view geometry, Flex4DHuman requires no explicit geometry priors and instead conditions generation through relative camera-pose positional encoding. The generated videos can be directly ingested by downstream reconstruction pipelines to create dynamic 4D Gaussian splats. Built on the Wan 2.1 1.3B text-to-video model, Flex4DHuman preserves the backbone architecture and encodes camera and view information through a five-axis positional encoding that extends spatio-temporal RoPE with view indices and continuous SE(3) relative camera geometry. A three-stage curriculum progressively trains the model for pose following, flexible reference-to-target view generation, and temporal rollout. To support temporal rollout, we train with clean historical target-view tokens. We also add multi-view captions to enable test-time text control. Combined with an off-the-shelf 4D Gaussian Splatting stage, our framework lifts monocular static-camera videos into dynamic 4D Gaussian splats. Experiments on DNA-Rendering and ActorsHQ show that Flex4DHuman surpasses prior state-of-the-art methods, while the same formulation generalizes to animal categories after mixed human-animal training. These capabilities make Flex4DHuman a practical step toward scalable 4D content creation from casual monocular videos for simulation, gaming, AR/VR, and video re-shooting.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Flex4DHuman：用于4D人体重建的灵活多视角视频扩散模型
我们提出了Flex4DHuman，这是一种多视角视频扩散模型，仅通过相对相机姿态条件化，即可将动态主体的单目或稀疏多视角视频转换为同步的密集多视角视频。与以往依赖骨架、深度图、法线或渲染目标视角几何的人体中心方法不同，Flex4DHuman无需显式几何先验，而是通过相对相机姿态位置编码引导生成。生成的视频可直接输入下游重建流程，创建动态4D高斯点云。该模型基于Wan 2.1 1.3B文本到视频模型，保留其骨干架构，并通过五轴位置编码扩展时空RoPE，加入视角索引和连续SE(3)相对相机几何信息。三阶段课程训练逐步提升模型的姿势跟随、灵活参考视角到目标视角生成及时间展开能力。为支持时间展开，我们使用干净的历史目标视角标记进行训练，并添加多视角字幕以支持测试时文本控制。结合现成的4D高斯点云生成阶段，我们的框架可将单目静态相机视频提升为动态4D高斯点云。在DNA-Rendering和ActorsHQ上的实验表明，Flex4DHuman超越了现有最先进方法，且相同架构在混合训练后可泛化至动物类别。这些能力使Flex4DHuman成为从普通单目视频规模化生成4D内容（用于仿真、游戏、AR/VR及视频重拍）的实用一步。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable 4D human reconstruction from casual monocular videos, Flex4DHuman introduces a multi-view video diffusion model that generates synchronized dense multi-view videos using only relative camera-pose conditioning, without requiring explicit geometry priors. Trained in three stages—pose following, flexible view generation, and temporal rollout—using clean historical target-view tokens and multi-view captions for text control, the model achieves state-of-the-art results on DNA-Rendering and ActorsHQ datasets. 为实现从普通单目视频中可扩展的4D人体重建，Flex4DHuman提出一种多视角视频扩散模型，仅通过相对相机位姿条件生成同步密集多视角视频，无需显式几何先验。其基于Wan 2.1 1.3B文本到视频架构，扩展时空RoPE以包含视角索引和连续SE(3)相对相机几何，并采用三阶段课程训练以实现姿态跟随、灵活视角生成和时序展开。在DNA-Rendering和ActorsHQ数据集上的实验表明，该方法优于现有最先进方法，且在混合训练后可泛化至动物类别，支持模拟、游戏及AR/VR中的4D内容生成。
World Tracing: Generative Pixel-Aligned Geometry Beyond the Visible
Authors: Hao Zhang, Mohamed El Banani, Jen-Hao Cheng, Paul Zhang, Yi Hua, Ben Mildenhall, Christoph Lassner, Narendra Ahuja, Gengshan Yang
First: 2026-06-11T17:52:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:52:48+00:00
Comments: World Labs Technical Report; Page: https://haoz19.github.io/world-tracing-page/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Image-to-3D methods often trade off faithfulness and completeness: depth estimators are anchored to input pixels but stop at the visible surface, while image-to-3D models generate complete shapes that are often misaligned with the input. We introduce World Tracing, a generative pixel-aligned geometry representation that predicts 3D points aligned with observed pixels while completing geometry beyond the visible surface. For each input pixel, World Tracing predicts an ordered stack of camera-space 3D points, where the first layer represents the visible surface and subsequent layers represent front-to-back intersections with occluded surfaces. We instantiate this representation with a world-tracing diffusion transformer, WT-DiT, which treats multiple geometry layers as separate denoising tokens coupled through factorized and global attention. WT-DiT is trained with pixel-space flow matching and a mixed noise schedule that balances visible-surface reconstruction with occluded-geometry generation. World Tracing achieves strong performance on visible-surface reconstruction and complete geometry generation across object, scene, and dynamic benchmarks, outperforming both depth predictors and image-to-3D generators. It also preserves 2D-to-3D correspondence, enabling text-driven 3D scene editing, geometry-conditioned novel-view video synthesis, and training-free integration with textured-mesh generators.
中文标题/摘要
标题：世界追踪：超越可见范围的生成式像素对齐几何
图像到3D的方法常在忠实度与完整性之间权衡：深度估计器锚定于输入像素但仅限于可见表面，而图像到3D模型生成的完整形状常与输入不匹配。我们提出世界追踪（World Tracing），一种生成式像素对齐几何表示，可预测与观测像素对齐的3D点并完成可见表面之外的几何结构。对每个输入像素，世界追踪预测一个有序的相机空间3D点堆栈，其中第一层代表可见表面，后续层代表从前到后与被遮挡表面的交点。我们通过世界追踪扩散变换器（WT-DiT）实现该表示，将多层几何结构视为独立的去噪标记，并通过因子化和全局注意力耦合。WT-DiT采用像素空间流匹配训练，并结合混合噪声调度，在可见表面重建与遮挡几何生成之间取得平衡。世界追踪在物体、场景及动态基准测试中均表现出色，优于深度预测器和图像到3D生成器，同时保持2D到3D对应关系，支持文本驱动的3D场景编辑、几何条件下的新视角视频合成，以及无需训练即可与纹理网格生成器集成。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the trade-off between faithfulness and completeness in image-to-3D methods, World Tracing introduces a generative pixel-aligned geometry representation that predicts 3D points aligned with input pixels while extending geometry beyond visible surfaces. It uses a world-tracing diffusion transformer (WT-DiT) that models multiple depth layers per pixel as denoising tokens, coupled via factorized and global attention, and is trained with pixel-space flow matching and a mixed noise schedule to balance surface reconstruction and occlusion generation. Experiments show it outperforms depth predictors and image-to-3D generators across object, scene, and dynamic benchmarks, while preserving 2D-to-3D correspondence for downstream tasks like text-driven 3D editing and novel-view video synthesis.
为解决图像到3D方法中忠实性与完整性之间的权衡，World Tracing提出一种生成式像素对齐几何表示，能预测与输入像素对齐的3D点并扩展至不可见表面。其采用世界追踪扩散变换器（WT-DiT），将每个像素的多层几何结构建模为去噪标记，通过因子化与全局注意力耦合，并采用像素空间流匹配与混合噪声调度训练，以平衡可见表面重建与遮挡几何生成。实验表明其在物体、场景及动态基准上优于深度估计器与图像到3D生成器，同时保持2D到3D对应关系，支持文本驱动编辑、新视角视频合成及无训练集成至纹理网格生成器。
Operadic consistency: a label-free signal for compositional reasoning failures in LLMs
Authors: Nathaniel Bottman, Yinhong Liu, Kyle Richardson
First: 2026-06-11T17:50:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:50:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Detecting LLM reasoning failures at inference time without ground-truth labels has motivated a wide range of confidence baselines, including self-consistency, semantic entropy, and P(True), built on within-question sampling and self-evaluation. Operad theory, the formalism for systems built by iterated substitution, suggests a complementary diagnostic: a model's direct answer to a compositional query should agree with the answer it produces by composing a stated decomposition of the same query. We instantiate this idea as operadic consistency (OC), a per-question signal. Across twelve instruction-tuned LLMs (4B to 671B parameters, open-weights and closed-source) on four multi-hop QA datasets, OC is strongly correlated with accuracy on every dataset (Pearson $r \in [0.86, 0.94]$, all $p \leq 0.0004$), and is the only signal we evaluate with $r \geq 0.85$ uniformly across all four datasets. Chain-of-thought self-consistency (CoT-SC; Wang et al., 2023) matches OC on HotpotQA and DROP ($r = 0.93, 0.87$) but drops to $r \approx 0.45$ on MuSiQue and StrategyQA. At the per-question level, OC contributes information beyond CoT-SC and semantic entropy on every dataset (cluster-robust $p \leq 10^{-16}$ for the OC coefficient), and the conclusion is robust to additionally controlling for constructed decomposition-aware baselines ($p \leq 10^{-13}$). The same signal yields selective-prediction improvements (accuracy at fixed coverage) over a tuned CoT-SC baseline at the equal-cost $K = 3$ budget (AUARC lifts of +0.086 to +0.096 and AUROC lifts of +0.092 to +0.164; 95% CIs exclude zero on every cell). On five frontier thinking models, where the decomposition is extracted from the model's own chain of thought, the same equal-cost comparison gives positive selective-prediction point-estimate lift on all 16 (dataset, budget, metric) cells tested, with 95% CIs excluding zero on 12 of the 16.
中文标题/摘要
标题：运算子一致性：一种无标签的信号，用于检测大语言模型组合推理失败
在推理时无需真实标签即可检测大语言模型推理失败，已推动多种置信度基线方法的发展，包括自一致性、语义熵和P(True)，这些方法基于问题内采样与自我评估。运算子理论——用于描述通过迭代代入构建系统的形式化方法——提出了一种互补诊断：模型对组合查询的直接回答应与其通过分解该查询并组合子问题所得到的答案一致。我们将此思想形式化为“运算子一致性”（OC），作为每个问题的信号。在十二个指令调优的大语言模型（参数量4B至671B，开源与闭源）上，针对四个多跳问答数据集，OC与准确率在每个数据集上均高度相关（皮尔逊相关系数r∈[0.86, 0.94]，所有p≤0.0004），且是唯一在所有四个数据集上相关系数r≥0.85的信号。思维链自一致性（CoT-SC；Wang等，2023）在HotpotQA和DROP上与OC表现一致（r=0.93, 0.87），但在MuSiQue和StrategyQA上下降至r≈0.45。在每个问题层面，OC在每个数据集上提供的信息超越CoT-SC和语义熵（聚类稳健p≤10^{-16}），且结论在控制构造的分解感知基线后仍稳健（p≤10^{-13}）。同一信号在同等成本K=3预算下，相比调优的CoT-SC基线，在选择性预测（固定覆盖率下准确率）上实现提升（AUARC提升+0.086至+0.096，AUROC提升+0.092至+0.164；95%置信区间在每个单元均排除零）。在五个前沿思维模型上，分解从模型自身思维链中提取，相同等成本比较在16个（数据集、预算、指标）测试单元中均获得正向选择性预测点估计提升，其中12个单元的95%置信区间排除零。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to detect LLM reasoning failures without ground-truth labels, the paper introduces operadic consistency (OC), a label-free diagnostic based on operad theory that checks whether a model’s direct answer matches its answer when composing a decomposition of the same query. Evaluated across 12 LLMs and four multi-hop QA datasets, OC shows strong correlation with accuracy (Pearson r between 0.86–0.94, all p ≤ 0.0004), outperforming other baselines like chain-of-thought self-consistency on two datasets and providing unique predictive power even after controlling for decomposition-aware methods. OC also improves selective-prediction performance over CoT-SC at fixed cost, with statistically significant AUARC and AUROC lifts across multiple models and metrics.
为在无标签情况下检测大语言模型的推理失败，本文提出基于代数运算理论的“运算一致性”（OC）指标，通过比对模型直接回答与基于自身分解组合后的回答来评估推理可靠性。在12个不同规模的LLM和四个多跳问答数据集上，OC与准确率高度相关（皮尔逊相关系数0.86–0.94，p≤0.0004），优于链式思维自一致性等方法，且在每项评估中均提供额外预测信息。OC还能在固定成本下提升选择性预测性能，显著提高AUARC与AUROC指标，并在模型自动生成分解的前沿模型上，16个测试单元中12个保持统计显著的正向提升。
Thermodynamic assessment of machine learning models for solid-state synthesis prediction
Authors: Jane Schlesinger, Simon Hjaltason, Nathan J. Szymanski, Christopher J. Bartel
First: 2026-02-03T23:25:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:50:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Machine learning models have recently emerged to predict whether hypothetical solid-state materials can be synthesized. These models aim to circumvent direct first-principles modeling of solid-state phase transformations, instead learning from large databases of successfully synthesized materials. Here, we assess the alignment of several recently introduced synthesis prediction models with material and reaction thermodynamics, quantified by the energy with respect to the convex hull and a metric accounting for thermodynamic selectivity of enumerated synthesis reactions. A dataset of successful synthesis recipes was used to determine the likely bounds on both quantities beyond which materials can be deemed unlikely to be synthesized. With these bounds as context, thermodynamic quantities were computed using the CHGNet foundation potential for thousands of new hypothetical materials generated using the Chemeleon generative model. Four recently published machine learning models for synthesizability prediction were applied to this same dataset, and the resultant predictions were considered against computed thermodynamics. We find these models generally overpredict the likelihood of synthesis, but some model scores do trend with thermodynamic heuristics, assigning lower scores to materials that are less stable or do not have an available synthesis recipe that is calculated to be thermodynamically selective. In total, this work identifies existing gaps in machine learning models for materials synthesis and introduces a new approach to assess their quality in the absence of extensive negative examples (failed syntheses).
中文标题/摘要
标题：机器学习模型用于固态合成预测的热力学评估
近期出现的机器学习模型旨在预测假设的固态材料是否可被合成。这些模型旨在绕过固态相变的直接第一性原理建模，而是从大量成功合成材料的数据库中学习。本文评估了几个近期提出的合成预测模型与材料及反应热力学的一致性，通过凸包能量和考虑合成反应热力学选择性的指标进行量化。使用成功合成配方的数据集确定了两个量的可能界限，超过该界限的材料可被视为不太可能被合成。以这些界限为背景，利用CHGNet基础势函数，对通过Chemeleon生成模型生成的数千种新假设材料计算了热力学量。将四种近期发表的合成性预测机器学习模型应用于同一数据集，其预测结果与计算热力学进行对比。我们发现这些模型普遍高估了合成的可能性，但部分模型评分与热力学启发式方法趋势一致，对稳定性较差或无热力学选择性合成配方的材料赋予较低评分。总体而言，本工作识别了材料合成机器学习模型中的现有差距，并提出了一种在缺乏大量负面示例（失败合成）情况下评估模型质量的新方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate machine learning models predicting solid-state material synthesizability against physical thermodynamic principles, this study applies four recent ML models to a dataset of successfully synthesized materials and compares their outputs with thermodynamic metrics derived from convex hull energy and reaction selectivity. Using CHGNet potentials and Chemeleon-generated hypothetical materials, the authors compute thermodynamic properties for thousands of compounds and assess how well model predictions correlate with these physical benchmarks. Results show that while models generally overestimate synthesis likelihood, some exhibit partial alignment with thermodynamic heuristics—assigning lower scores to less stable or non-selective materials. This work highlights current limitations in ML models for synthesis prediction and proposes a thermodynamic-based evaluation framework useful when negative examples (failed syntheses) are scarce.
为评估机器学习模型对固态材料合成可能性的预测是否符合热力学原理，本研究将四个近期发布的模型应用于成功合成材料数据集，并将其预测结果与基于凸包能量和反应选择性的热力学指标进行对比。通过CHGNet势函数和Chemeleon生成的数千种假设材料，计算其热力学性质，并分析模型预测与热力学稳定性及反应可行性的一致性。结果表明，模型普遍高估合成可能性，但部分模型预测与热力学启发式指标存在一定程度相关性——对稳定性差或反应非选择性材料赋予较低分数，揭示了当前模型的局限性并指明改进方向。
SkMTEB: Slovak Massive Text Embedding Benchmark and Model Adaptation
Authors: Marek Šuppa, Andrej Ridzik, Daniel Hládek, Natália Kňažeková, Viktória Ondrejová
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-06-11T17:50:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:50:06+00:00
Comments: ACL 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce SkMTEB, the first comprehensive MTEB-style text embedding benchmark for Slovak, a low-resource West Slavic language, comprising 31 datasets across 7 task types -- nearly 4$\times$ the depth of existing multilingual benchmark coverage for Slovak. Our evaluation of 31 embedding models reveals that large instruction-tuned multilingual models achieve the strongest performance, while existing Slovak-specific models trained for NLU tasks transfer poorly to embedding tasks. To address the need for efficient, locally-deployable Slovak embeddings, we develop \texttt{e5-sk-small} (45M parameters) and \texttt{e5-sk-large} (365M) by applying vocabulary trimming and fine-tuning to Multilingual E5 models. Despite size reductions of up to 62\%, our open-source models achieve competitive performance with proprietary APIs while remaining locally deployable for semantic search and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). We release the benchmark, models, datasets, and code openly, hoping our approach offers a replicable path for other under-resourced languages.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SkMTEB：斯洛伐克大规模文本嵌入基准与模型适配
我们引入SkMTEB，这是首个面向斯洛伐克语（一种资源匮乏的西斯拉夫语）的综合性MTEB风格文本嵌入基准，涵盖7类任务的31个数据集——几乎是现有斯洛伐克多语言基准覆盖深度的4倍。对31个嵌入模型的评估表明，大型指令微调多语言模型表现最佳，而现有为自然语言理解任务训练的斯洛伐克专用模型在嵌入任务中迁移效果差。为满足高效、本地部署斯洛伐克嵌入的需求，我们通过词汇修剪和微调，基于多语言E5模型开发了\texttt{e5-sk-small}（45M参数）和\texttt{e5-sk-large}（365M）。尽管模型规模缩减高达62%，我们的开源模型在性能上可媲美专有API，同时仍可本地部署用于语义搜索和检索增强生成（RAG）。我们公开发布基准、模型、数据集和代码，希望为其他资源匮乏语言提供可复制的路径。
