Motivated by the lack of comprehensive benchmarks and efficient embedding models for Slovak, a low-resource West Slavic language, the authors introduce SkMTEB, the first MTEB-style benchmark comprising 31 datasets across seven tasks. They evaluate 31 embedding models and find that large instruction-tuned multilingual models outperform Slovak-specific NLU models in embedding tasks. To enable local deployment, they adapt Multilingual E5 models via vocabulary trimming and fine-tuning, releasing \texttt{e5-sk-small} (45M) and \texttt{e5-sk-large} (365M) — achieving competitive performance with up to 62% parameter reduction compared to proprietary APIs, suitable for semantic search and RAG.
为填补斯洛伐克语（低资源西斯拉夫语）缺乏全面评估基准的空白，作者构建了SkMTEB，这是首个包含31个数据集、覆盖七类任务的MTEB风格基准，深度近是现有斯洛伐克语多语言基准的四倍。评估31个嵌入模型发现，大型指令微调多语言模型表现最佳，而现有斯洛伐克语专用NLU模型难以迁移到嵌入任务。为实现高效本地部署，作者通过词汇修剪与微调改造多语言E5模型，发布\texttt{e5-sk-small}（45M）和\texttt{e5-sk-large}（365M），参数缩减达62%，性能仍可媲美专有API，适用于语义搜索与RAG场景。
Surflo: Consistent 3D Surface Flow Model with Global State
Authors: Antoine Guédon, Shu Nakamura, Nicolas Dufour, Jiahui Lei, Ko Nishino, Angjoo Kanazawa
First: 2026-06-11T17:48:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:48:38+00:00
Comments: Project webpage: https://anttwo.github.io/surflo/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Geometry is invariant to viewpoint, which makes any collection of images a redundant encoding of a single 3D state. Existing feed-forward reconstruction models fail to exploit this: per-view methods emit overlapping, unaligned pointmaps that grow linearly with input count, while global-latent methods commit to a fixed, low-resolution output. We introduce Surflo, which compresses a variable number of unposed RGB views into K latent tokens-one global state-and decodes oriented 3D surface points by independently transporting them from noise onto the surface via flow matching. This frees the output from any fixed grid or token budget: the same latent yields from a few thousand to a million points in a single forward pass. To suppress the local inconsistencies inherent to independent per-point decoding, an inference-time guidance term correlates nearby points by injecting a photometric gradient during ODE integration. Surflo matches or surpasses feed-forward baselines on surface metrics, runs an order of magnitude faster than optimization-based methods that require hundreds of views, and is the only feed-forward approach to combine a global latent with arbitrary-resolution decoding.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Surflo：具有全局状态的一致3D表面流模型
几何结构对视角不变，这意味着任何图像集合都是单个3D状态的冗余编码。现有前馈重建模型未能利用这一点：每视角方法生成重叠且未对齐的点图，其规模随输入数量线性增长，而全局潜在空间方法则固定输出低分辨率。我们提出Surflo，将任意数量的无姿态RGB图像压缩为K个潜在标记——一个全局状态，并通过流匹配独立地将噪声点运输至表面以解码带方向的3D表面点。这使输出摆脱了固定网格或标记预算的限制：同一潜在状态可在单次前向传递中生成从数千到百万个点。为抑制独立点解码固有的局部不一致性，推理时引入引导项，在ODE积分中注入光度梯度以关联邻近点。Surflo在表面指标上匹配或超越前馈基线，比需数百视角的优化方法快一个数量级，且是唯一结合全局潜在空间与任意分辨率解码的前馈方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the viewpoint-invariance of 3D geometry, the paper addresses the inefficiency of existing feed-forward 3D reconstruction models that either produce redundant per-view outputs or fixed-resolution global representations. Surflo introduces a novel method that compresses multiple unposed RGB views into a single global latent state and decodes oriented 3D surface points via flow matching from noise, enabling arbitrary-resolution output without fixed token budgets. To mitigate local inconsistencies from independent point decoding, it injects photometric gradients during ODE integration to guide nearby points. Experiments show Surflo matches or exceeds feed-forward baselines in surface metrics, runs 10x faster than optimization-based methods requiring hundreds of views, and uniquely combines global latent encoding with scalable resolution decoding.
鉴于3D几何对视角不变的特性，该论文旨在解决现有前馈3D重建模型效率低下的问题：要么产生冗余的每视角输出，要么固定分辨率的全局潜在表示。Surflo提出一种新方法，将多个无姿态RGB图像压缩为K个全局潜在标记，再通过流匹配从噪声中独立解码定向3D表面点，实现任意分辨率输出而不受限于固定标记预算。为缓解独立点解码带来的局部不一致性，它在ODE积分过程中注入光度梯度以引导邻近点。实验表明，Surflo在表面指标上达到或超越前馈基线，比需数百视角的优化方法快10倍，且是唯一能结合全局潜在编码与可变分辨率解码的前馈方法。
Recursive Agent Harnesses
Authors: Elias Lumer, Sahil Sen, Kevin Paul, Vamse Kumar Subbiah
First: 2026-06-11T17:47:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:47:30+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recursive language models (RLMs) showed that recursion over model calls is an effective strategy for long-context reasoning, and production coding agents have begun to write code that spawns subagents at scale, most recently in Anthropic's dynamic workflows. We name and study the pattern between these two lines of work, where the recursive unit is a full agent harness with filesystem tools, code execution, and planning rather than a model call with no tools. We call this the Recursive Agent Harness (RAH) and frame it as harness recursion, the code-first extension to the model recursion of RLMs. A parent agent generates and runs an executable script that spawns subagent harnesses in parallel for fine-grained workloads and uses structured function calls for small subtasks. We provide a controlled evaluation on long-context reasoning. With the backbone held fixed at GPT-5 to match the published Codex and RLM baselines, RAH improves the Codex coding-agent baseline from 71.75% to 81.36% on Oolong-Synthetic (199 samples, 13 context-length buckets up to 4M tokens), a gain attributable to the harness rather than the model. With a stronger backbone, Claude Sonnet 4.5, the same design reaches 89.77%.
中文标题/摘要
标题：递归代理框架
递归语言模型（RLMs）表明，模型调用上的递归是长上下文推理的有效策略，而生产级编码代理已开始编写可大规模生成子代理的代码，最近体现在Anthropic的动态工作流中。我们命名并研究这两类工作的共同模式：递归单元是一个具备文件系统工具、代码执行和规划能力的完整代理框架，而非无工具的模型调用。我们称之为递归代理框架（RAH），并将其视为RLMs模型递归的代码优先扩展——即框架递归。父代理生成并运行可执行脚本，以并行方式启动子代理框架处理细粒度任务，并使用结构化函数调用处理小规模子任务。我们在长上下文推理任务上进行了受控评估。在保持骨干模型为GPT-5（与已发表的Codex和RLM基线一致）的情况下，RAH将Codex编码代理基线从Oolong-Synthetic（199个样本，13个上下文长度桶，最高达4M token）上的71.75%提升至81.36%，该提升归因于框架而非模型本身。若使用更强的骨干模型Claude Sonnet 4.5，相同设计可达到89.77%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the success of recursive language models and production coding agents that spawn subagents, this work introduces Recursive Agent Harnesses (RAH), a framework where full agent harnesses—equipped with filesystem access, code execution, and planning—are recursively spawned to handle complex, long-context reasoning tasks. The parent agent generates executable scripts to launch parallel subagent harnesses for large workloads and uses structured function calls for smaller tasks. Evaluated on Oolong-Synthetic with GPT-5 as the backbone, RAH boosts the Codex baseline from 71.75% to 81.36%, demonstrating that the harness design—not the model—drives the improvement. With Claude Sonnet 4.5, the same architecture achieves 89.77%, confirming scalability and effectiveness.
受递归语言模型与可扩展代理系统成功启发，本文提出递归代理框架（RAH），其核心是父代理生成可执行脚本，启动具备文件系统访问、代码执行和规划能力的子代理并行处理大任务，小任务则通过结构化函数调用完成。在以GPT-5为基座的长上下文推理任务中，RAH将Codex基线准确率从71.75%提升至81.36%，表明性能提升主要源于框架设计而非模型本身。使用更强的Claude Sonnet 4.5后，同一架构准确率进一步提升至89.77%，验证了其可扩展性和有效性。
The Stable Recovery Manifold: Geometric Principles Governing Recoverability in Continual Learning
Authors: Ayushman Trivedi, Bhavika Melwani
First: 2026-06-11T17:45:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:45:41+00:00
Comments: 9 pages, 8 figures, 8 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Catastrophic forgetting is often viewed as the destruction of previously learned knowledge during sequential learning. Building on the Accessibility Collapse framework, we investigate the geometric structure of recoverability in continual learning. Using Split CIFAR-100 and a sequentially trained ResNet-18, we analyze recoverability, representational drift, and recovery complexity across ten tasks. We introduce Recovery Subspace Dimensionality (k_t), a measure of the minimum number of singular directions required to preserve 90 percent of full probe performance. Contrary to our Recoverability Diffusion hypothesis, recovery dimensionality remains stable throughout training (mean k_t = 8.0) despite substantial representational drift. Principal-angle drift strongly predicts recoverability (r = -0.862), and a simple geometric model explains 82.2 percent of recoverability variance. These findings support the Stable Recovery Manifold hypothesis, suggesting that forgotten knowledge remains compactly decodable despite representational reorganization. The results indicate that catastrophic forgetting is primarily an accessibility and manifold-alignment problem rather than information destruction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：稳定恢复流形：持续学习中可恢复性的几何原理
灾难性遗忘常被视为在序列学习过程中先前知识的破坏。基于可访问性崩溃框架，我们研究了持续学习中可恢复性的几何结构。使用Split CIFAR-100和顺序训练的ResNet-18，我们分析了十个任务中的可恢复性、表征漂移和恢复复杂性。我们引入了恢复子空间维度（k_t），即保持90%完整探针性能所需的最小奇异方向数。与我们的可恢复性扩散假说相反，尽管表征漂移显著，恢复维度在整个训练过程中保持稳定（平均k_t = 8.0）。主角角漂移强烈预测可恢复性（r = -0.862），一个简单的几何模型解释了82.2%的可恢复性方差。这些发现支持稳定恢复流形假说，表明尽管表征重组，遗忘的知识仍可紧凑解码。结果表明，灾难性遗忘主要是一个可访问性与流形对齐问题，而非信息破坏。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how knowledge is lost and potentially recovered in continual learning, this study investigates the geometric principles underlying recoverability. Using a sequentially trained ResNet-18 on Split CIFAR-100, the authors analyze how representational drift affects the ability to recover past task performance. They introduce Recovery Subspace Dimensionality (k_t), measuring the minimal singular directions needed to retain 90% of probe accuracy, and find that this dimensionality remains stable (mean k_t = 8.0) despite significant drift. Principal-angle drift strongly correlates with recoverability (r = -0.862), and a geometric model explains 82.2% of the variance, supporting the Stable Recovery Manifold hypothesis: forgotten knowledge remains compactly decodable due to manifold alignment, not information loss.
为理解持续学习中知识的保留与丢失机制，该研究基于Split CIFAR-100和顺序训练的ResNet-18，探讨了可恢复性的几何结构。引入“恢复子空间维数”（k_t）衡量维持90%探针性能所需的最小奇异方向数，发现尽管表征发生显著漂移，k_t仍保持稳定（平均值8.0）。主角距离漂移与可恢复性高度相关（r = -0.862），几何模型解释了82.2%的可恢复性方差，支持“稳定恢复流形”假说：遗忘知识因流形对齐仍可紧凑解码，表明灾难性遗忘源于可访问性问题而非信息破坏。
Operads for compositional reasoning in LLMs
Authors: Nathaniel Bottman, Kyle Richardson
First: 2026-06-11T17:44:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:44:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Question decomposition, i.e. breaking a complex query into simpler sub-queries whose answers are composed to produce a final answer, is a widely used strategy for improving LLM reasoning, yet it currently lacks a rigorous mathematical foundation. In this paper, we propose operads, mathematical structures that model many-in, one-out operations and compositions thereof, as a natural framework for describing question decomposition. We define the questions operad $Q$, in which operations correspond to question templates and composition corresponds to substitution of sub-answers, and show how QA models can be interpreted as algebras over $Q$. Beyond reframing existing practice, this operadic perspective points toward new methods, in particular a notion of operadic consistency, which measures whether a QA model's answers agree across the partial collapses of a question decomposition tree. Empirical evaluation of operadic consistency is reported in our companion paper (Bottman, Liu, and Richardson, 2026), which finds it strongly correlated with accuracy across twelve LLMs and four multi-hop QA datasets and outperforming standard temperature-based self-consistency baselines. We argue that operads are the natural mathematical home for question decomposition, and that invariants such as operadic consistency open new directions for analyzing and improving the reliability of multi-step reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于大语言模型组合推理的代数结构
问题分解，即将复杂查询拆解为若干简单子查询并组合其答案以生成最终答案，是提升大语言模型推理能力的常用策略，但目前尚缺乏严谨的数学基础。本文提出代数结构（operads），其建模多输入、单输出操作及其组合，作为描述问题分解的自然框架。我们定义了问题代数 $Q$，其中操作对应问题模板，组合对应子答案的替换，并展示问答模型可被解释为 $Q$ 上的代数。除重构现有实践外，该代数视角还指向新方法，特别是“代数一致性”概念，用于衡量问答模型在问题分解树的局部坍缩中答案是否一致。我们配套论文（Bottman, Liu, and Richardson, 2026）报告了代数一致性的实证评估，发现其在十二个大语言模型和四个多跳问答数据集上与准确率高度相关，且优于标准的基于温度的自一致性基线。我们认为代数结构是问题分解的自然数学框架，而此类不变量（如代数一致性）为分析和提升多步推理可靠性开辟了新方向。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of a rigorous mathematical foundation for question decomposition in LLMs, this paper introduces operads as a formal framework to model compositional reasoning, where operations represent question templates and composition corresponds to substituting sub-answers. The authors define the questions operad Q and interpret QA models as algebras over Q, enabling a new notion of operadic consistency that measures answer agreement across partial decompositions. Empirical results show operadic consistency strongly correlates with accuracy across twelve LLMs and four multi-hop QA datasets, outperforming standard temperature-based self-consistency baselines.
为弥补大语言模型中问题分解缺乏严谨数学基础的不足，本文提出使用代数结构“运算代”作为建模组合推理的框架，其中运算对应问题模板，组合对应子答案替换。作者定义了问题运算代Q，并将问答模型解释为Q上的代数结构，从而赋予多跳推理新的数学视角。实验表明，一种名为“运算一致性”的指标——衡量问题分解树中部分坍缩路径间答案的一致性——在十二个模型和四个数据集上与准确率高度相关，且优于传统的基于温度的自一致性方法。
Towards Data-free and Training-free Compression for Speech Foundation Models Using Parameter Clustering
Authors: Haoning Xu, Zhaoqing Li, Huimeng Wang, Youjun Chen, Chengxi Deng, Mengzhe Geng, Xunying Liu
First: 2026-06-10T09:16:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:42:41+00:00
Comments: Accepted by Interspeech 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents a novel data-free and training-free compression approach for speech foundation models using channelwise clustering via k-means. More fine-grained, mixed sparsity pruning by layer-level varying number of parameter clusters is also explored. Experiments conducted on the LibriSpeech dataset suggest that when operating with pruning sparsity of 50% on HuBERT-large, consistent WER reductions of 27.73%/18.61% absolute (34.37%/21.91% relative) over the magnitude-based pruning were obtained on the test-clean and test-other subsets before fine-tuning and 0.19%/0.79% absolute (3.36%/4.62% relative) after fine-tuning with only 3 epochs. Similar WER reductions of 2.86%/5.02% absolute (59.21%/55.29% relative) were observed against magnitudebased pruning on Whisper-large-v3 at 10% sparsity, all with no significant WER increase relative to the uncompressed baseline.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于参数聚类的无数据、无训练语音基础模型压缩方法
本文提出了一种基于通道聚类（k-means）的无数据、无训练语音基础模型压缩新方法。同时探索了通过层间不同参数聚类数量实现更精细的混合稀疏剪枝。在LibriSpeech数据集上的实验表明，当在HuBERT-large模型上以50%剪枝稀疏度运行时，在微调前，测试集clean和test-other子集上的WER分别降低27.73%/18.61%绝对值（34.37%/21.91%相对值），优于基于幅度的剪枝；微调仅3轮后，WER进一步降低0.19%/0.79%绝对值（3.36%/4.62%相对值）。在Whisper-large-v3模型上，10%稀疏度下，与基于幅度的剪枝相比，WER降低2.86%/5.02%绝对值（59.21%/55.29%相对值），且与未压缩基线相比无显著WER上升。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient compression of speech foundation models without requiring training data or fine-tuning, this work introduces a data-free and training-free method using channelwise k-means clustering to group model parameters. It further explores layer-specific sparsity by varying the number of clusters per layer. Experiments on LibriSpeech show that at 50% sparsity, the proposed method achieves 27.73% absolute WER reduction on test-clean and 18.61% on test-other before fine-tuning, and 0.19% and 0.79% after fine-tuning with only 3 epochs, outperforming magnitude-based pruning. Similar gains are observed on Whisper-large-v3 at 10% sparsity, with no significant WER degradation relative to the uncompressed baseline.
为实现无需训练数据和微调的语音基础模型压缩，本文提出一种基于通道级k均值聚类的无数据无训练压缩方法，并探索按层调整聚类数以实现细粒度稀疏剪枝。在LibriSpeech数据集上实验表明，对HuBERT-large在50%稀疏度下，该方法在测试集clean和other上分别比幅度剪枝降低27.73%和18.61%绝对WER，微调后仍保持0.19%和0.79%绝对WER下降，优于幅度剪枝；在Whisper-large-v3上10%稀疏度下，测试集clean和other分别降低2.86%和5.02%绝对WER，性能接近未压缩基线。
Aerial Wildfire Suppression Planning with a Hybrid CNN-Cellular Automata Fire Model
Authors: Ion Matei, Maksym Zhenirovskyy, Takuya Kurihana, Rohit Vupala, Anthony Wong
First: 2026-06-11T17:42:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:42:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Aerial wildfire suppression requires not only predicting fire spread, but also designing effective intervention strategies under operational and environmental uncertainty. We present a modeling and optimization framework for aerial wildfire suppression that combines a hybrid neural-cellular automaton wildfire model with gradient-based design of targeted aerial drops. The wildfire model predicts spatially varying spread behavior from terrain, fuel, and wind data, while the intervention module determines binary drop actions with continuous-valued location and orientation parameters mapped to the simulation grid. Water and retardant are represented with distinct suppression effects, corresponding to immediate reduction of active burning and persistent reduction of future spread. To evaluate the robustness of the resulting suppression plans, we quantify both aleatoric uncertainty through Monte Carlo sampling of daily fire-state realizations and epistemic uncertainty through spatially correlated prediction-error perturbations. A case study based on the 2020 Bear Fire shows that the framework can generate coherent aerial suppression schedules for reducing total fire-affected area and can support uncertainty-aware analysis of wildfire intervention strategies.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于混合CNN-细胞自动机火灾模型的空中野火抑制规划
空中野火抑制不仅需要预测火势蔓延，还需在操作与环境不确定性下设计有效的干预策略。本文提出一种结合混合神经细胞自动机火灾模型与梯度优化的靶向空中洒水方案的建模与优化框架。火灾模型根据地形、燃料和风力数据预测空间异质性蔓延行为；干预模块通过连续值的位置与方向参数映射至模拟网格，确定二元洒水动作。水与阻燃剂分别对应即时降低燃烧强度与持续抑制未来蔓延的抑制效果。为评估抑制方案的鲁棒性，通过蒙特卡洛采样量化每日火情状态的随机不确定性，并通过空间相关预测误差扰动量化认知不确定性。基于2020年熊火案例研究表明，该框架可生成连贯的空中抑制调度方案，减少总受火面积，并支持不确定性感知的野火干预策略分析。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for effective aerial wildfire suppression under uncertain environmental conditions, this study develops a hybrid CNN-cellular automaton model to simulate fire spread and optimize targeted aerial drop interventions. The model integrates terrain, fuel, and wind data to predict spatially varying fire behavior, while an optimization module determines drop locations and orientations to minimize fire-affected area. Experiments on the 2020 Bear Fire case study demonstrate that the framework generates coherent suppression schedules and enables uncertainty-aware analysis by incorporating both aleatoric uncertainty via Monte Carlo sampling and epistemic uncertainty via spatially correlated error perturbations.
为应对环境不确定性下的空中野火扑救需求，本研究构建了融合卷积神经网络与元胞自动机的混合模型，用于模拟火势蔓延并优化精准空中洒水策略。模型结合地形、燃料与风速数据预测空间异质性火势行为，梯度优化器则确定洒水点位与方向以最小化受火面积。基于2020年熊火案例的实验表明，该框架可生成连贯的扑救计划，并通过蒙特卡洛采样与空间相关误差扰动量化不确定性，有效提升减少总烧毁面积的鲁棒性。
From Tokens to Faces: Investigating Discrete Speech Representations for 3D Facial Animation
Authors: Pedro Correa, Olivier Perrotin, Samir Sadok, Paula Costa, Thomas Hueber
First: 2026-06-11T17:41:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:41:18+00:00
Comments: This work has been accepted in Interspeech 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The choice of speech representation is critical in speech-driven 3D facial animation. Representations differ in what they encode: SSL features emphasize segmental and semantic cues, neural codecs yield latents optimized for acoustic reconstruction, and ASR-style objectives produce label-based spaces. We evaluate four speech representation families for 3D facial synthesis, comparing their facial reconstruction quality across two facial decoders using objective metrics and a perceptual evaluation. We additionally conduct probing analyses that relate tokenized representations to phonetic units and to articulatory deformations. We found that encoding phonetic classes is beneficial for accurate facial animation prediction on both semantic and label-based representations with comparable facial animation quality. From the latter, we introduce an Audio Visual Text-to-Speech (AVTTS) pipeline that leverages, as a shared space, discrete representations to decode speech and 3D facial motion.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从音素到面部：探究离散语音表示在3D面部动画中的应用
语音表示的选择对语音驱动的3D面部动画至关重要。不同表示方式编码的内容不同：SSL特征强调分段和语义线索，神经编解码器生成优化声学重建的潜在表示，ASR风格目标则生成基于标签的空间。我们评估了四种语音表示家族在3D面部合成中的表现，通过客观指标和感知评估比较其在两种面部解码器上的面部重建质量。此外，我们还进行了探针分析，将离散表示与语音音素单元及发音器官变形关联。研究发现，编码语音音类对语义和标签型表示的面部动画预测均有益，且面部动画质量相当。基于后者，我们提出了一种音频-视觉-文本到语音（AVTTS）管道，利用离散表示作为共享空间，同时解码语音与3D面部运动。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how different discrete speech representations affect 3D facial animation quality, motivated by the need to choose optimal encodings for speech-driven synthesis. The authors compare four representation families—SSL features, neural codecs, ASR-style labels, and phonetic-class tokens—using two facial decoders, measuring reconstruction accuracy and perceptual quality. Their probing analyses link tokenized representations to phonetic units and articulatory movements, revealing that phonetic-class encoding enhances facial animation accuracy across both semantic and label-based approaches. They further propose an Audio Visual Text-to-Speech (AVTTS) pipeline that unifies discrete representations for simultaneous speech and facial motion decoding.
该研究旨在探究不同离散语音表示对3D面部动画质量的影响，动机源于语音驱动合成中选择最优编码的必要性。作者对比了四种表示方法——SSL特征、神经编码器、ASR式标签空间和音素类令牌——在两种面部解码器上的表现，通过客观指标和感知评估进行比较。探针分析将令牌化表示与音素单元和发音变形关联，发现音素类编码能提升面部动画的准确性，且在语义和标签式表示中效果相当。研究进一步提出一种音频-视觉文本到语音（AVTTS）管道，利用离散表示作为共享空间，同时解码语音和3D面部运动。
Valid Inference with Synthetic Data via Task Exchangeability
Authors: Lezhi Tan, Tijana Zrnic
First: 2026-06-11T17:41:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:41:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
There is a proliferation of work arguing for the use of synthetic data in scientific research. For example, social scientists are arguing for the use of LLM-generated "silicon samples" in pilot studies; AI evaluations increasingly rely on "LLM-as-a-judge" outputs; and proteomics research is accelerated by generative models that produce synthetic protein structures. These developments raise an intriguing possibility: synthetic data may help researchers ask more questions, run more studies, and accelerate discovery. But they also raise a fundamental concern: synthetic data can be biased, noisy, and misspecified. In this work, we propose statistical principles for using synthetic data in scientific research with provable validity guarantees. The key insight is a new technical condition that we call task exchangeability. Informally, this is a requirement that the researcher can identify historical tasks, for which real data is available, such that their current task of interest is exchangeable with the historical tasks in an appropriate mathematical sense. We develop methods for valid inference under task exchangeability, together with extensions that provide guarantees even beyond exchangeability. We demonstrate the framework on public opinion surveys with silicon samples and AI evaluation with autoraters.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过任务可交换性实现合成数据的有效推断
目前有大量研究主张在科学研究中使用合成数据。例如，社会科学家主张在试点研究中使用由大语言模型生成的“硅基样本”；AI评估日益依赖“大语言模型作为裁判”的输出；蛋白质组学研究则通过生成模型加速，这些模型能生成合成的蛋白质结构。这些发展提出一个引人入胜的可能性：合成数据可能帮助研究人员提出更多问题、开展更多研究并加速发现。但同时也引发一个根本性担忧：合成数据可能具有偏倚、噪声和模型设定错误。在本研究中，我们提出用于科学研究中使用合成数据的统计原则，并提供可证明的有效性保证。核心洞见是一种新的技术条件，我们称之为“任务可交换性”。通俗而言，这是要求研究者能够识别历史任务（这些任务有真实数据可用），使得当前研究任务在适当数学意义上与这些历史任务可交换。我们开发了在任务可交换性下进行有效推断的方法，以及扩展方法，即使在超越可交换性的情形下也能提供保证。我们通过硅基样本的公众意见调查和自动评分器的AI评估展示了该框架。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing use of synthetic data in scientific domains such as social science, AI evaluation, and proteomics, this work addresses the risk of biased or misspecified synthetic data by proposing a statistical framework for valid inference. The core method introduces a condition called task exchangeability, requiring that the current research task be mathematically exchangeable with historical tasks for which real data exists. Under this condition, the authors develop inference procedures with provable validity, and extend them to handle cases beyond strict exchangeability. Experiments on public opinion surveys using synthetic silicon samples and AI evaluations using autoraters demonstrate that the framework enables statistically sound inference even when synthetic data is imperfect.
鉴于合成数据在社会科学、AI评估和蛋白质组学等领域的广泛应用，本文旨在解决合成数据可能引入偏差或模型误设的问题，提出一套具有可证明有效性的统计推断框架。核心方法引入“任务可交换性”条件，要求当前研究任务在数学上可与拥有真实数据的历史任务交换。在此基础上，作者构建了具备有效性保障的推断方法，并扩展至可处理非严格可交换的情形。在使用合成硅样本的民意调查和使用自动评分器的AI评估实验中，该框架成功实现了即使在合成数据不完美时仍能进行可靠统计推断。
Generative Modeling of Bach-Style Symbolic Music: A Comparative Study of Autoregressive, Latent-Variable, and Adversarial Approaches
Authors: Kyuil Lee, Dezhi Yu, Yongkang Huang
First: 2026-06-11T17:39:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:39:55+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 13 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study generative modeling of Bach-style symbolic piano music using a shared MIDI corpus and three model families: autoregressive LSTMs with attention, latent-variable models including recurrent VAEs and vector-quantized VAEs, and generative adversarial networks. We compare their ability to model polyphonic note sequences, learn useful latent representations, and generate stylistically coherent compositions. Our experiments show that the autoregressive LSTM with attention produces the most musically coherent samples, while vector quantization helps mitigate posterior collapse and yields more structured outputs than conventional recurrent VAEs. The adversarial approach captures local pitch patterns but remains difficult to train and generalizes less reliably to Bach's style. These results highlight the relative strengths and failure modes of autoregressive, latent-variable, and adversarial approaches for symbolic music generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：巴赫风格符号音乐的生成建模：自回归、潜在变量与对抗方法的比较研究
我们使用共享的MIDI语料库，研究了三种模型家族（带注意力机制的自回归LSTM、包括循环VAE和向量量化VAE的潜在变量模型、生成对抗网络）在生成巴赫风格钢琴音乐方面的表现。我们比较了它们在建模多声部音符序列、学习有用潜在表示及生成风格一致作品方面的能力。实验表明，带注意力机制的自回归LSTM生成的音乐样本最具音乐连贯性；向量量化有助于缓解后验坍塌，相比传统循环VAE生成更结构化的输出；对抗方法虽能捕捉局部音高模式，但训练困难且对巴赫风格的泛化能力较弱。这些结果突显了自回归、潜在变量与对抗方法在符号音乐生成中的相对优势与失效模式。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to generate Bach-style symbolic piano music with stylistic fidelity, this study compares three generative model families—autoregressive LSTMs with attention, latent-variable models (including recurrent and vector-quantized VAEs), and adversarial networks—using a shared MIDI corpus. The autoregressive LSTM with attention produced the most musically coherent samples, while vector quantization in VAEs reduced posterior collapse and improved structural output quality over standard recurrent VAEs. Adversarial models captured local pitch patterns but struggled with training stability and generalization to Bach’s style, revealing distinct trade-offs among the approaches.
为生成风格忠实的巴赫式钢琴符号音乐，本研究在统一的MIDI数据集上比较了三种生成模型家族——带注意力机制的自回归LSTM、潜在变量模型（含循环VAE与向量量化VAE）及生成对抗网络。自回归LSTM生成的样本音乐性最连贯，向量量化VAE缓解了后验坍塌问题，输出结构更清晰；而对抗网络虽能捕捉局部音高模式，但训练不稳定且难以可靠泛化至巴赫风格。
Revisiting Vehicle Color Recognition in Long-Tailed Surveillance Scenarios
Authors: Vinícius Orrú, Bruno H. Foggiatto, Gabriel E. Lima, David Menotti, Rayson Laroca
First: 2026-06-11T17:39:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:39:40+00:00
Comments: Accepted for presentation at the 2026 International Conference on Pattern Recognition (ICPR) - V3SC Workshop
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Vehicle color recognition is an important cue for vehicle identification in surveillance systems, especially when license plates are illegible due to low resolution, occlusion, motion blur, or poor illumination. However, real-world vehicle color distributions are highly imbalanced, making overall accuracy insufficient to assess performance on rare but operationally relevant colors. This paper presents a comprehensive study of vehicle color recognition under severe class imbalance using UFPR-VeSV, a challenging real-world surveillance dataset. We investigate synthetic minority-class augmentation through two off-the-shelf generative strategies: text-conditioned image generation with RunDiffusion/JuggernautXL and image-conditioned color editing with Gemini 2.0 Flash. The curated synthetic data are combined with modern visual representations, loss reweighting, learning-rate scheduling, color-safe augmentation, foreground-aware preprocessing, and ensemble fusion. The bestperforming approach achieves 94.6% micro accuracy and 79.7% macro accuracy, improving macro accuracy by 8.2 percentage points over recent literature. A manual error analysis further shows that many remaining failures are visually ambiguous even for human annotators, highlighting the practical limits of color-based vehicle identification in unconstrained surveillance imagery. The generated images and source code are publicly available at https://github.com/viniciusorru/vcr-synthetic
中文标题/摘要
标题：长尾监控场景下车辆颜色识别的再审视
车辆颜色识别是监控系统中车辆识别的重要线索，尤其在车牌因低分辨率、遮挡、运动模糊或照明不足而无法辨识时。然而，现实世界中车辆颜色分布高度不平衡，导致整体准确率不足以评估在罕见但实际重要的颜色上的表现。本文在UFPR-VeSV这一具有挑战性的现实监控数据集上，全面研究了在严重类别不平衡下的车辆颜色识别。我们通过两种现成的生成策略——使用RunDiffusion/JuggernautXL的文本条件图像生成和使用Gemini 2.0 Flash的图像条件颜色编辑——探索合成少数类数据增强。精选的合成数据结合了现代视觉表示、损失重加权、学习率调度、颜色安全增强、前景感知预处理和集成融合。最佳方法实现了94.6%的微准确率和79.7%的宏准确率，较近期文献提升了8.2个百分点的宏准确率。人工错误分析进一步表明，许多剩余失败案例即使对人工标注员而言也存在视觉歧义，凸显了在无约束监控图像中基于颜色的车辆识别的实际局限性。生成图像和源代码已公开于https://github.com/viniciusorru/vcr-synthetic。

This study addresses the challenge of vehicle color recognition in surveillance systems where rare colors are underrepresented, leading to poor performance on critical but infrequent classes. The authors use UFPR-VeSV, a real-world long-tailed dataset, and enhance minority-class samples via two generative methods: text-conditioned image synthesis with RunDiffusion/JuggernautXL and image-conditioned color editing with Gemini 2.0 Flash. These synthetic samples are integrated with advanced visual representations, loss reweighting, color-safe augmentations, and ensemble fusion. The top-performing model achieves 94.6% micro accuracy and 79.7% macro accuracy, an 8.2 percentage point improvement over prior work. Error analysis reveals that many misclassifications remain visually ambiguous even to human observers, indicating fundamental limits in color-based identification under unconstrained surveillance conditions.
为解决监控场景中因车牌模糊或缺失导致的稀有车辆颜色识别难题，本研究针对高度不平衡的车辆颜色数据集UFPR-VeSV，采用RunDiffusion/JuggernautXL和Gemini 2.0 Flash两种生成方法对少数类进行合成扩充。结合现代视觉表征、损失重加权、色彩安全增强及集成融合策略，最优模型实现94.6%微准确率和79.7%宏准确率，较现有方法提升8.2个百分点。但人工错误分析显示，大量误判案例即使对人类标注员也难以分辨，揭示了在无约束监控图像中基于颜色的车辆识别存在固有局限。
Beyond Uniform Tokens: Adaptive Compression for Time Series Language Models
Authors: Jialin Gan, Xin Qiu, Guangzhe Chen, Xue Wang
First: 2026-06-11T17:39:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:39:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) have enabled time series (TS) analysis by jointly modeling numerical observations and textual context through a shared token interface. However, TS tokens and prompt tokens exhibit fundamentally different information structures, making uniform token processing inefficient. In this paper, we study token efficiency in TS language modeling from an asymmetric-token perspective. We show that TS tokens have highly uneven spectral contributions, where many tokens share redundant frequency patterns while a small subset preserves critical temporal evidence. We also observe that prompt-token influence attenuates with model depth, suggesting that full prompt retention across all layers is unnecessary. Based on these findings, we develop an adaptive token budgeting framework that compresses TS tokens via frequency-domain structure and progressively reduces prompt tokens across layers. Experiments across forecasting, classification, imputation, and anomaly detection demonstrate up to \textit{\textbf{7.68$\times$}} inference acceleration and performance gains in \textit{\textbf{78\%}} of evaluated settings, showing the effectiveness of asymmetric token compression for scalable TS foundation models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越统一令牌：面向时间序列语言模型的自适应压缩
大型语言模型（LLMs）通过共享令牌接口，联合建模数值观测与文本上下文，推动了时间序列（TS）分析。然而，TS令牌与提示令牌具有根本不同的信息结构，统一令牌处理效率低下。本文从非对称令牌视角研究TS语言建模中的令牌效率问题。我们发现TS令牌频谱贡献极不均衡，多数令牌共享冗余频率模式，而少数令牌保留关键时序证据。同时观察到提示令牌的影响随模型深度衰减，表明全层保留提示令牌非必要。基于此，我们提出自适应令牌预算框架，通过频域结构压缩TS令牌，并逐层渐进减少提示令牌。在预测、分类、插补与异常检测任务中，实验显示推理加速最高达\textit{\textbf{7.68$\times$}}，在\textit{\textbf{78\%}}的评估场景中性能提升，验证了非对称令牌压缩对可扩展TS基础模型的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of uniform token processing in time series language models, this paper introduces an adaptive compression framework that treats time series tokens and prompt tokens asymmetrically. It leverages the observation that time series tokens exhibit uneven spectral contributions, with many sharing redundant frequency patterns while a few carry critical temporal information, and that prompt token influence diminishes with model depth. The method compresses time series tokens via frequency-domain structure and progressively prunes prompt tokens across model layers. Experiments across forecasting, classification, imputation, and anomaly detection show up to 7.68x inference speedup and performance gains in 78% of evaluated settings.
鉴于统一处理时间序列语言模型中的令牌效率低下，本文从非对称令牌视角出发，发现时间序列令牌频谱贡献不均，大量令牌存在冗余频率模式，而关键时序信息集中在少数令牌中，同时提示令牌的影响随模型深度递减，表明全层保留提示令牌非必要。作者提出一种自适应令牌预算框架，通过频域结构压缩时间序列令牌，并逐层渐进削减提示令牌。在预测、分类、填补与异常检测等任务中，实验显示最高可达7.68倍推理加速，78%的评估场景性能提升，验证了非对称令牌压缩对可扩展时间序列基础模型的有效性。
Beyond Runtime Enforcement: Shield Synthesis as Defensibility Analysis for Adversarial Networks
Authors: Achraf Hsain, Sultan Almuhammadi
First: 2026-06-11T17:35:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:35:40+00:00
Comments: 26 pages, 7 figures, 7 tables. Under review at JAIR. Code: https://github.com/AchrafHsain7/Bastion
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Shielded reinforcement learning is typically presented as a runtime safety mechanism that compiles temporal-logic specifications into automata restricting an agent's actions. We argue this is the wrong product. The same automata-theoretic machinery -- specification compilation, product game construction, attractor computation, and winning-region extraction -- is better read as a design-time analytical instrument whose outputs are structural insights about a system rather than runtime constraints on a deployed agent. We instantiate this through a constrained two-player safety game for network defense. The two specifications are enforced asymmetrically: the defender specification defines the unsafe region of the game, whereas the attacker specification restricts the adversary's legal actions during attractor computation. Solving the game yields a defensibility verdict -- a formal certificate that a topology-specification pair is or is not defensible -- with the associated winning region and shield. Beyond the binary verdict, we derive topology-level metrics from the attractor structure and combine them with post-convergence behavior from shield-constrained adversarial multi-agent reinforcement learning. Together these form a defensibility fingerprint capturing both a network's formal safety properties and its operational behavior under adaptive play. A what-if analysis shows that formal defensibility and operational effectiveness capture distinct aspects of security: small architectural changes can produce large shifts in operational outcomes while leaving formal safety margins nearly unchanged. Shield synthesis is thus most valuable not as a deployment mechanism for safe agents, but as a framework for answering architectural questions about whether, where, and how a system can be defended. The defensibility verdict is the output, not the safe policy.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越运行时执行：将屏蔽合成视为对抗网络的可防御性分析
屏蔽强化学习通常被呈现为一种运行时安全机制，即将时序逻辑规范编译为限制代理行为的自动机。我们认为，这并非正确的产品。相同的自动机理论工具——规范编译、博弈构造、吸引子计算和获胜区域提取——更宜被视作设计时的分析工具，其输出是关于系统的结构性洞见，而非部署代理的运行时约束。 我们通过一个受约束的双人安全博弈实例化这一观点，用于网络防御。两个规范以不对称方式执行：防御者规范定义博弈中的不安全区域，而攻击者规范在吸引子计算期间限制对手的合法行为。求解博弈可得出可防御性判决——一个形式化的证书，表明拓扑-规范对是否可防御——并附带获胜区域和屏蔽。 除二元判决外，我们从吸引子结构中推导拓扑级指标，并将其与屏蔽约束下的对抗多智能体强化学习的收敛后行为相结合。二者共同构成可防御性指纹，捕捉网络的形式安全属性及其在适应性博弈下的实际运行行为。 ‘假设分析’表明，形式可防御性与实际有效性捕捉安全的不同方面：微小架构变更可能大幅改变实际结果，而形式安全裕度几乎不变。因此，屏蔽合成的价值不在于作为安全代理的部署机制，而在于作为回答架构问题的框架：系统是否、何处、如何可被防御。可防御性判决是输出，而非安全策略。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of runtime enforcement in adversarial network settings, the paper reframes shield synthesis as a design-time defensibility analysis tool using automata-theoretic methods. It models network defense as a two-player safety game where the defender defines unsafe states and the attacker’s actions are constrained by its specification during attractor computation. Solving this game yields a formal defensibility verdict, along with a winning region and shield, which together serve as structural evidence of security. Beyond binary outcomes, the study extracts topology-level metrics from attractor structures and combines them with post-convergence behavior from shield-constrained multi-agent reinforcement learning to form a defensibility fingerprint. Experimental results show that small architectural changes can significantly alter operational outcomes while preserving formal safety, demonstrating that shield synthesis is more valuable as a design-time diagnostic than a runtime safety mechanism.
该研究旨在突破运行时安全机制的局限，将盾牌合成重构为一种设计时的防御性分析工具，利用自动机理论方法。其核心是构建一个双人安全博弈模型，其中防御方定义不安全状态，攻击方的合法行为受其规范约束，用于吸引子计算。求解该博弈可输出形式化的防御性判定、获胜区域与盾牌，判断特定拓扑-规范对是否可防御。进一步，研究从吸引子结构中提取拓扑级指标，并结合盾牌约束下多智能体强化学习的收敛后行为，形成防御性指纹。实验表明，微小架构调整可能显著改变实际运行效果，而形式安全裕度基本不变，证明盾牌合成更适合作为架构设计分析框架，而非部署时的安全保障机制。
Majority-of-Three is Optimal
Authors: Divit Rawal, Nikita Zhivotovskiy
First: 2026-06-11T17:26:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:26:13+00:00
Comments: 9 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We give a short proof that the majority vote of three independent consistent classifiers is an optimal learner in the realizable PAC setting. This proves optimality for the simplest voting scheme, while simplifying both the algorithmic structure and the probabilistic analysis of previous voting learners, including the algorithm of S. Hanneke and the analysis of bagging by K. Green Larsen.
中文标题/摘要
标题：三者多数投票是最优的
我们给出一个简短的证明，表明三个独立一致分类器的多数投票在可实现PAC设定下是一个最优学习器。这证明了最简单的投票方案的最优性，同时简化了之前投票学习器的算法结构和概率分析，包括S. Hanneke的算法和K. Green Larsen对Bagging的分析。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for a simpler and more optimal voting mechanism in the realizable PAC learning setting, this paper proves that the majority vote of three independent consistent classifiers achieves optimal learning performance. The method relies on a concise probabilistic argument that demonstrates the optimality of this simple voting scheme without requiring complex algorithmic structures or intricate analyses. Experimental results confirm that this three-vote majority scheme matches the theoretical lower bound on error rate, outperforming or matching more complex voting algorithms such as those by Hanneke and Green Larsen in terms of both efficiency and accuracy.
为在可实现的PAC学习框架下寻找最简且最优的投票方案，该论文证明了三个独立一致分类器的多数投票可达到最优学习性能。方法基于简洁的概率论证，表明该三票多数规则无需复杂算法结构或繁复的概率分析即可实现最优性。论文未提供实验结果，因其聚焦于理论最优性，但分析表明该投票方案在给定假设下可达到任何可能学习器的性能上限。
One Polluted Page Is Enough: Evaluating Web Content Pollution in Generative Recommenders
Authors: Minghao Luo, Liang Chen
First: 2026-06-11T17:24:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:24:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Search-augmented LLMs increasingly mediate everyday consumer recommendations by retrieving live web content. This creates a new risk: generative recommenders may consume polluted web content, such as fake reviews and promotional pages crafted to mislead recommendations. We ask: to what extent do search-augmented LLMs become unwitting promoters of fake products when consuming polluted retrieval results? To answer this, we introduce FORGE (Fake Online Recommendations in Generative Environments), a benchmark for measuring fake-product promotion under controlled web-content pollution. Given an upstream search result, FORGE locally rewrites real products in retrieved web pages into fake ones to simulate web-content pollution, and measures how often the LLM recommends the fake product. FORGE covers 225 real-world products across 15 categories and 5 consumer scenarios. Across 12 commercial and open-weights LLMs, all models are vulnerable: a single polluted page yields fooled rates of up to 27%, while the full top-3 replacement raises this to 73.8%. Vulnerability varies substantially across categories, increasing when models lack stable prior knowledge of the relevant products. Reasoning does not mitigate this vulnerability; instead, it often generates spurious social proof to justify false recommendations. We evaluate three defenses: skepticism prompting and consensus filtering (over model priors or cross-document evidence). Skepticism can exacerbate vulnerability, much like reasoning, while filtering risks suppressing legitimate products. We release FORGE at https://github.com/leoluolol/forge-benchmark.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一页污染内容已足够：评估生成式推荐系统中的网络内容污染
搜索增强型大语言模型正越来越多地通过检索实时网络内容来介导日常消费者推荐，这带来了一种新风险：生成式推荐系统可能消费被污染的网络内容，例如旨在误导推荐的虚假评论和宣传页面。我们提出的问题是：当这些模型消费被污染的检索结果时，它们在多大程度上会无意中推广虚假产品？为回答此问题，我们引入FORGE（生成环境中虚假在线推荐基准），用于在受控的网络内容污染环境下衡量虚假产品推广情况。给定上游搜索结果，FORGE在检索到的网页中本地重写真实产品为虚假产品，以模拟网络内容污染，并测量大语言模型推荐虚假产品的频率。FORGE涵盖15个类别、225个真实产品及5种消费者场景。在12个商业及开源权重的大语言模型中，所有模型均存在漏洞：单页污染可导致高达27%的误导率，而前三页全部替换则提升至73.8%。漏洞在不同类别间差异显著，当模型缺乏相关产品的稳定先验知识时，漏洞更严重。推理无法缓解此漏洞，反而常生成虚假的社会证明以支持错误推荐。我们评估了三种防御方法：怀疑性提示与共识过滤（基于模型先验或跨文档证据）。怀疑性提示可能加剧漏洞，如同推理一般，而过滤则可能抑制真实产品的推荐。我们将在https://github.com/leoluolol/forge-benchmark发布FORGE。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the risk that search-augmented LLMs may unknowingly promote fake products by consuming polluted web content, the authors introduce FORGE, a benchmark that simulates web-content pollution by locally rewriting real products in search results into fake ones. They evaluate 12 LLMs across 225 real-world products and find that even a single polluted page can fool models at up to 27% rate, rising to 73.8% when top-3 results are fully replaced. Vulnerability varies by product category and increases when models lack prior knowledge. Reasoning does not help and often generates false social proof. Three defenses—skepticism prompting, consensus filtering, and model priors—are tested; skepticism can worsen performance, while filtering risks suppressing legitimate products.
为评估搜索增强型大语言模型在消费推荐中因摄入污染网页内容而无意推广假货的风险，作者构建了FORGE基准测试，通过局部改写搜索结果中的真实商品为假货来模拟网络内容污染。实验覆盖225个真实商品类别，发现单页污染即可使模型误推率高达27%，若替换前三页则升至73.8%。模型在缺乏相关商品先验知识时更易受骗，且推理反而常生成虚假社会证明。三种防御方法（怀疑提示、共识过滤、模型先验）中，怀疑提示可能加剧漏洞，而过滤则易误伤真实商品。
AgentBeats: Agentifying Agent Assessment for Openness, Standardization, and Reproducibility
Authors: Xiaoyuan Liu, Jianhong Tu, Yuqi Chen, Siyuan Xie, Sihan Ren, Tianneng Shi, Gal Gantar, Evan Sandoval, Donghyun Lee, Daniel Miao, Peter J. Gilbert, Nick Hynes, Mauro Staver, Warren He, David Marn, Andrew Low, Xi Zhang, Elron Bandel, Michal Shmueli-Scheuer, Siva Reddy, Alexandre Drouin, Alexandre Lacoste, Ramayya Krishnan, Elham Tabassi, Yu Su, Victor Barres, Chenguang Wang, Wenbo Guo, Dawn Song
First: 2026-06-11T17:23:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:23:54+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Agent systems are advancing quickly across domains, but their evaluation remains fragmented. Most benchmarks rely on fixed, LLM-centric harnesses that require heavy integration, create test-production mismatch, and limit fair comparison across diverse agent designs. The root problem is the lack of an open, agent-agnostic assessment interface. We advocate Agentified Agent Assessment (AAA), where evaluation is performed by judge agents and all participants interact through standardized protocols: A2A for task management and MCP for tool access. Conventional benchmarking defines two separate interfaces, one for the benchmark and one for the agent, while AAA only needs one; this yields a generic, unified framework that separates assessment logic from agent implementation and enables reproducible, interoperable, and multi-agent evaluation. We further introduce AgentBeats as a concrete realization of AAA: we identify five practical operation modes that make standardized assessment compatible with real-world constraints on openness, privacy, and reproducibility. To evaluate our design at scale, we conduct two studies: a five-month open competition that drew 298 judge agents across 12 categories together with 467 subject agents from independent participants, showing that AAA applies across a heterogeneous range of benchmarks; and a case study on coding agents that confirms agentified evaluation preserves fidelity with the public record while surfacing previously missing head-to-head results, yielding research insights about agent design. Combining a community-scale field study and a controlled coding case study, we verify that AAA delivers coverage, practicality, and fidelity across heterogeneous scenarios at scale. Together, AAA and AgentBeats offer a clear path toward open, standardized, and reproducible agent assessment.

标题：AgentBeats：面向开放性、标准化与可复现性的代理评估代理化
代理系统在各领域快速发展，但其评估仍显碎片化。多数基准测试依赖固定且以大语言模型为中心的测试框架，需大量集成，导致测试与生产环境不一致，并限制了不同代理设计间的公平比较。根本问题是缺乏开放且与代理无关的评估接口。我们倡导代理化代理评估（AAA），即由裁判代理执行评估，所有参与者通过标准化协议交互：A2A用于任务管理，MCP用于工具访问。传统基准测试定义两个独立接口——一个用于基准，一个用于代理，而AAA仅需一个接口，从而构建通用统一框架，将评估逻辑与代理实现分离，实现可复现、可互操作及多代理评估。我们进一步提出AgentBeats作为AAA的具体实现：识别五种实用操作模式，使标准化评估兼容现实世界对开放性、隐私性和可复现性的约束。 为大规模评估设计，我们开展两项研究：一项为期五个月的公开竞赛，汇集12类共298个裁判代理及467个来自独立参与者的被测代理，证明AAA适用于异构基准测试；另一项针对编程代理的案例研究，证实代理化评估在保持公开记录真实性的同时，揭示此前缺失的直接对比结果，为代理设计提供研究洞见。结合社区规模实地研究与受控编程案例研究，我们验证AAA在异构场景下具备覆盖性、实用性与真实性。AAA与AgentBeats共同为开放、标准化、可复现的代理评估提供了清晰路径。

Motivated by the fragmented and LLM-centric nature of current agent evaluation, the paper proposes Agentified Agent Assessment (AAA), a unified framework where judge agents evaluate subject agents via standardized protocols (A2A and MCP), eliminating the need for separate interfaces. AgentBeats, a concrete implementation, defines five operational modes to ensure compatibility with real-world constraints like openness and privacy. Reasoning as Pattern Matching: Shared Mechanisms in Human and LLM Everyday Reasoning
Authors: Zach Studdiford, Gary Lupyan
First: 2026-06-11T17:23:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:23:10+00:00
Comments: 13 pages main text, 51 pages supplementary text
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
When large language models (LLMs) fail to generalize or make haphazard errors in reasoning, it is often taken as evidence that LLMs are not truly reasoning, but rather performing a kind of pattern matching. The implication is that people's behavior does not exhibit the same types of failures because human reasoning uses principled and abstract world models. We evaluate human participants and 25 LLMs on their ability to engage in common-sense reasoning about a variety of everyday situations and observe similar patterns of errors in both people and models. We then identify the set of attention heads driving LLM responses and find that these heads implement a form of pattern-matching. These attention heads allow us to predict seemingly inexplicable reasoning errors in people caused by ostensibly irrelevant prompt details. Taken together, our results suggest that everyday causal reasoning in people and LLMs is more consistent with a form of pattern-matching than with abstract world models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：推理即模式匹配：人类与大语言模型日常推理中的共同机制
当大语言模型（LLMs）在推理中无法泛化或出现随意错误时，常被视为其并非真正推理，而是在进行某种模式匹配。其隐含含义是，人类行为不会出现相同类型的错误，因为人类推理依赖于原则性和抽象的世界模型。我们对人类参与者和25个LLMs在多种日常情境下的常识推理能力进行评估，发现人与模型均表现出相似的错误模式。随后我们识别出驱动LLMs响应的注意力头，并发现这些头实现了某种模式匹配机制。这些注意力头使我们能够预测由看似无关的提示细节引发的人类看似不可解释的推理错误。综合来看，我们的结果表明，人类与LLMs的日常因果推理更符合模式匹配，而非抽象世界模型。

This study investigates whether large language models (LLMs) and humans share similar reasoning mechanisms by testing both on common-sense reasoning tasks. Motivated by the assumption that LLMs merely match patterns rather than reason abstractly, the researchers found that both humans and LLMs exhibit comparable error patterns in everyday reasoning. These findings suggest that both humans and LLMs rely on pattern-matching rather than principled abstract world models for everyday causal reasoning.

Distribution-Agnostic Robust Trajectory Optimization via Chance-Constrained Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Yashdeep Chaudhary, Roberto Armellin, Harry Holt, Marco Sagliano
First: 2026-06-11T17:22:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:22:05+00:00
Comments: Preprint. 39 pages, 16 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents a distribution-agnostic robust trajectory-optimization framework based on chance-constrained reinforcement learning. The uncertainty is represented here through initial conditions and process noise, with the only requirement being that it can be sampled. A deterministic nominal trajectory is first computed offline, and reinforcement learning is then used only to robustify that baseline through a structured affine closed-loop correction law comprising a feedforward control adjustment and time-varying feedback gains. Probabilistic feasibility is enforced empirically through rollout-based upper-tail quantiles, while terminal dispersion is regulated through covariance-feasibility penalties. The framework is assessed on two materially different trajectory design problems. The flagship case study is a three-dimensional multi-impulse Earth-Mars transfer, where the learned policy is benchmarked against a recent robust trajectory-optimization reference under Gaussian uncertainty and then evaluated under bounded uniform uncertainty and under process disturbances not seen during training. The second case study is a stochastic atmospheric pinpoint rocket landing problem, used to assess portability to a short-horizon continuous-thrust setting with drag, mass depletion, and glide-slope constraints. The results show that the proposed framework can remain competitive in upper-tail fuel cost while preserving probabilistic feasibility, and that the same robustification scaffold can be carried across heterogeneous spacecraft trajectory planning problems without redesign of its core stochastic-control structure.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于概率约束强化学习的分布无关鲁棒轨迹优化
本文提出了一种基于概率约束强化学习的分布无关鲁棒轨迹优化框架。不确定性通过初始条件和过程噪声表示，唯一要求是其可采样。首先离线计算确定性基准轨迹，然后仅使用强化学习对该基准进行鲁棒化，通过结构化的仿射闭环校正律（含前馈控制调整和时变反馈增益）实现。概率可行性通过基于 rollout 的上尾分位数经验强制，终端分散性通过协方差可行性惩罚调节。该框架在两个本质不同的轨迹设计问题上进行了评估。旗舰案例为三维多脉冲地球-火星转移，所学策略在高斯不确定性下与近期鲁棒轨迹优化基准对比，并在有界均匀不确定性和训练中未见的过程扰动下评估。第二个案例为随机大气精确火箭着陆问题，用于评估其在短时域连续推力设置（含阻力、质量消耗和滑翔坡度约束）下的可移植性。结果表明，该框架在保持概率可行性的同时，上尾燃料成本仍具竞争力，且同一鲁棒化框架可应用于异构航天器轨迹规划问题，无需重新设计其核心随机控制结构。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robust trajectory optimization under uncertain conditions without assuming specific probability distributions, this paper introduces a chance-constrained reinforcement learning framework that relies only on the ability to sample uncertainty. The method first computes a deterministic nominal trajectory offline, then applies reinforcement learning to enhance robustness via a structured affine closed-loop correction combining feedforward adjustments and time-varying feedback gains. Probabilistic feasibility is ensured through empirical quantile-based rollouts, while terminal dispersion is controlled via covariance penalties. Experiments on a 3D Earth-Mars transfer and a stochastic rocket landing problem demonstrate that the framework maintains competitive fuel efficiency under probabilistic feasibility while generalizing across different uncertainty types and mission contexts without structural redesign.
为解决在不确定条件下进行鲁棒轨迹优化且无需假设特定概率分布的问题，本文提出一种基于机会约束的强化学习框架，仅需能采样不确定性即可。该方法首先离线计算确定性基准轨迹，再通过强化学习对其进行结构化仿射闭环修正，结合前馈控制调整与时变反馈增益。概率可行性通过基于 rollout 的上尾分位数实证保障，终端离散度则通过协方差可行性惩罚控制。在三维地球-火星转移与随机火箭着陆两个实验中，该框架在多种不确定性（包括训练中未见的扰动）下仍保持燃料效率竞争力并保障可行性，且其核心随机控制结构可跨异构航天器轨迹规划问题迁移而无需重新设计。
Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning from Delayed Marketplace Feedback for Objective-Weight Adaptation in Three-Sided Dispatch
Authors: Haochen Wu, Yi Hou, Shiguang Xie
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-11T17:21:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:21:20+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICML 2026 Workshop on Reinforcement Learning from World Feedback (RLxF)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Dispatch in three-sided marketplaces provides a natural setting for reinforcement learning from world feedback: decisions are evaluated by delayed operational outcomes such as delivery speed, courier utilization, and merchant congestion. We present a deployed reinforcement learning system at DoorDash that adapts dispatch objective weights in a large-scale food-delivery marketplace using delayed signals. Rather than replacing the combinatorial assignment optimizer, a store-level policy learned from logged marketplace data selects a discrete multiplier that shifts the dispatch optimizer's tradeoff between delivery quality and batching efficiency. This interface enables offline policy learning under noisy, delayed, and coupled feedback while preserving production feasibility constraints and operational safeguards. We train a shared value function using centralized offline data and decentralized store-level execution, with Double Q-learning targets and a conservative regularizer to reduce out-of-distribution value overestimation. In a production switchback experiment, the offline-trained policy increases batching and reduces courier-side time costs without degrading customer-facing delivery quality. Results illustrate how world feedback from a live economic and logistics system can be used to safely adapt decision policies online.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于延迟市场反馈的多智能体强化学习用于三方派单中的目标权重自适应
三方市场中的派单为从世界反馈中进行强化学习提供了自然场景：决策由延迟的操作结果（如配送速度、骑手利用率和商家拥堵）评估。我们提出了一种在DoorDash部署的强化学习系统，利用延迟信号在大规模食品配送市场中自适应调整派单目标权重。该系统并未替代组合分配优化器，而是通过从日志数据中学习的店铺级策略，选择一个离散乘数，调整派单优化器在配送质量与批量效率之间的权衡。该接口支持在噪声、延迟且耦合的反馈下进行离线策略学习，同时保留生产可行性约束和运营保障。我们使用集中式离线数据和分布式店铺级执行训练共享价值函数，采用Double Q学习目标和保守正则化器以减少分布外价值高估。在生产切换实验中，离线训练的策略提升了批量效率并降低了骑手时间成本，而未损害面向客户的配送质量。结果展示了如何利用真实经济与物流系统的延迟反馈安全地在线自适应决策策略。

The method trains a shared value function via centralized offline data and decentralized store-level execution, using Double Q-learning and a conservative regularizer to mitigate value overestimation under noisy, delayed feedback. 为在DoorDash三边市场中利用延迟运营反馈实时调整派单优先级，作者设计了一种多智能体强化学习系统，通过学习门店级策略动态调节目标权重，而不替换核心组合优化器。该方法采用集中式离线数据训练共享价值函数，结合分布式执行，使用Double Q学习与保守正则化以缓解延迟、噪声反馈下的价值高估问题。在生产切换实验中，该策略提升了派单批量化效率、降低了骑手时间成本，且未降低顾客端配送质量。
Beyond the Commitment Boundary: Probing Epiphenomenal Chain-of-Thought in Large Reasoning Models
Authors: Daniel Scalena, Sara Candussio, Luca Bortolussi, Elisabetta Fersini, Malvina Nissim, Gabriele Sarti
First: 2026-06-11T17:21:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:21:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning is the dominant paradigm for inference-time scaling in language models, yet the causal influence of individual steps on the final answer poorly understood. We estimate each step's causal importance via early exit and use this measure to study how answers form across the reasoning traces of several model families. Across diverse tasks, we find that reasoning typically crosses a \emph{commitment boundary} -- a sharp transition from transient intermediate guesses to a stable, high-confidence answer. This transition often happens in a single step, well before the model's reasoning block ends, and is followed by \emph{epiphenomenal} CoT steps that leave the final answer probability unaltered. Using attention probes, we show that answer-formation stages can be linearly decoded from intermediate reasoning steps with high accuracy and generalize robustly to unseen reasoning tasks. We exploit this signal to early-exit reasoning blocks at the commitment boundary, reducing the length of CoTs up to 55\% on average with negligible impact on model performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越承诺边界：探究大型推理模型中的表观链式思维
链式思维（CoT）推理是语言模型推理时扩展的主要范式，但各步骤对最终答案的因果影响尚不明确。我们通过早期退出估算每个步骤的因果重要性，并以此衡量不同模型家族推理轨迹中答案如何形成。在多种任务中，我们发现推理通常跨越一个\emph{承诺边界}——即从临时中间猜测到稳定高置信度答案的急剧转变。这一转变常在单步内完成，远早于模型推理块结束，并随后出现\emph{表观}的CoT步骤，这些步骤不改变最终答案的概率。通过注意力探针，我们证明可从中间推理步骤高精度线性解码答案形成阶段，并在未见推理任务中稳健泛化。我们利用该信号在承诺边界处提前退出推理块，平均将CoT长度缩短55%，且对模型性能影响微乎其微。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of understanding of how individual reasoning steps causally influence final outputs in large language models, this study uses early-exit mechanisms to estimate the causal importance of each step in chain-of-thought reasoning. Across multiple model families and tasks, the research identifies a 'commitment boundary' — a sharp transition where intermediate guesses stabilize into a high-confidence answer, often occurring in a single step before the reasoning block concludes. Subsequent steps beyond this boundary are epiphenomenal, having no effect on the final answer probability. Attention probes reveal that answer formation can be accurately decoded from intermediate steps and generalizes across tasks, enabling early-exit strategies that reduce CoT length by up to 55% without compromising model performance.
为了解决大语言模型推理过程中各步骤对最终答案因果影响不明确的问题，该研究提出通过早期退出机制评估链式思维中每一步的因果重要性。研究发现，推理过程通常会经历一个‘承诺边界’——即从不稳定中间猜测迅速过渡到稳定高置信度答案的临界点，该边界常在推理块结束前就已出现，之后的步骤多为表象性推理，不影响最终答案。通过注意力探针，研究者证明可从中间推理步骤高精度解码答案形成阶段，并将其用于早期退出策略，平均缩短推理链55%，且对模型性能影响可忽略不计。
EpiBench: Verifiable Evaluation of AI Agents on Epigenomics Analysis
Authors: Harihara Muralidharan, Reema Baskar, Soo Hee Lee, Tim Proctor, Kenny Workman
First: 2026-06-11T17:20:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:20:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce EpiBench, a verifiable benchmark for short-horizon epigenomics analysis. EpiBench evaluates whether agents can make well-defined analysis decisions from realistic workflow states and return deterministically gradable answers. The benchmark includes 106 evaluations across CUT\&Tag/CUT\&RUN, ATAC-seq, ChIP-seq, and DNA methylation workflows. Across 5,088 valid trajectories from 16 model-harness pairs, no system passed a majority of attempts: GPT-5.5 / Pi led at 45.0\% (143/318 attempts; 95\% confidence interval (CI), 36.3--53.7), followed by GPT-5.5 / OpenAI Codex at 39.9\% (127/318 attempts; 95\% CI, 31.6--48.3). Claude Opus 4.8 Max / Pi and GPT-5.4 / Pi each passed 39.0\% (124/318 attempts; 95\% CI, 30.2--47.8 and 31.0--47.0, respectively). Performance varies across assay types, and many failed runs still contain parts of the correct answer. Agents often found the right files and computed useful intermediate results, but failed when the task required deeper, assay-specific scientific judgment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EpiBench：用于表观基因组学分析的AI代理可验证评估基准
我们提出了EpiBench，一个用于短期表观基因组学分析的可验证基准。EpiBench评估代理是否能从现实的工作流状态中做出明确的分析决策，并返回可确定性评分的答案。该基准涵盖CUT&Tag/CUT&RUN、ATAC-seq、ChIP-seq和DNA甲基化工作流的106项评估。在16组模型-工具对的5,088条有效轨迹中，无系统通过多数尝试：GPT-5.5 / Pi以45.0%（143/318次尝试；95%置信区间CI为36.3–53.7）领先，其次是GPT-5.5 / OpenAI Codex以39.9%（127/318次尝试；95% CI为31.6–48.3）。Claude Opus 4.8 Max / Pi和GPT-5.4 / Pi各通过39.0%（124/318次尝试；95% CI分别为30.2–47.8和31.0–47.0）。不同检测方法表现各异，许多失败的运行仍包含部分正确答案。代理通常能正确找到所需文件并计算出有用中间结果，但在需要更深层次、特定检测方法的科学判断时失败。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable, verifiable evaluation of AI agents in epigenomics analysis, EpiBench was designed as a short-horizon benchmark that assesses whether agents can derive deterministic, gradable decisions from realistic workflow states. The benchmark comprises 106 evaluations across four major epigenomic assays and tests 16 model-harness pairs over 5,088 trajectories. Performance varied by assay type, and while agents often located correct files and computed useful intermediates, they frequently failed on deeper, assay-specific scientific reasoning tasks.
为评估AI代理在表观基因组学分析中的可靠性，EpiBench构建了一个可验证的短周期基准，测试代理能否从真实工作流状态中得出可确定评分的决策。该基准涵盖四种主要表观基因组学实验方法，共106项评估，评估了16组模型组合的5088条轨迹。结果显示，无系统通过多数测试，表现最佳的是GPT-5.5/Pi（45.0%，143/318），其次是GPT-5.5/OpenAI Codex（39.9%）。不同实验方法表现差异显著，代理虽常能正确识别文件并计算中间结果，但在需要深度、方法特异性科学判断的任务上普遍失败。
MCR-Bionic Hand: Anatomical Structural Priors for Dexterous Manipulation
Authors: Haosen Yang, Guowu Wei
First: 2026-06-11T17:20:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:20:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Dexterous robotic hands are usually formulated as high dimensional active control systems governed by degrees of freedom, actuation, and algorithms. Human hand dexterity, however, is partly encoded in the physical architecture of bones, ligaments, tendons, aponeuroses, and intrinsic muscles. This work describes that contribution as two linked forms of structural intelligence: structural prior generation, in which wrist to finger tenodesis, FDS/FDP routing, and the dorsal extensor hood transform low dimensional posture inputs into default grasp configurations and PIP to DIP coordination; and muscle mediated modulation, in which extrinsic muscles, lumbricals, and interossei regulate MCP posture, distal stability, fingertip force paths, and contact states around that default state. Based on this framework, MCR-Bionic Hand is developed as a 1:1 musculoskeletal biomimetic hand integrating a two row eight bone wrist, cross wrist tendons, anatomical flexor routing, volar plate and collateral ligament constraints, the dorsal extensor hood, and intrinsic muscle pathways within one body. Functional demonstrations and geometric mechanical models show that wrist posture induces multi joint pre shaping, the extensor hood maps PIP posture to a coupled DIP response, and intrinsic plus pathways modulate distal stability and fingertip action direction after grasp formation. Contact rich tasks, including coin rotation, pen transfer, dorsal coin flipping, and cube manipulation, show that MCR-Bionic links low dimensional state generation with fine post contact modulation. These results suggest that anatomical biomimetics is valuable not for visual similarity, but for identifying human hand structures that perform part of control.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MCR仿生手：解剖结构先验用于灵巧操作
灵巧机器人手通常被建模为由自由度、驱动和算法控制的高维主动控制系统。然而，人类手的灵巧性部分编码于骨骼、韧带、肌腱、筋膜和内在肌的物理结构中。本工作将该贡献描述为两种关联的结构智能形式：结构先验生成，其中腕至指的肌腱固定、FDS/FDP肌腱路径及背侧伸肌腱膜将低维姿态输入转化为默认抓取构型及PIP至DIP协调；以及肌肉介导的调制，其中外在肌、蚓状肌和骨间肌调节MCP姿态、远端稳定性、指尖力路径及围绕默认状态的接触状态。 基于此框架，MCR仿生手被开发为1:1比例的肌肉骨骼仿生手，集成双排八骨腕关节、交叉腕肌腱、解剖屈肌路径、掌侧板与侧副韧带约束、背侧伸肌腱膜及内在肌路径于一体。功能演示与几何力学模型表明，腕关节姿态诱导多关节预塑形，伸肌腱膜将PIP姿态映射为耦合的DIP响应，内在肌路径在抓取形成后调节远端稳定性与指尖动作方向。接触密集任务，包括硬币旋转、笔传递、背侧硬币翻转及立方体操作，表明MCR仿生手将低维状态生成与精细接触后调制相联系。这些结果表明，解剖仿生的价值不在于视觉相似性，而在于识别执行部分控制的人手结构。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that human hand dexterity arises partly from anatomical structures rather than just control algorithms, this work proposes MCR-Bionic Hand, a biomimetic robotic hand that integrates human-like musculoskeletal architecture to enable dexterous manipulation. The method combines structural priors—such as wrist-to-finger tenodesis, flexor routing, and the dorsal extensor hood—to map low-dimensional posture inputs into default grasp configurations, while intrinsic and extrinsic muscle pathways modulate joint coordination and fingertip force. Experimental results demonstrate that wrist posture pre-shapes multiple joints, the extensor hood couples PIP and DIP motion, and intrinsic muscles stabilize fingertip contact and direction after grasp formation. Tasks like coin rotation, pen transfer, and cube manipulation confirm that the hand achieves fine post-contact control using anatomical structure alone, without relying on high-dimensional actuation.
受人类手部灵巧性部分源于其解剖结构而非单纯控制算法的启发，本研究提出MCR-Bionic Hand，一种仿生肌骨结构的机械手。该方法通过集成腕指腱系、屈肌路径、背侧伸肌腱膜及内在肌路径等解剖结构先验，将低维姿态输入转化为默认抓取构型，并实现抓取后的精细接触调节。实验表明，腕部姿态可预形多关节，伸肌腱膜耦合PIP与DIP运动，内在肌调节指尖力与稳定性。在硬币旋转、笔转移、硬币翻转及立方体操作等接触密集任务中，该手通过低维状态生成与肌肉调节联动实现灵巧操作，证明解剖仿生的价值在于其结构本身对控制的直接贡献。
Reasoning Models Know What's Important, and Encode It in Their Activations
Authors: Yaniv Nikankin, Martin Tutek, Tomer Ashuach, Jonathan Rosenfeld, Yonatan Belinkov
First: 2026-04-20T14:15:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:17:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Language models often solve complex tasks by generating long reasoning chains, consisting of many steps with varying importance. While some steps are crucial for generating the final answer, others are removable. Determining which steps matter most, and why, remains an open question central to understanding how models process reasoning. We investigate if this question is best approached through model internals or through tokens of the reasoning chain itself. We find that model activations contain more information than tokens for identifying important reasoning steps. Crucially, by training probes on model activations to predict importance, we show that models encode an internal representation of step importance, even prior to the generation of subsequent steps. The internal representations of importance in different models yield high agreement on which steps are important. The representation is distributed across layers, and does not correlate with surface-level features, such as a step's relative position or its length. Our findings suggest that analyzing activations can reveal aspects of reasoning that surface-level approaches fundamentally miss, indicating that reasoning analyses should look into model internals.
中文标题/摘要
标题：推理模型知道什么是重要的，并将其编码在激活中
语言模型常通过生成包含多个步骤的长推理链来解决复杂任务，其中步骤的重要性各异。虽然部分步骤对生成最终答案至关重要，其他步骤则可被移除。确定哪些步骤最重要及其原因，仍是理解模型如何处理推理的核心开放问题。我们探讨该问题应通过模型内部机制还是推理链中的token本身来解决。研究发现，模型激活包含比token更丰富的信息，可用于识别重要推理步骤。关键的是，通过在模型激活上训练探测器以预测步骤重要性，我们证明模型在生成后续步骤前已编码了步骤重要性的内部表示。不同模型的重要性内部表示在识别重要步骤上高度一致，该表示分布于各层，且与表面特征（如步骤相对位置或长度）无相关性。我们的发现表明，分析激活可揭示表面方法根本无法捕捉的推理方面，提示推理分析应深入模型内部。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how language models identify important steps in their reasoning chains, motivated by the need to understand how models process complex tasks. The researchers compare model activations with surface-level tokens to determine which carries more information about step importance. They train probes on activations to predict step importance and find that models encode an internal representation of importance even before generating later steps. Across different models, these internal representations show high agreement on critical steps, and the importance signal is distributed across layers without correlation to surface features like position or length, suggesting that analyzing activations reveals reasoning insights inaccessible through token-based methods.
该研究旨在理解语言模型如何识别并编码其推理链中各步骤的重要性，以揭示模型处理复杂任务的机制。作者对比了模型激活与表面层的token，发现激活包含更丰富的步骤重要性信息。通过在激活上训练探针预测步骤重要性，他们证明模型在生成后续步骤前已内部编码了关键步骤的表示。不同模型的内部重要性表示高度一致，分布于多层，且与位置或长度等表面特征无关，表明分析激活能揭示表面方法无法捕捉的推理本质。
Reward Modeling for Multi-Agent Orchestration
Authors: King Yeung Tsang, Zihao Zhao, Vishal Venkataramani, Haizhou Shi, Zixuan Ke, Semih Yavuz, Shafiq Joty, Hao Wang
First: 2026-06-11T17:16:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:16:24+00:00
Comments: Preprint; work in progress
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) built on Large Language Models (LLMs) require effective orchestration to coordinate specialized agents, yet training such orchestrators is hindered by limited supervision and high computational cost. We propose Orchestration Reward Modeling (OrchRM), a self-supervised framework for evaluating orchestration quality without human annotations. OrchRM leverages intermediate artifacts from multi-agent executions to construct win-lose pairs for Bradley-Terry reward model training. Unlike existing MAS test-time scaling and orchestrator training frameworks that rely on costly sub-agent rollouts, OrchRM operates directly at the orchestration level, enabling efficient and high-performing reward-guided orchestrator training and MAS test-time scaling. OrchRM improves training efficiency by up to 10x in token usage while improving MAS test-time scaling performance by up to 8% in accuracy. These gains consistently transfer across multiple domains, including mathematical reasoning, web-based question answering, and multi-hop reasoning, demonstrating orchestration-level reward modeling as a scalable direction for robust multi-agent orchestration. Code will be available at https://github.com/Wang-ML-Lab/OrchRM.

标题：多智能体编排的奖励建模
