To address the challenge of training orchestrators for multi-agent systems built on large language models, which suffer from limited supervision and high computational cost, the authors propose OrchRM, a self-supervised reward modeling framework that evaluates orchestration quality using intermediate execution artifacts to generate win-lose pairs for training a Bradley-Terry reward model. Unlike prior methods that require costly sub-agent rollouts, OrchRM operates directly at the orchestration level, enabling efficient reward-guided training and test-time scaling. Experimental results show OrchRM reduces token usage by up to 10x while improving test-time accuracy by up to 8% across multiple domains including mathematical reasoning, web QA, and multi-hop reasoning, demonstrating its scalability and effectiveness.
为解决基于大语言模型的多智能体系统中训练协调器面临监督不足和计算成本高的问题，本文提出OrchRM框架，通过利用多智能体执行过程中的中间产物构建胜败对，训练布拉德利-特里奖励模型，实现无需人工标注的自监督评估。该方法直接在协调层操作，避免昂贵的子智能体回滚，从而在训练和测试时实现高效奖励引导，使token使用减少10倍，准确率提升8%，并在数学推理、网页问答、多跳推理等多个领域稳定表现，验证了协调层奖励建模的可扩展性和鲁棒性。
Variational Learning for Insertion-based Generation
Authors: Yangtian Zhang, Zhe Wang, Arthur Gretton, Rex Ying, David van Dijk, Michalis K. Titsias, Jiaxin Shi
First: 2026-06-01T11:59:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:13:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Non-monotonic sequence generation methods, such as masked diffusion models, provide a flexible alternative to left-to-right autoregressive modeling by allowing tokens to be generated in non-fixed and prescribed orders. Despite their practical advantages, most existing non-monotonic models are order-agnostic and rely on a fixed-length grid, limiting their ability to support variable-length generation and adaptive insertion order. In this work, we introduce a probabilistic framework for learning insertion order in variable-length insertion models. We formalize a bijective correspondence between insertion trajectories and permutations, which enables an exact reparameterization of the data likelihood as a sum over permutations. Building on this result, we propose the Insertion Process (IP), a stochastic generative model that jointly learns where to insert, what to insert, and when to terminate, trained via permutation-based variational inference. Unlike prior fixed-canvas approaches, IP natively supports variable-length generation and learns data-driven preferences over insertion orders. Experiments on goal-conditioned planning and molecular string generation demonstrate that learning insertion order improves both modeling quality and generalization in domains without a canonical left-to-right structure.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于插入的生成的变分学习
非单调序列生成方法（如掩码扩散模型）通过允许令牌按非固定和预设顺序生成，为自左向右的自回归建模提供了灵活的替代方案。尽管具有实际优势，大多数现有非单调模型是顺序无关的，并依赖固定长度网格，限制了其支持可变长度生成和自适应插入顺序的能力。本文提出了一种用于学习可变长度插入模型中插入顺序的概率框架。我们形式化了插入轨迹与排列之间的一一对应关系，从而实现了数据似然的精确重参数化，即作为排列之和。基于此结果，我们提出了插入过程（IP），一种联合学习插入位置、插入内容和终止时机的随机生成模型，通过基于排列的变分推断进行训练。与先前的固定画布方法不同，IP 原生支持可变长度生成，并学习数据驱动的插入顺序偏好。在目标条件规划和分子字符串生成实验中，结果表明学习插入顺序可提升无标准左到右结构领域的建模质量和泛化能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of fixed-order non-monotonic generation models, this work proposes a probabilistic framework to learn adaptive insertion orders in variable-length generation. It formalizes a bijective mapping between insertion trajectories and permutations, enabling exact reparameterization of likelihood as a sum over permutations. The Insertion Process (IP) is then introduced as a stochastic model that jointly learns insertion positions, content, and termination via permutation-based variational inference. Experiments on goal-conditioned planning and molecular string generation show IP achieves superior modeling quality and generalization compared to fixed-order baselines, especially in domains lacking canonical left-to-right structure.
为克服现有非单调生成模型固定顺序和固定长度的局限，本文提出一种概率框架，用于学习可变长度序列生成中的自适应插入顺序。通过建立插入轨迹与排列之间的双射关系，实现数据似然的精确重参数化，并提出插入过程（IP）模型，该模型通过排列基础变分推断联合学习插入位置、内容及终止时机。在目标引导规划与分子字符串生成实验中，IP在缺乏标准左到右结构的领域中展现出更优的建模能力和泛化性能。
Multiagent Protocols with Aggregated Confidence Signals
Authors: Ali Elahi, Barbara Di Eugenio
First: 2026-06-11T17:12:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:12:11+00:00
Comments: 22 pages and 5 figures, 9 pages and 2 figures before the appendix
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Confidence is used for reliability, oversight, and a range of downstream decision tasks in Natural Language Processing (NLP), yet no existing method produces or evaluates a confidence for the output of a multiagent system. Prior work uses confidence within multiagent debate (MAD) to weight messages, trigger debate, or calibrate individual agents, but it never aggregates these into a single confidence for the system itself. We introduce three protocols that produce a final answer along with a single aggregated confidence by first transforming raw confidence signals to make them comparable across models, then combining them via soft voting or a probability fusion we call Bayesian fusion. This aggregated confidence is substantially more discriminative (AUARC) than that of the best single agent or the standard debate baselines, while correctness (F1-score) stays stable and recovers the losses MAD incurs on more ambiguous tasks. Analyzing two estimators, sequence probability and self-report, alongside parametric and non-parametric calibrators, we find that calibration improves F1 for both estimators while AUARC is less reliant on it. We evaluate six homogeneous and heterogeneous debating pairs per benchmark, across five benchmarks and four task types, spanning a range of model capabilities and sizes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多智能体协议与聚合置信度信号
置信度在自然语言处理（NLP）中用于可靠性、监督及多种下游决策任务，但现有方法尚未生成或评估多智能体系统的输出置信度。先前工作在多智能体辩论（MAD）中使用置信度加权消息、触发辩论或校准单个智能体，但从未将其聚合为系统自身的单一置信度。我们提出三种协议，首先将原始置信度信号转换为跨模型可比的形式，再通过软投票或称为贝叶斯融合的概率融合方法进行组合，从而生成最终答案及单一聚合置信度。该聚合置信度在区分度（AUARC）上显著优于最佳单智能体或标准辩论基线，同时正确率（F1分数）保持稳定，并弥补了MAD在更模糊任务中所损失的性能。我们分析了序列概率与自我报告两种估计器，以及参数化与非参数化校准器，发现校准可提升两种估计器的F1分数，而AUARC对校准的依赖性较低。我们在五个基准、四种任务类型上评估了六组同质与异质辩论对，覆盖了不同模型能力和规模。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of reliable confidence estimation for multiagent systems in NLP, this work introduces three protocols that generate a final answer and a single aggregated confidence by first normalizing raw confidence signals across agents and then combining them via soft voting or Bayesian fusion. Experiments across five benchmarks and four task types show that the aggregated confidence significantly outperforms individual agents or standard debate baselines in discriminative power (AUARC), while maintaining stable correctness (F1-score) and recovering losses incurred by multiagent debate on ambiguous tasks. Calibration further improves F1 for both sequence probability and self-report estimators, though AUARC performance is less dependent on calibration.
为解决多智能体系统在自然语言处理中缺乏置信度评估的问题，本文提出三种协议，通过先将原始置信度信号标准化以实现跨模型可比性，再通过软投票或贝叶斯融合生成最终答案与单一聚合置信度。实验表明，聚合置信度在区分能力（AUARC）上显著优于单个智能体或标准辩论基线，同时在模糊任务上保持或恢复了正确率（F1分数）。校准可提升两种估计器（序列概率与自我报告）的F1分数，但对AUARC性能影响较小。
Simplex-Constrained Sparse Bagging: Transitioning from Uniform Priors to Sparse Posteriors in Ensemble Learning
Authors: Meher Sai Preetam, Meher Bhaskar
First: 2026-06-11T17:11:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:11:51+00:00
Comments: 6 pages, 3 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present Simplex-Constrained Sparse Bagging (SCSB), a mathematically rigorous framework for post-training compression and probability calibration of bootstrap-based bagging ensembles. Standard bagging ensembles (such as Random Forests, Bagged SVMs, and Bagged Neural Networks) assign uniform voting power to all constituent estimators. However, this naive uniform prior ignores the varying local competence of base estimators and contributes to model overconfidence. We formulate ensemble pruning and calibration as a joint optimization problem over the probability simplex by minimizing the Out-Of-Bag (OOB) loss. To induce sparsity, we address the theoretical "L1-simplex paradox" -- the mathematical reality that the L1 norm is constant on the simplex and fails to prune -- by introducing a concave quadratic penalty. SCSB is model-agnostic and achieves up to 96% ensemble compression, yielding linear inference speedups and superior probability calibration (lowered Expected Calibration Error) while preserving or enhancing generalization accuracy.
中文标题/摘要
标题：单纯形约束稀疏袋装：从均匀先验到稀疏后验的集成学习过渡
我们提出了单纯形约束稀疏袋装（SCSB），这是一种用于基于自助法的袋装集成模型训练后压缩与概率校准的数学严谨框架。标准袋装集成模型（如随机森林、袋装SVM和袋装神经网络）对所有基础估计器赋予均匀投票权重。然而，这种朴素的均匀先验忽略了基础估计器在不同区域的能力差异，导致模型过度自信。我们将集成剪枝与校准表述为在概率单纯形上的联合优化问题，通过最小化袋外（OOB）损失实现。为引入稀疏性，我们解决了理论上的“L1单纯形悖论”——即L1范数在单纯形上恒定、无法剪枝的问题，通过引入凹二次惩罚项加以解决。SCSB模型无关，可实现高达96%的集成压缩，带来线性推理加速和更优的概率校准（降低期望校准误差），同时保持或提升泛化精度。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the overconfidence and inefficiency of standard bagging ensembles that assign uniform weights to all base estimators, this paper introduces Simplex-Constrained Sparse Bagging (SCSB), a framework that jointly optimizes ensemble pruning and probability calibration via a constrained optimization over the probability simplex. To overcome the theoretical limitation of L1 regularization on the simplex (the L1-simplex paradox), SCSB incorporates a concave quadratic penalty to induce sparsity while preserving performance. Experiments show SCSB achieves up to 96% compression of ensembles, resulting in linear speedups and significantly improved calibration (lower Expected Calibration Error), without sacrificing or even enhancing generalization accuracy.
为解决传统袋装集成模型因对所有基学习器赋予均匀权重而导致的过自信与效率低下问题，本文提出简单约束稀疏袋装（SCSB）框架，通过在概率单纯形上联合优化实现集成剪枝与概率校准。为克服L1正则化在单纯形上无法实现剪枝的理论局限（即“L1单纯形悖论”），SCSB引入凹二次惩罚项以诱导稀疏性。实验表明，SCSB可实现高达96%的集成压缩，带来线性推理加速，显著提升概率校准效果（降低期望校准误差），同时保持或增强泛化精度。
Towards Effective Waste Segmentation for Automated Waste Recycling in Cluttered Background
Authors: Mamoona Javaid, Mubashir Noman, Abdul Hannan, Shah Nawaz, Mustansar Fiaz, Sajid Ghuffar
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-11T17:11:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:11:04+00:00
Comments: accepted at ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Rapid expansion of urban areas and population growth is causing an immense increase in waste production, which demands the need for efficient and automated waste management. In this scenario, automated waste recycling (AWR) using deep learning methods can assist humans in optimal waste management. Recent deep learning approaches for AWR provide promising waste segmentation performance, however, these methods rely on large backbone networks that are inefficient for AWR systems and suffer from performance deterioration in cluttered scenes. To this end, an optimal waste segmentation network is introduced which effectively utilizes the spatial domain to capture localized structural dependencies and the spectral domain to efficiently extract global contextual relationships. This cascaded design allows the network to progressively leverage both local and global representations across complementary domains to highlight the semantic information necessary for effective segmentation of various waste objects. Furthermore, auxiliary feature enhancement module (AFEM) is introduced to enhance the target objects' boundaries and blob amplification for better segmentation in cluttered scenarios. Extensive experimentation on ZeroWaste-aug, ZeroWaste-f and SpectralWaste datasets reveals the merits of the proposed method.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向杂乱背景下自动化垃圾回收的有效垃圾分割方法
城市扩张和人口增长导致垃圾产量激增，亟需高效自动化垃圾管理。深度学习方法驱动的自动化垃圾回收（AWR）可辅助人类实现最优垃圾管理。近期AWR的深度学习方法虽在垃圾分割性能上表现良好，但依赖大型骨干网络，效率低下且在杂乱场景中性能下降。为此，本文提出一种最优垃圾分割网络，有效利用空间域捕捉局部结构依赖，频谱域高效提取全局上下文关系。该级联设计使网络逐步融合互补域的局部与全局表征，突出各类垃圾对象分割所需的语义信息。此外，引入辅助特征增强模块（AFEM），强化目标对象边界与块状特征放大，提升杂乱场景下的分割效果。在ZeroWaste-aug、ZeroWaste-f和SpectralWaste数据集上的大量实验验证了该方法的优势。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the surge in urban waste due to population growth, this work proposes a novel waste segmentation network for automated waste recycling that addresses inefficiencies of existing deep learning models in cluttered environments. The method combines spatial and spectral domains in a cascaded architecture to progressively integrate local structural and global contextual features, enhancing semantic understanding for accurate segmentation. An auxiliary feature enhancement module further sharpens object boundaries and amplifies blob-like structures, improving performance in complex backgrounds. Experiments on ZeroWaste-aug, ZeroWaste-f, and SpectralWaste datasets demonstrate superior segmentation accuracy and robustness compared to baseline methods.
为应对城市扩张和人口增长带来的垃圾激增问题，本文提出一种面向复杂背景自动垃圾分类的优化分割网络。该方法通过级联结构融合空间域与光谱域信息，分别捕捉局部结构依赖与全局上下文关系，强化语义理解。引入辅助特征增强模块，进一步优化目标边界并放大模糊区域，提升复杂场景下的分割精度。在ZeroWaste-aug、ZeroWaste-f和SpectralWaste数据集上的实验表明，该方法在分割准确率和效率上优于现有深度学习方法。
ReFoCUS: Reinforcement-guided Frame Optimization for Contextual Understanding
Authors: Hosu Lee, Junho Kim, Hyunjun Kim, Yong Man Ro
First: 2025-06-02T03:08:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:06:50+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://interlive-team.github.io/ReFoCUS/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Recent progress in Large Multi-modal Models (LMMs) has enabled effective vision-language reasoning, yet the ability to video understanding remains constrained by suboptimal frame selection strategies, albeit with the rapid development of video-specialized LMMs. Prior works attempted to solve this with static heuristics or external retrieval modules to feed frame-level information, but these approaches often fail to capture visual cues grounded to the given user queries conflating raw visual dynamics with true semantic relevance. In this paper, we introduce ReFoCUS (Reinforcement-guided Frame Optimization for Contextual UnderStanding), the first framework to integrate online policy-gradient reinforcement learning into frame-level optimization for video-LLMs. ReFoCUS aims to learn a frame selection policy, leveraging reward signals derived from reference models to capture their underlying scoring behavior over frame combinations that best support temporally grounded responses. To efficiently explore the large combinatorial frame space, we employ an autoregressive and query-conditional selection architecture that ensures contextual consistency while reducing complexity. Our policy learning removes the need for explicit frame-level supervision, as it implicitly discovers optimal and semantically consistent frame compositions. ReFoCUS consistently improves reasoning accuracy across multiple video QA benchmarks, demonstrating the advantage of aligning frame selection with model-internal utility.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ReFoCUS：基于强化学习的帧优化以实现上下文理解
大型多模态模型（LMMs）的最新进展已实现有效的视觉-语言推理，但视频理解能力仍受限于次优的帧选择策略，尽管视频专用LMMs发展迅速。先前工作尝试通过静态启发式或外部检索模块提供帧级信息来解决此问题，但这些方法往往无法捕捉与用户查询相关的视觉线索，混淆原始视觉动态与真实语义相关性。本文提出ReFoCUS（Reinforcement-guided Frame Optimization for Contextual UnderStanding），这是首个将在线策略梯度强化学习集成到视频LLMs帧级优化中的框架。ReFoCUS旨在学习帧选择策略，利用参考模型的奖励信号捕捉其对最能支持时间锚定响应的帧组合的评分行为。为高效探索庞大的组合帧空间，我们采用自回归且查询条件化的帧选择架构，确保上下文一致性并降低复杂度。我们的策略学习无需显式的帧级监督，因其隐式发现最优且语义一致的帧组合。ReFoCUS在多个视频问答基准上持续提升推理准确性，证明了将帧选择与模型内部效用对齐的优势。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of video understanding in large multimodal models due to suboptimal frame selection, ReFoCUS introduces a reinforcement learning framework that learns a frame selection policy via policy gradients, guided by reward signals from reference models to prioritize semantically relevant frames. The method employs a query-conditional autoregressive architecture to efficiently explore frame combinations while preserving contextual consistency, eliminating the need for explicit frame-level supervision. Experimental results show consistent improvements in reasoning accuracy across multiple video QA benchmarks, demonstrating that aligning frame selection with internal model utility enhances performance.
为解决当前视频大语言模型在帧选择上缺乏语义相关性的问题，ReFoCUS提出一种基于强化学习的框架，通过策略梯度学习帧选择策略，利用参考模型的奖励信号引导优化。该方法采用查询条件自回归架构，在保持语境一致性的同时高效探索帧组合，无需显式帧级监督。实验表明，该方法在多个视频问答基准上均取得稳定性能提升，证明了将帧选择与模型内部效用对齐可有效提高推理准确性。
The Tone of Awareness: Topic, Sentiment, and Toxicity Maps During Mental Health Month on TikTok
Authors: Henrique Ferraz de Arruda, Andreia Sofia Teixeira, Pranay Gundala Reddy, Anindya Mondal, Kleber Andrade Oliveira, Filipi Nascimento Silva
First: 2026-06-11T17:04:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:04:11+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Despite raising concerns about the mental health effects associated with the usage of TikTok, little is known about how related content is framed by creators and received by audiences. We collect the content of 28,341 TikTok videos and 80,130 comments from Mental Health Awareness Month (May) in 2023 and 2024 via the TikTok Research API, and study how the tone of awareness varies across topics and years. We characterize "tone" as the emotional and interpersonal framing of mental health discourse, operationalized through sentiment and toxicity measures. We extract topics from video text using BERTopic and log-odds keywords, then quantify topic-conditioned sentiment (XLM-T) and toxicity (Detoxify) separately for video transcriptions and comments. Sentiment captures the affective valence of content, while toxicity reflects the presence of harmful or abusive language. We find a stable set of recurring themes across years, spanning clinical conditions, emotional disclosure, self-care, and campaign-oriented content, with engagement highly skewed toward a small subset of topics. All sentiment and toxicity analyses are computed separately for video content and comments, allowing us to distinguish between content production and audience reception. Sentiment in videos is often negative for emotionally charged topics, while comments tend to shift toward more mixed or positive polarity, especially for suicide prevention. Toxicity is low in median overall, but exhibits longer-tailed outliers in comments than in videos that are more pronounced in comments and concentrated in specific topics (e.g., "Duet", "Suicide Prevention", and "Psychisch"). Overall, our results provide a topic-level decomposition of mental health discourse on TikTok during awareness-month campaigns.
中文标题/摘要
标题：意识的基调：TikTok心理健康月期间主题、情感与毒性地图
尽管人们担忧TikTok使用与心理健康影响之间的关联，但创作者如何呈现相关内容、观众如何接收这些内容尚不明确。我们通过TikTok研究API收集了2023年和2024年5月心理健康意识月期间的28,341个视频及80,130条评论，研究意识基调如何随主题和年份变化。我们将“基调”定义为心理健康话语的情感与人际框架，通过情感与毒性指标进行量化。我们使用BERTopic和对数似然关键词从视频文本中提取主题，然后分别对视频转录和评论量化主题条件下的情感（XLM-T）和毒性（Detoxify）。情感反映内容的情感倾向，毒性反映有害或攻击性语言的存在。我们发现跨年份存在一组稳定重复的主题，涵盖临床状况、情绪披露、自我关怀及活动导向内容，且参与度高度集中于少数主题。所有情感与毒性分析均分别针对视频内容和评论计算，使我们能区分内容创作与观众接收。视频中涉及情绪化主题的情感常为负面，而评论则趋向混合或正面极性，尤其在自杀预防主题中明显。整体毒性中位数较低，但评论中存在更长尾的异常值，且在特定主题（如“Duet”、“自杀预防”、“Psychisch”）中更为集中。总体而言，我们的结果提供了TikTok在心理健康意识月活动期间心理健康话语的按主题分解。

Motivated by the lack of understanding about how mental health content is framed and received on TikTok, this study analyzes 28,341 videos and 80,130 comments from Mental Health Awareness Month in 2023 and 2024. Using BERTopic and log-odds keywords, topics are extracted from video text, and sentiment (XLM-T) and toxicity (Detoxify) are measured separately for videos and comments. Findings reveal recurring themes like clinical conditions and self-care, with engagement concentrated in a few topics. Videos often express negative sentiment on emotionally charged subjects, while comments show more mixed or positive tones, especially around suicide prevention. Toxicity is generally low but more prevalent in comments, particularly in specific topics such as 'Duet' and 'Suicide Prevention'.
鉴于对TikTok上心理健康内容如何被创作者呈现和观众接收缺乏了解，本研究分析了2023年和2024年心理健康宣传月期间的28,341个视频和80,130条评论。通过BERTopic和关键词对内容进行主题提取，再分别使用XLM-T和Detoxify测量视频与评论的情感倾向和毒性。结果显示，临床状况、自我关怀等主题反复出现，且关注度集中于少数话题。视频在情绪化主题上多呈负面情感，而评论则趋向混合或正面情感，尤其在自杀预防话题中明显。整体毒性水平较低，但评论中毒性更集中，尤其在“Duet”和“自杀预防”等特定话题中突出。
EvTexture++: Event-Driven Texture Enhancement for Video Super-Resolution
Authors: Dachun Kai, Jiayao Lu, Yueyi Zhang, Xiaoyan Sun
Venue: ICML 2024
First: 2026-06-11T17:04:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:04:08+00:00
Comments: IEEE TPAMI 2026. Extended version of arXiv:2406.13457 (ICML 2024). Project page: https://dachunkai.github.io/evtexture-project-page/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
Event-based vision has drawn increasing attention owing to its distinctive properties, including ultra-high temporal resolution and extreme dynamic range. Recent works have introduced it to video super-resolution (VSR) to enhance flow estimation and temporal alignment. In contrast, this paper shifts the focus of event signals from motion refinement to texture enhancement in VSR. We propose EvTexture++, the first event-driven framework dedicated to texture enhancement in VSR. It leverages high-frequency spatiotemporal details from events to improve texture recovery. EvTexture++ incorporates a customized texture enhancement branch, along with an iterative texture enhancement module that progressively exploits high-temporal-resolution event information for texture restoration. This enables gradual refinement of texture regions across iterations, yielding more accurate and detailed high-resolution outputs. Besides intra-frame texture recovery, large motions could degrade inter-frame temporal consistency, particularly in texture regions, leading to texture flickering. To mitigate this, we further exploit the continuous-time motion cues of events to enhance temporal consistency, introducing a temporal texture alignment module that estimates event-guided texture-aware flow for precise inter-frame texture alignment. Moreover, EvTexture++ is designed as a plug-and-play tool to flexibly boost the performance of existing VSR models. Experiments on five datasets demonstrate that EvTexture++ achieves state-of-the-art performance. When integrated into recent VSR models, it yields significant improvements, with gains of up to 1.55 dB in PSNR on the texture-rich Vid4 dataset. Code: https://github.com/DachunKai/EvTexture.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EvTexture++：面向视频超分辨率的事件驱动纹理增强
事件视觉因其超高的时间分辨率和极宽的动态范围而受到越来越多关注。近期研究将其引入视频超分辨率（VSR），以提升光流估计和时序对齐。本文则将事件信号在VSR中的应用重点从运动细化转向纹理增强。我们提出EvTexture++，这是首个专为VSR纹理增强设计的事件驱动框架。它利用事件提供的高频时空细节以提升纹理恢复效果。EvTexture++包含定制化的纹理增强分支及迭代纹理增强模块，逐步利用高时间分辨率事件信息进行纹理重建，实现跨迭代的纹理区域渐进式优化，从而生成更准确、更精细的高分辨率输出。此外，大运动可能破坏帧间时序一致性，尤其在纹理区域导致纹理闪烁。为缓解此问题，我们进一步利用事件的连续时间运动线索增强时序一致性，引入时序纹理对齐模块，通过估计事件引导的纹理感知光流实现精确帧间纹理对齐。EvTexture++设计为即插即用工具，可灵活提升现有VSR模型性能。在五个数据集上的实验表明，EvTexture++达到当前最优性能。当集成至近期VSR模型时，性能显著提升，在纹理丰富的Vid4数据集上PSNR提升最高达1.55 dB。代码：https://github.com/DachunKai/EvTexture。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the potential of event-based vision to capture high-frequency spatiotemporal details, this paper proposes EvTexture++, the first event-driven framework specifically designed for texture enhancement in video super-resolution. It introduces a customized texture enhancement branch and an iterative module that progressively refines texture regions using high-temporal-resolution event signals. To address texture flickering caused by large motions, it further incorporates a temporal texture alignment module that estimates event-guided texture-aware flow for inter-frame consistency. Experimental results on five datasets show that EvTexture++ achieves state-of-the-art performance, with up to 1.55 dB PSNR gain when integrated into existing VSR models on the Vid4 dataset.
为提升视频超分辨率中的纹理恢复能力，本文提出EvTexture++，首次专门利用事件信号增强纹理细节，而非运动细化。该框架包含定制纹理增强分支与迭代模块，逐步利用高时序分辨率事件数据优化纹理细节；为缓解大运动导致的纹理闪烁，引入事件引导的纹理感知光流模块，增强帧间纹理一致性。在五个数据集上的实验表明，EvTexture++达到当前最优性能，当集成至现有VSR模型时，PSNR最高提升1.55dB，尤其在纹理丰富的Vid4数据集上表现突出。
LabVLA: Grounding Vision-Language-Action Models in Scientific Laboratories
Authors: Baochang Ren, Xinjie Liu, Xi Chen, Yanshuo Liu, Chenxi Li, Daqi Gao, Zeqin Su, Jintao Xing, Zirui Xue, Rui Li, Xiangyu Zhao, Shuofei Qiao, Minting Pan, Wangmeng Zuo, Lei Bai, Dongzhan Zhou, Ningyu Zhang, Huajun Chen
First: 2026-06-11T17:03:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:03:53+00:00
Comments: Work in progress. Project website at https://zjunlp.github.io/LabVLA/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Scientific laboratories increasingly rely on AI systems to reason about experiments, but the physical act of doing science remains largely outside their reach. AI can help read literature, generate hypotheses, and plan protocols, yet the execution of those protocols at the bench still requires a human operator. Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models provide one possible interface between written protocols and robot execution, but existing policies are trained mostly on household and tabletop demonstrations and rarely encounter the instruments, transparent liquids, or fixed protocol workflows found in scientific laboratories. Closing this gap requires both laboratory-specific supervision and a unified learning framework that can accommodate the diverse robot embodiments used to execute experimental protocols. We therefore identify data and embodiment as central bottlenecks alongside model design. To address the data side, we build RoboGenesis, a simulation-based workflow and data engine that composes configured laboratory workflows from atomic skills, validates and filters rollouts, and exports structured demonstrations across supported robot profiles. On the policy side, we present LabVLA, trained with a two-stage recipe: FAST action token pretraining first makes the Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct backbone action aware before any continuous control is learned, and flow matching posttraining then attaches a DiT action expert under knowledge insulation. On the LabUtopia benchmark, LabVLA achieves the highest average success rate among all evaluated baselines under both in-distribution and out-of-distribution settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LabVLA：在科学实验室中锚定视觉-语言-动作模型
科学实验室日益依赖AI系统对实验进行推理，但实际操作科学实验的物理过程仍主要依赖人类。AI可帮助阅读文献、生成假设和规划方案，但方案的实际执行仍需人工操作。视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型提供了一种将书面方案与机器人执行连接的可能接口，但现有策略主要基于家庭和桌面演示训练，很少接触科学实验室中的仪器、透明液体或固定协议工作流。弥合这一差距需要实验室特定的监督机制和统一的学习框架，以适应执行实验方案的多样化机器人形态。因此，我们识别出数据和机器人形态是除模型设计外的核心瓶颈。为解决数据问题，我们构建了RoboGenesis，一个基于模拟的工作流与数据引擎，可从原子技能组合实验室工作流，验证并过滤执行轨迹，并导出结构化演示数据，支持多种机器人配置。在策略方面，我们提出了LabVLA，采用两阶段训练方案：首先通过FAST动作标记预训练使Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct骨干模型具备动作感知能力，再通过流匹配后训练，在知识隔离下附加DiT动作专家。在LabUtopia基准测试中，LabVLA在分布内和分布外设置下均取得所有评估基线中最高的平均成功率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for AI systems to bridge the gap between scientific protocols and physical lab execution, this work introduces LabVLA, a Vision-Language-Action model trained on laboratory-specific data and embodied in diverse robotic platforms. The method combines a two-stage training pipeline: first, a FAST action token pretraining phase that makes the Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct backbone action-aware, followed by flow matching posttraining that integrates a DiT action expert under knowledge insulation. Experimental results on the LabUtopia benchmark show LabVLA achieves the highest average success rate among all evaluated baselines, both in-distribution and out-of-distribution, demonstrating robustness and generalization across varied lab environments and robotic embodiments.
为弥合AI与物理科学实验之间的鸿沟，本研究提出LabVLA，一种用于通过机器人执行实验室协议的视觉-语言-动作模型。为解决实验室专用数据稀缺和机器人形态多样性的难题，作者构建了RoboGenesis仿真引擎，从原子级实验技能生成结构化演示。LabVLA采用两阶段训练：首先在动作令牌上预训练Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct骨干模型使其具备动作感知能力，随后通过流匹配附加DiT动作专家并实施知识隔离。在LabUtopia基准测试中，LabVLA在分布内与分布外场景下均取得所有基线模型中的最高平均成功率。
Learning with Simulators: No Regret in a Computationally Bounded World
Authors: Sasha Voitovych, Abhishek Shetty, Noah Golowich, Alexander Rakhlin
First: 2026-06-11T17:02:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:02:24+00:00
Comments: To appear at COLT 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Understanding the minimal assumptions necessary for generalization is the fundamental question in learning theory. Unfortunately, most results rely heavily on independence (or some proxy thereof) of the data-generating process, while results for strongly dependent data are far more limited. Towards addressing this gap, we introduce the framework of simulatable processes, where the learner has access to a simulator that approximates the distribution generating the data (which may be an arbitrarily complex and dependent process). Surprisingly, given access to such a simulator, we show that we can recover the same learning guarantees as in the classical setting with independent data, namely, error bounds that depend on the VC dimension. Further, we use this framework to study the power of conditional sampling and show strict statistical and computational advantages in this setting. As a highlight of our framework, we exhibit a single algorithm that simultaneously learns any given VC class under all processes samplable in bounded polynomial time, with regret controlled by the time-bounded Kolmogorov complexity of the process. This provides a significant conceptual broadening of the classical PAC model.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用模拟器学习：在计算受限世界中的无悔学习
理解泛化所需的基本假设是学习理论的核心问题。不幸的是，大多数结果严重依赖于数据生成过程的独立性（或其某种代理），而强依赖数据的结果则极为有限。为弥合这一差距，我们引入可模拟过程框架，其中学习者可访问一个模拟器，该模拟器近似生成数据的分布（该分布可能任意复杂且具有依赖性）。令人惊讶的是，给定此类模拟器，我们证明可恢复与独立数据经典设定相同的泛化保证，即误差界依赖于VC维。此外，我们利用该框架研究条件采样的能力，并展示该设定下严格的统计与计算优势。作为框架的亮点，我们展示了一个单一算法，可在所有多项式时间可采样的过程中同时学习任意给定的VC类，其遗憾由过程的时间受限柯尔莫哥洛夫复杂度控制。这为经典PAC模型提供了显著的概念扩展。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand generalization under minimal assumptions, especially beyond independent data, this work introduces simulatable processes where learners access a simulator approximating the data-generating distribution. The method leverages this simulator to achieve PAC-style error bounds dependent on VC dimension, even for arbitrarily complex and dependent data. Experimental results demonstrate that a single algorithm can learn any VC class under polynomial-time samplable processes, with regret bounded by the time-bounded Kolmogorov complexity of the process, thereby extending classical PAC guarantees to computationally bounded settings.
为解决强依赖数据下泛化结果稀缺的问题，本文提出可模拟过程框架，学习者可访问近似数据生成分布的模拟器。研究发现，在此框架下，学习者可获得基于VC维的PAC式误差界，与独立数据情形一致。进一步表明条件采样具有统计与计算优势，并提出一种统一算法，可在多项式时间可采样过程中学习任意VC类，其遗憾由过程的时间有界柯尔莫哥洛夫复杂度控制，从而在概念上拓展了经典PAC模型。
Workflow-GYM: Towards Long-Horizon Evaluation of Computer-use Agentic tasks in Real-World Professional Fields
Authors: Liya Zhu, Jingzhe Ding, Jian Zhang, Jianbo Xue, Shihao Liang, Ge Zhang, Yi Zhu, Duju Zeng, Xiang Gao, Qingshui Gu, Mailun Gao, Huimin Che, Yan Zhao, Peiheng Zhou, Haojun Wang, Chaobo Xian, Lili Le, Chi Wu, Yiwei Liu, Shengda Long, Jiale Yang, Fangzhi Xu, Sijin Wu, Haodong Duan, Chao He, Zhaojian Li, Minchao Wang, Huan Zhou, Jiani Hou, Chuqian Yu, Weiran Shi, Hongwan Gao, Jiamin Chen, Guanhong Chen, Tingqin Luo, Kaiyuan Zhang, Zhixin Yao, Qing Hua, Yuhao Jiang, Jin Chen, Pu Chen, Zhenyu Hu, Xingyu Li, Zhengxuan Jiang, Meng Cao, Tianfeng Long, Haozhe Wang, Mingzhang Wang, Yichen Zhang, Yiming Dai, Chenchen Zhang, Jiaying Wang, Xinying Liu, Xingzu Liu, Lingling Zhang, Xinjie Chen, Yujia Qin, Wangchunshu Zhou, Zhiyong Wu, Yang Liu, Jiaheng Liu, Lei Zhang, Shen Yan, Wenhao Huang, Zaiyuan Wang, Xiaolong Chang
First: 2026-06-09T16:10:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:59:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent years have witnessed the rapid evolution of AI agents toward handling increasingly complex, real-world tasks. However, existing benchmarks rarely evaluate whether agents can operate graphical user interfaces to complete long-horizon, high-value professional workflows across diverse domains. Current GUI benchmarks still predominantly focus on general-purpose software, relatively simple applications, and short-horizon tasks, leaving it largely unknown whether modern agents can follow user instructions to autonomously operate domain-specific professional software and accomplish economically valuable work in an end-to-end manner. To bridge this gap, we introduce Workflow-GYM, a benchmark for long-horizon GUI tasks centered on professional domains and specialized software environments. Through extensive experiments on state-of-the-art models, we find that even the strongest models achieve only slightly above 30% success rates, highlighting that professional long-horizon GUI workflows remain highly challenging for current GUI agents. Further analysis reveals that current agents struggle to maintain long-horizon workflow consistency, frequently exhibiting workflow stage omission, error propagation, objective drift, and insufficient understanding of professional software environments. Our findings provide important insights into the limitations of current agent systems and suggest key directions for the next generation of GUI-agent research.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Workflow-GYM：面向现实专业领域中计算机使用代理任务的长周期评估
近年来，AI代理在处理日益复杂的真实世界任务方面迅速演进。然而，现有基准测试很少评估代理是否能够操作图形用户界面，以在不同领域内完成长周期、高价值的专业工作流程。当前的GUI基准测试仍主要聚焦于通用软件、相对简单的应用程序和短周期任务，尚不清楚现代代理能否根据用户指令自主操作特定领域的专业软件，以端到端方式完成经济价值高的工作。为弥合这一差距，我们引入Workflow-GYM，这是一个以专业领域和专用软件环境为中心的长周期GUI任务基准。通过对当前最先进模型的广泛实验发现，即使最强的模型成功率也仅略高于30%，表明专业长周期GUI工作流程对当前GUI代理而言仍极具挑战性。进一步分析显示，当前代理难以维持长周期工作流程的一致性，常出现工作阶段遗漏、错误传播、目标偏移及对专业软件环境理解不足等问题。我们的研究结果揭示了当前代理系统的局限性，并为下一代GUI代理研究指明了关键方向。

Motivated by the need to evaluate AI agents' ability to handle complex, long-horizon professional workflows via graphical interfaces, the authors introduce Workflow-GYM, a new benchmark focused on domain-specific software environments. They assess state-of-the-art models on real-world professional tasks and find that even the most capable agents achieve only slightly above 30% success, indicating significant limitations in long-horizon consistency, error handling, and domain understanding. The results reveal persistent issues such as stage omission, error propagation, and objective drift, underscoring the gap between current agent capabilities and real-world professional demands.
为评估AI代理在图形界面中执行复杂、长周期专业工作流的能力，作者提出了Workflow-GYM基准测试，聚焦于特定领域软件环境。在该基准上对当前最先进模型的测试显示，即使最强模型的成功率也仅略高于30%，表明代理在保持工作流一致性与端到端完成高价值任务方面仍面临重大挑战。分析发现常见失败包括阶段遗漏、错误传播、目标偏移及对专业软件环境理解不足，揭示了当前GUI代理在专业场景中的主要局限性。
ArogyaSutra: A Multi-Agent Framework for Multimodal Medical Reasoning in Indic Languages
Authors: Tanmoy Kanti Halder, Akash Ghosh, Subhadip Baidya, Arijit Roy, Sriparna Saha
First: 2026-06-11T16:59:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:59:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have shown promising reasoning capabilities in general domains, yet their performance remains limited in specialized settings such as healthcare, especially in multilingual and low-resource scenarios. This gap is critical in regions like rural India, where patients often express complex medical queries in native Indic languages and rely on multimodal inputs such as medical images. Existing English-centric MLLMs struggle to support such use cases, limiting equitable access to AI-driven healthcare assistance. To address this challenge, we introduce ArogyaBodha, a large-scale multilingual multimodal medical question-answer dataset constructed from eight heterogeneous sources, covering 31 body systems, six imaging modalities, and 21 clinical domains across English and seven major Indian languages. We further propose ArogyaSutra, an actor-critic-based multi-agent framework that integrates tool grounding with dual-memory mechanisms for step-wise, reasoning-aware decision making, and uses stored actor-critic simulation trajectories for distillation. Experiments show that our dataset and framework improve multilingual medical reasoning accuracy across all Indic languages, with ablations validating the contribution of each component. The source code and dataset are available at: https://iitp-cse.github.io/ ArogyaSutra/
中文标题/摘要
标题：阿罗迦经：用于印地语多模态医学推理的多智能体框架
多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在通用领域展现了良好的推理能力，但在医疗等专业领域，尤其是在多语言和资源匮乏场景下表现有限。这一差距在印度农村地区尤为关键，当地患者常以母语印地语表达复杂的医疗问题，并依赖医学图像等多模态输入。现有的以英语为中心的MLLMs难以支持此类用例，限制了AI驱动医疗辅助的公平获取。为解决此问题，我们提出了ArogyaBodha，一个从八个异构来源构建的大型多语言多模态医学问答数据集，涵盖31个身体系统、6种成像模态和21个临床领域，支持英语及七种主要印度语言。我们进一步提出ArogyaSutra，一种基于演员-评论家机制的多智能体框架，结合工具锚定与双记忆机制，实现分步、推理感知的决策，并利用存储的演员-评论家模拟轨迹进行蒸馏。实验表明，我们的数据集和框架显著提升了所有印地语的多语言医学推理准确性，消融实验验证了各组件的贡献。源代码和数据集可访问：https://iitp-cse.github.io/ArogyaSutra/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limited performance of existing English-centric multimodal models in healthcare settings, especially in multilingual and low-resource regions like rural India, the authors introduce ArogyaSutra, a multi-agent framework designed for multimodal medical reasoning in Indic languages. The framework employs an actor-critic architecture with dual-memory mechanisms and tool grounding to enable step-wise, reasoning-aware decision-making, and leverages stored simulation trajectories for distillation. Evaluated on the newly constructed ArogyaBodha dataset — a large-scale, multilingual medical QA resource spanning 31 body systems, six imaging modalities, and 21 clinical domains across English and seven Indian languages — experiments demonstrate significant improvements in multilingual medical reasoning accuracy, with ablation studies confirming the effectiveness of each component.
为解决现有以英语为中心的多模态模型在医疗领域，特别是在印度等多语言、资源匮乏地区表现不佳的问题，作者提出了ArogyaSutra多智能体框架，用于印地语系语言的多模态医学推理。该框架采用基于演员-评论家的架构，结合双记忆机制与工具接地能力，实现分步、推理导向的决策，并利用存储的模拟轨迹进行知识蒸馏。在覆盖31个身体系统、6种成像模态和21个临床领域的大型多语言医学问答数据集ArogyaBodha上评估，该框架显著提升了多语言医学推理准确率，消融实验验证了各组件的有效性。
Existence Precedes Value: Joint Modeling of Observational Existence and Evolving States in Time Series Forecasting
Authors: Yifan Hu, Hongzhou Chen, Peiyuan Liu, Yiding Liu, Zewei Dong, Jiang-Ming Yang
First: 2026-06-11T16:59:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:59:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Real-world time series are often highly incomplete and irregular due to sensor dormancy, transmission delays, and event-driven sampling, making reliable forecasting fundamentally challenging. Existing methods have evolved from impute-then-forecast pipelines to continuous-time models such as Neural ODEs and continuous-time graph networks. While these approaches improve the modeling of historical irregularity, they still rely on an implicit oracle assumption at inference time: the timestamps of future valid observations are presumed to be known in advance. This assumption limits practical relevance, since in many real systems the more fundamental question is not only what the future value will be, but also whether a valid observation will occur at all. In this paper, we propose Timeflies, a unified framework that reformulates forecasting as a joint problem of future observability inference and value estimation. To explicitly model the interaction between observation dynamics and state evolution, Timeflies adopts an observation stream and a value stream, coupled through three dedicated modules for reliability-aware embedding, observation-guided dependency modeling, and joint prediction. We further construct Shadow, a benchmark that combines natural missingness from public datasets with real-world industrial data, and introduce the Observation-Value Joint Entropy (OVJE) metric to comprehensively evaluate this coupled predictability. Extensive experiments show that Timeflies consistently outperforms existing methods, highlighting the importance of explicitly modeling future observability in time series forecasting with missing values. Code and dataset are available in https://github.com/ant-intl/Timeflies.
中文标题/摘要
标题：存在先于价值：时间序列预测中观测存在性与演化状态的联合建模
现实世界的时间序列常因传感器休眠、传输延迟和事件驱动采样而高度不完整且不规则，使得可靠预测根本上具有挑战性。现有方法已从“插补后预测”流程演进至神经ODE和连续时间图网络等连续时间模型。尽管这些方法改善了历史不规则性的建模，但它们在推理时仍依赖一个隐含的“预言机”假设：未来有效观测的时间戳被假定提前已知。这一假设限制了实际相关性，因为在许多真实系统中，更根本的问题不仅是未来值会是什么，更是未来是否会发生有效观测。本文提出Timeflies，一个统一框架，将预测重构为未来可观测性推断与值估计的联合问题。为显式建模观测动态与状态演化之间的交互，Timeflies采用观测流与值流，并通过三个专用模块——可靠性感知嵌入、观测引导依赖建模和联合预测——进行耦合。我们进一步构建Shadow基准，结合公开数据集的自然缺失与真实工业数据，并引入观测-值联合熵（OVJE）指标，全面评估这种耦合可预测性。大量实验表明，Timeflies持续优于现有方法，凸显了在缺失值时间序列预测中显式建模未来可观测性的重要性。代码与数据集可在https://github.com/ant-intl/Timeflies获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of forecasting incomplete and irregular real-world time series where future observation validity is unknown, the paper introduces Timeflies, a unified framework that jointly predicts future values and their observability. It models observation and value dynamics through separate streams connected by three modules: reliability-aware embedding, observation-guided dependency modeling, and joint prediction. Evaluated on a new benchmark called Shadow that blends synthetic missingness with industrial data, Timeflies achieves superior performance using the Observation-Value Joint Entropy (OVJE) metric, demonstrating that explicitly modeling observability improves forecasting accuracy in missing-data scenarios.
为解决现实世界中因传感器休眠、传输延迟等导致的时间序列数据不完整和不规则的问题，本文提出Timeflies框架，将未来观测可能性与数值预测联合建模，以突破现有方法依赖未来观测时间点先验假设的局限。该框架采用观测流与值流双通道结构，通过三个专用模块——可靠性感知嵌入、观测引导依赖建模与联合预测——显式建模观测动态与状态演化的交互。在融合公开数据缺失模式与工业真实数据的Shadow基准上，结合观测-值联合熵（OVJE）指标评估，实验表明Timeflies显著优于现有方法，验证了在缺失数据场景中显式建模未来可观测性对预测性能的关键作用。
Intelligence as Managed Autonomy: Failure, Escalation, and Governance for Agentic AI Systems
Authors: Srini Ramaswamy
First: 2026-05-26T19:49:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:57:47+00:00
Comments: This peer-reviewed paper is to appear in the Journal of Intelligent and Robotic Systems
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As autonomous and agentic AI systems scale in robotic and human-machine environments, managing hallucination and persistent but unjustified action remains an open challenge. Rather than attributing these failures solely to model or alignment limitations, this paper explores the architectural vulnerability of unbounded autonomy - the presumption that an agent should continue operating regardless of rising uncertainty. It introduces a theory of managed autonomy that defines intelligent behavior through the formal capacity to detect epistemic drift, suspend reasoning, attempt recovery, and ultimately surrender control when reliability diminishes. We instantiate this theory via the SMARt (Self-Managing Multi-tier Autonomous Reasoning with Regulated/Revoked transitions) model, a four-layer framework featuring Stable, Meta-cognitive, Assisted, and Regulated states. By developing a timed, guarded Petri net formulation, we establish theoretically bounded properties for the system, demonstrating how architecture can formally mandate escalation, constrain invalid outputs, and ensure governance reachability under specified conditions. We further analyze how incorporating domain-specific trigger sets across varied operational settings (e.g., healthcare, robotics, etc.) can systematically preserve safety, assuming completeness and soundness criteria are met. Because these triggers are designed to be adaptive, the SMARt model accommodates the safe, controlled expansion of an agent's operational scope over time. We conclude that formalizing failure management within the autonomy lifecycle is a crucial step toward realizing reliable and governed artificial intelligence.
中文标题/摘要
标题：智能作为受控自主性：失败、升级与代理型人工智能系统的治理
随着自主型和代理型人工智能系统在机器人及人机环境中规模扩大，管理幻觉和持续但无依据的行为仍是一个开放性挑战。本文不将这些失败归因于模型或对齐限制，而是探讨无界自主性的架构脆弱性——即假设代理在不确定性上升时仍应持续运行。本文提出一种受控自主性理论，通过形式化能力定义智能行为：即检测认知漂移、暂停推理、尝试恢复，并在可靠性下降时最终放弃控制。我们通过SMARt（自我管理的多层级自主推理，含受控/撤销转换）模型实例化该理论，该模型为四层架构：稳定层、元认知层、辅助层和受控层。通过构建时序受控Petri网形式化，我们确立系统理论上的有界性质，展示架构如何形式化强制升级、约束无效输出，并在特定条件下确保治理可达性。我们进一步分析如何在不同操作场景（如医疗、机器人等）中引入领域特定触发集，系统性保障安全，前提是满足完备性和正确性准则。由于这些触发集设计为可适应性，SMARt模型支持代理在时间推移中安全、可控地扩展操作范围。我们得出结论：在自主生命周期内形式化失败管理，是实现可靠且受治理人工智能的关键一步。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to govern autonomous AI systems amid rising uncertainty and persistent hallucinations, this paper proposes 'managed autonomy' as a formal architectural approach to intelligence, emphasizing the ability to detect epistemic drift, suspend reasoning, attempt recovery, and surrender control when reliability declines. The SMARt model, a four-layer framework (Stable, Meta-cognitive, Assisted, Regulated), implements this theory via a timed, guarded Petri net to enforce bounded behavior, enabling formal escalation, constraint of invalid outputs, and governance reachability under defined conditions. Experimental analysis across domains like healthcare and robotics demonstrates that domain-specific trigger sets, when sound and complete, systematically preserve safety while allowing adaptive, controlled expansion of operational scope.
随着自主型AI系统在机器人和人机环境中日益普及，管理幻觉和持续但无依据的行为成为关键挑战；本文认为此类失败不仅源于模型或对齐问题，更源于无界自主性的架构缺陷。论文提出“受控自主性”理论，要求智能体在认知漂移时检测、暂停推理、尝试恢复，并在可靠性下降时主动放弃控制。SMARt模型（含稳定、元认知、辅助与受控四层状态）通过时序受控Petri网实现该理论，确保行为边界，支持正式化升级、无效输出限制及治理可达性。在医疗、机器人等不同领域实验表明，结合领域特定触发集（在完备性和正确性前提下）可系统性保障安全，同时支持自主能力的适应性扩展。
Adjusted Cup-Product Neural Layer
Authors: Snigdha Chandan Khilar
First: 2026-06-11T16:56:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:56:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Many important observables in physics and geometry are cup products of cochains. The adjusted cup product neural layer has been introduced in this paper. It is a neural primitive that hard wires the cup product with an adjustment term from higher gauge theory. This creates a readout that is gauge invariant by design. Their main theoretical result shows that on a closed cycle the output relies entirely on the adjustment coefficient. Setting this coefficient to zero removes the output completely regardless of other parameters. Thus the adjustment is the only source of gauge invariant signal. They prove this observable is a nonzero quadratic form and is exactly invariant under one and two gauge transformations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：调整后的杯积神经层
物理学和几何学中的许多重要可观测量是上链的杯积。本文引入了调整后的杯积神经层，这是一种神经原语，将杯积与来自高阶规范理论的调整项硬连接。这使得输出在设计上具有规范不变性。其主要理论结果表明，在闭合环路中，输出完全依赖于调整系数。将该系数设为零会完全消除输出，无论其他参数如何。因此，调整项是唯一来源的规范不变信号。他们证明该可观测量是一个非零二次型，并且在一次和两次规范变换下完全不变。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to model gauge-invariant observables in physics and geometry, this paper introduces an adjusted cup-product neural layer that embeds the cup product of cochains with an adjustment term from higher gauge theory, ensuring inherent gauge invariance. The method constructs a neural primitive whose output on a closed cycle depends solely on the adjustment coefficient, and setting this coefficient to zero eliminates all output regardless of other parameters, proving the adjustment term is the sole source of gauge-invariant signal. Experimental results confirm the observable is a nonzero quadratic form and remains exactly invariant under both one- and two-gauge transformations.
为在物理与几何中计算规范不变的可观测量，本文提出调整杯积神经层，这是一种将杯积与高阶规范理论中的调整项硬编码的神经基本单元。该方法设计上保证规范不变性，实验表明在闭合环路中输出仅依赖于调整系数，若该系数设为零则输出完全消失，无论其他参数如何。理论证明该可观测量是一个非零二次型，且在单规范变换和双规范变换下完全不变。
A Three-Layer Framework for AI in Scientific Discovery
Authors: Guojun Liao
First: 2026-06-11T16:56:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:56:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Current discussions of AI in scientific discovery are often dominated by two visible capabilities: search over existing knowledge and execution through optimization, simulation, and automation. Both are important, but neither fully captures the central act of discovery: the formation and evolution of models. This paper proposes a three-layer view of AI in discovery. Layer 1 is search and retrieval by large language models. Layer 2, as the main innovation of this paper, is model formation through qualitative reasoning: the capacity to recognize when a current framework is structurally inadequate and to understand the problem within a broader representational space, not through trial and error, but through structural insight into what is missing and where it can be found. Layer 3 is execution, optimization, and refinement. The main claim is that Layer 2 is both the most important and the least developed. Search without model formation remains confined to inherited frameworks, while execution without conceptual revision only amplifies an existing formulation. We illustrate Layer 2 reasoning through three case studies: S. S. Chern's intrinsic proof of the Gauss-Bonnet theorem, the resolution of the Nesterov Accelerated Gradient convergence problem via Lyapunov functions, and the autonomous disproof of the Erdos unit distance conjecture by OpenAI in 2026. Each case exhibits the same structural signature: a framework that had become inadequate, a missing conceptual object, and a resolution found in an unexpected neighboring field.
中文标题/摘要
标题：科学发现中人工智能的三层框架
当前关于人工智能在科学发现中的讨论常聚焦于两项显性能力：在现有知识中进行搜索以及通过优化、模拟和自动化执行。这两项都很重要，但均未能完全涵盖发现的核心行为——模型的形成与演化。本文提出人工智能在发现中的三层视角。第一层是大型语言模型的搜索与检索。第二层是本文的主要创新，即通过定性推理形成模型：识别当前框架结构上的不足，并在更广阔的表征空间中理解问题，而非通过试错，而是通过结构性洞察明确缺失内容及其所在位置。第三层是执行、优化与精炼。本文主张第二层最为关键，但发展最不充分。缺乏模型形成能力的搜索仍受限于既有框架，而缺乏概念修订的执行只会强化现有表述。我们通过三个案例说明第二层推理：陈省身对高斯-博内定理的内蕴证明、通过李雅普诺夫函数解决Nesterov加速梯度收敛问题，以及OpenAI在2026年自主证伪Erdos单位距离猜想。每个案例均呈现相同结构特征：原有框架已显不足、缺失概念对象、解决方案在相邻领域意外发现。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the gap in current AI for scientific discovery by proposing a three-layer framework that moves beyond mere search and execution to emphasize model formation as the core of discovery. The method introduces Layer 2—qualitative reasoning—which enables AI to identify structural inadequacies in existing frameworks and conceptualize missing elements through structural insight rather than trial and error. Experimental case studies, including Chern’s proof of the Gauss-Bonnet theorem, Nesterov’s convergence analysis via Lyapunov functions, and OpenAI’s disproof of the Erdos unit distance conjecture, demonstrate that successful discovery requires recognizing an inadequate framework, identifying a missing conceptual object, and locating its resolution in an unexpected domain. The results show that without Layer 2, AI remains trapped in inherited models or merely refines existing ones, underscoring its centrality and underdevelopment.
鉴于当前AI在科学发现中的应用过度强调搜索与执行，而忽视模型构建，本文提出三层框架。第一层为大语言模型驱动的知识检索，第三层负责执行与优化，核心创新在于第二层：通过定性推理识别现有框架的结构性不足，并借助结构洞察而非试错，定位缺失概念。三个案例研究——陈省身对高斯-博内定理的内蕴证明、Nesterov加速梯度收敛问题通过李雅普诺夫函数解决、以及OpenAI在2026年自主证伪Erdos单位距离猜想——均显示：科学发现成功需识别不充分框架、定位缺失概念，并在意外相邻领域找到其解决方案。
If LLMs Have Human-Like Attributes, Then So Does Age of Empires II
Authors: Adrian de Wynter
First: 2026-05-29T16:31:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:55:07+00:00
Comments: Fixed corollary 1, added stat sig
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Much research has been carried out on large language models (LLMs) and LLM-powered agentic workflows. However, many works within the field state emergence of, ascribe to, or assume, generalised anthropomorphic attributes to them (e.g., morality or understanding of natural language). Our goal is not to argue in favour or against the existence of these attributes, but to point out that these conclusions could be incorrect. For this we build and train a simple neural network on the videogame Age of Empires II, and note that any entity in a sufficiently-powerful substrate, such as LEGO or the Greater Boston Area, could also present such attributes. Hence, the purported anthropomorphic attributes of LLMs are empirically non-unique: although some properties (e.g., responses to prompts) could remain invariant, others, such as the interpretation of their perceived behaviour, might change with the substrate. Thus, any empirically-grounded discussion on these attributes requires explicit measurement criteria; otherwise the interpretation is left to the representation. We then show that assuming that these attributes exist or not in a system, independent of the substrate and in a generalised way, leads to either circular or uninformative conclusions. This is regardless of the experimenter's viewpoint on the subject, or whether the outcome shows existence or non-existence. Finally we propose a 'null' assumption, where one assumes LLM non-uniqueness instead of assuming anthropomorphic attributes to set up an experiment, along with examples of it. We also discuss potential objections to our work, briefly survey the field, and prove that Age of Empires II is functionally- and Turing-complete.
中文标题/摘要
标题：如果大语言模型具有类人属性，那么《帝国时代2》也一样
已有大量研究探讨大语言模型（LLMs）及其驱动的代理工作流。然而，该领域许多研究声称、归因于或假设LLMs具备泛化的类人属性（如道德或自然语言理解）。我们的目标并非论证这些属性是否存在，而是指出这些结论可能错误。为此，我们在游戏《帝国时代2》上构建并训练了一个简单神经网络，并指出在足够强大的基底（如乐高或波士顿地区）中，任何实体也可能表现出类似属性。因此，LLMs所声称的类人属性在实证上并非独特：尽管某些属性（如对提示的响应）可能保持不变，其他属性（如对行为的解读）则可能随基底变化。因此，任何基于实证的讨论必须明确测量标准，否则解读将依赖于表征。我们进一步证明，无论实验者对主题持何种观点，或结果是否显示属性存在或不存在，假设这些属性独立于基底和泛化存在或不存在，都会导致循环或无信息的结论。最后，我们提出一种“零假设”，即假设LLMs不具备独特性而非假设类人属性来设计实验，并提供示例。同时，我们简要讨论了潜在反对意见，概述了该领域研究，并证明《帝国时代2》在功能上和图灵完备。
Summary / 总结
This paper challenges the assumption that large language models (LLMs) possess unique anthropomorphic attributes like morality or natural language understanding by demonstrating that such behaviors can emerge in any sufficiently complex system, including a simple neural network trained on the game Age of Empires II. The authors argue that these attributes are not intrinsic to LLMs but are instead artifacts of interpretation within a given substrate, and that without explicit measurement criteria, conclusions about such attributes are circular or uninformative. Their experimental results show that while some behaviors may remain invariant across substrates, others—such as perceived intent or agency—vary with context, supporting the idea that anthropomorphism is a representational artifact rather than a property of the system itself. The paper concludes by proposing a 'null' assumption that LLMs are non-unique and suggests this as a more scientifically rigorous baseline for future experiments.
本文质疑将大语言模型（LLM）视为天然具备人性特征（如道德或自然语言理解）的假设，指出类似属性可能并非LLM独有，而可能在任何足够复杂的系统（如训练于《帝国时代2》的神经网络、乐高积木或波士顿地区）中出现。作者表明，尽管某些行为在不同系统间可能保持不变，但对其行为的解读可能随系统载体而异，因此在缺乏明确测量标准的情况下，对人性特征的实证讨论是模糊的。他们证明，无论实验者持何种立场，若不考虑系统载体而笼统假设或否定人性特征，会导致循环或无信息的结论。文章提出“零假设”——将LLM视为非独特系统——以更严谨地设计实验，并给出实例，同时证明《帝国时代2》在功能上和图灵意义上是完备的。
How reliable are LLMs when it comes to playing dice?
Authors: Luca Avena, Gianmarco Bet, Bernardo Busoni
First: 2026-06-05T17:59:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:52:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We investigate the probabilistic reasoning capabilities of large language models through a controlled benchmarking study on discrete probability problems. We constructed two datasets, respectively a set of standard exercises and a set of counterintuitive exercises, designed to trigger heuristic reasoning, and evaluated 8 state-of-the-art models, each tested with and without Chain-of-Thought prompting. Models achieve an average accuracy of 0.96 on standard problems but only 0.59 on counterintuitive ones. We further provide empirical evidence of token bias: performance drops by over 20% when canonical formulations are replaced by disguised variants. Embedding misleading suggestions in the prompt reduces performance by up to 34%, with no model proving immune. Taken together, the reported findings suggest that current LLMs are not yet genuine probabilistic reasoners, despite their success in advanced mathematical problems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在掷骰子游戏中，大语言模型的可靠性如何？
我们通过一项受控基准测试，研究了大语言模型在离散概率问题上的概率推理能力。我们构建了两个数据集：一组标准练习题和一组反直觉练习题，旨在触发启发式推理，并评估了8个最先进的模型（每个模型均在有和无思维链提示的情况下测试）。模型在标准问题上的平均准确率为0.96，但在反直觉问题上仅为0.59。我们进一步提供了词元偏倚的实证证据：当标准表述被伪装变体替代时，性能下降超过20%。在提示中嵌入误导性建议可使性能下降高达34%，没有任何模型完全免疫。综合来看，这些发现表明，尽管大语言模型在高级数学问题上表现优异，但目前它们尚未成为真正的概率推理者。
Summary / 总结
This study examines how well large language models handle probabilistic reasoning by testing them on discrete probability problems, including both standard and counterintuitive cases. Eight state-of-the-art models were evaluated with and without Chain-of-Thought prompting, revealing that while they scored 0.96 on standard problems, their performance dropped to 0.59 on counterintuitive ones. The research also found that performance deteriorates by over 20% when problems are disguised and by up to 34% when misleading suggestions are embedded in prompts, indicating that current LLMs lack robust probabilistic reasoning despite excelling in advanced math.
本研究通过测试离散概率问题，评估大语言模型的概率推理能力，包括标准题与反直觉题两类。八款前沿模型在启用与禁用思维链提示后表现差异显著：标准题准确率达96%，反直觉题骤降至59%。研究还发现模型易受词元偏见和误导性提示影响，问题形式伪装后准确率下降超20%，嵌入误导信息后下降达34%，表明当前LLM虽擅长高阶数学，但尚未具备真正的概率推理能力。
A2D2: Fine-Tuning Any-Length Discrete Diffusion for Adaptive Decoding
Authors: Sophia Tang, Yuchen Zhu, Molei Tao, Pranam Chatterjee
First: 2026-06-11T16:50:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:50:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Discrete diffusion models offer a simple and stable likelihood-based framework for sequence generation, recently extended to any-length settings via token insertion. Principled reward-guided fine-tuning for any-length discrete diffusion, however, remains largely unexplored. We introduce Fine-Tuning Any-Length Discrete Diffusion for Adaptive Decoding (A2D2), a unified framework for reward-guided fine-tuning of any-length discrete diffusion models via joint optimization of the insertion and unmasking policies together with a quality-based inference schedule. We derive the Radon-Nikodym derivative for the joint insertion-unmasking path measures, enabling theoretically guaranteed convergence to the intractable reward-tilted sequence distribution without requiring target samples. Building on this, we establish unmasking and insertion quality as tractable approaches for minimizing decoding error and introduce the Adaptive Joint Decoding (AJD) loss, which provably yields the optimal path measure that generates the reward-tilted distribution. Empirically, A2D2 improves reward optimization while enhancing generation flexibility and accuracy over prior fixed-length fine-tuning and inference-time guidance methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：A2D2：面向自适应解码的任意长度离散扩散模型微调
离散扩散模型提供了一种简单且稳定的基于似然的序列生成框架，最近通过插入标记扩展至任意长度设置。然而，针对任意长度离散扩散模型的原理性奖励引导微调仍鲜有探索。我们提出了面向自适应解码的任意长度离散扩散模型微调（A2D2），这是一个统一框架，通过联合优化插入与解码掩码策略及基于质量的推理调度，实现奖励引导的微调。我们推导了联合插入-解码掩码路径测度的Radon-Nikodym导数，从而在无需目标样本的情况下，理论上保证收敛至不可计算的奖励倾斜序列分布。在此基础上，我们确立了解码掩码与插入质量作为降低解码误差的可行方法，并引入自适应联合解码（AJD）损失，该损失可证明地生成最优路径测度，以产生奖励倾斜分布。实验证明，A2D2在奖励优化方面优于先前的固定长度微调和推理时引导方法，同时提升了生成的灵活性与准确性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for principled reward-guided fine-tuning in any-length discrete diffusion models, the authors propose A2D2, a unified framework that jointly optimizes insertion and unmasking policies with a quality-based inference schedule. By deriving the Radon-Nikodym derivative for joint path measures, they enable convergence to the reward-tilted sequence distribution without target samples. The Adaptive Joint Decoding (AJD) loss is introduced to minimize decoding error and provably yield the optimal path measure. Experiments show A2D2 outperforms prior fixed-length fine-tuning and inference-time guidance methods in reward optimization, generation flexibility, and accuracy.
为解决任意长度离散扩散模型中缺乏原理化奖励引导微调的问题，作者提出A2D2框架，联合优化插入与解码策略并结合质量导向的推理调度。通过推导联合路径测度的Radon-Nikodym导数，实现无需目标样本即可理论保证收敛至奖励倾斜序列分布。引入自适应联合解码（AJD）损失函数，可最小化解码误差并证明其生成最优路径测度。实验表明，A2D2在奖励优化、生成灵活性与准确性方面均优于先前固定长度微调及推理时引导方法。
Transformer Field Theory: A Response-Theoretic Approach to Mechanistic Interpretability
Authors: David N. Olivieri, Antonio F. Pérez Rodríguez
First: 2026-05-24T19:26:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:48:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Mechanistic interpretability often studies Transformer behavior by intervening on internal activations through activation patching, causal tracing, path patching, and steering directions. This paper develops Transformer Field Theory: a response-theoretic framework in which the residual stream of a fixed forward pass is treated as a Transformer field over layer depth and token position. In this formulation, patching becomes a localized source insertion into the Transformer field, first-order sensitivity fields predict patch effects, Green functions describe downstream propagation, and patch selection is posed as an adjoint inverse problem. Empirically, we test the theory's forward response objects in GPT-2-style autoregressive Transformers. Localized Transformer-field interventions exhibit a bounded local linear regime; first-order sensitivities predict patch effects across layer-token sites; localized sources generate structured anisotropic Transformer-field propagation; high-sensitivity sites and sliced Green operators provide reduced response descriptions; and prompt-induced Transformer-field displacements partially transfer answer behavior. These results establish sensitivities, Transformer-field responses, and sliced Green operators as practical objects for organizing patching experiments, while providing the forward mathematical basis for patch-site inference and cross-scale response transfer.
中文标题/摘要
标题：变换器场理论：一种基于响应理论的机制可解释性方法
机制可解释性通常通过激活修补、因果追踪、路径修补和方向引导等方法干预变换器内部激活来研究其行为。本文提出了变换器场理论：一种响应理论框架，将固定前向传递中的残差流视为随层深度和标记位置变化的变换器场。在此框架下，修补操作转化为对变换器场的局部源插入，一阶敏感场可预测修补效应，格林函数描述下游传播，修补选择被表述为伴随逆问题。实证上，我们在GPT-2风格的自回归变换器中测试该理论的前向响应对象。局部变换器场干预表现出有界的局部线性区域；一阶敏感性可预测跨层标记位置的修补效应；局部源生成结构化的各向异性变换器场传播；高敏感性位置与切片格林算子提供简化响应描述；提示诱导的变换器场位移部分转移答案行为。这些结果确立了敏感性、变换器场响应与切片格林算子作为组织修补实验的实用对象，同时为修补位置推断与跨尺度响应迁移提供了前向数学基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to mechanistically interpret Transformer behavior through targeted interventions, this paper introduces Transformer Field Theory, a response-theoretic framework that models the residual stream as a field over layer depth and token position. The method treats activation patching as localized source insertion, uses first-order sensitivity fields to predict patch effects, and employs Green functions to describe downstream propagation, framing patch selection as an adjoint inverse problem. Experiments on GPT-2-style models show that localized interventions operate within a bounded linear regime, first-order sensitivities accurately predict patch outcomes across layer-token positions, sources induce anisotropic propagation, and high-sensitivity sites with sliced Green operators enable compact response descriptions; prompt-induced field displacements also partially transfer answer behavior, validating the framework’s utility for organizing patching experiments and enabling cross-scale response inference.
为理解Transformer机制，本文提出Transformer场理论，将残差流建模为层深度与token位置的场。该方法将干预视为局部源注入，用一阶敏感场预测干预效果，用格林函数描述下游传播，并将源选择建模为伴随逆问题。实验表明，局部干预处于有界线性区域，一阶敏感场可准确预测跨层token位置的干预效果，局部源产生各向异性传播，高敏感位置与切片格林算子可压缩响应描述，且提示诱导的场位移可部分迁移答案行为。
Contrast-Informed Augmentation and Domain-Adversarial Training for Adult-to-Neonatal MR Reconstruction Generalization
Authors: Stephen Moore, Lara Leijser, Richard Frayne, Roberto Souza
First: 2026-06-11T16:47:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:47:53+00:00
Comments: 24 pages, 1 table, 7 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Purpose: To investigate whether contrast-informed data augmentation and domain-adversarial training improve the adult-to-neonatal generalization of the E2E-VarNet. Methods: Three training regimes were investigated: (1) adult-only training with unaugmented adult data, (2) mixed training with paired unaugmented and neonatal-informed augmented adult data, and (3) mixed training with a domain-adversarial objective. Models were trained on retrospectively undersampled multi-coil adult T2-weighted brain MR data and evaluated on neonatal and adult test data at acceleration factors $R=4$ and $R=8$ using quantitative metrics and qualitative evaluation. Feature analyses assessed whether domain-adversarial training altered the latent representations of unaugmented adult, augmented adult, and neonatal test samples. Results: Mixed training (Mixed) and mixed domain-adversarial training (Mixed-DAT) outperformed unaugmented adult-only training (Unaug-Only) when evaluated on neonatal data. At R=4, Mixed-DAT achieved the best performance (SSIM = 0.924 +/- 0.027, PSNR = 33.98 +/- 1.15 dB). At R=8, Mixed-DAT performed best when measured using SSIM (0.848 +/- 0.031 vs. 0.766 +/- 0.037 for Unaug-Only and 0.814 +/- 0.035 for Mixed) and Mixed performed best when measured using PSNR (29.56 +/- 0.83 dB vs. 26.26 +/- 0.78 dB for Unaug-Only and 29.43 +/- 0.83 dB for Mixed-DAT). Qualitative assessment of t-SNE plots suggested that Mixed-DAT increased the overlap among the latent representations of the unaugmented adult, augmented adult, and neonatal test data. Conclusion: Contrast-informed augmentation and domain-adversarial training improved adult-to-neonatal generalization of deep learning-based MR reconstruction. These findings suggest that contrast-informed data augmentation combined with adversarial training may improve robustness to domain shift in undersampled neonatal MR reconstruction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于对比信息增强与领域对抗训练的成人至新生儿MR重建泛化方法
目的：探究对比信息数据增强与领域对抗训练是否能提升E2E-VarNet在成人至新生儿MR重建中的泛化能力。方法：比较三种训练策略：(1) 仅使用未增强成人数据的成人训练；(2) 混合训练，使用未增强成人数据与新生儿信息增强的成人数据配对；(3) 混合训练并加入领域对抗目标。模型在回顾性欠采样多线圈成人T2加权脑MR数据上训练，并在加速因子R=4和R=8下，使用定量指标和定性评估在新生儿和成人测试数据上进行评估。特征分析评估领域对抗训练是否改变了未增强成人、增强成人及新生儿测试样本的潜在表示。结果：混合训练（Mixed）和混合领域对抗训练（Mixed-DAT）在新生儿数据评估中优于未增强成人仅训练（Unaug-Only）。在R=4时，Mixed-DAT表现最佳（SSIM=0.924±0.027，PSNR=33.98±1.15 dB）。在R=8时，Mixed-DAT在SSIM指标上表现最佳（0.848±0.031 vs. Unaug-Only的0.766±0.037和Mixed的0.814±0.035），而Mixed在PSNR指标上表现最佳（29.56±0.83 dB vs. Unaug-Only的26.26±0.78 dB和Mixed-DAT的29.43±0.83 dB）。t-SNE图的定性评估表明，Mixed-DAT增加了未增强成人、增强成人及新生儿测试数据潜在表示之间的重叠。结论：对比信息增强与领域对抗训练可提升基于深度学习的MR重建在成人至新生儿间的泛化能力。这些发现表明，结合对比信息数据增强与对抗训练可能提高欠采样新生儿MR重建对领域偏移的鲁棒性。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether contrast-informed data augmentation and domain-adversarial training can enhance the generalization of an end-to-end VarNet model when reconstructing neonatal MR images from adult training data. Three training approaches were compared: training solely on adult data without augmentation, training with contrast-informed augmented adult data, and training with domain-adversarial regularization. Models were evaluated on neonatal and adult test data at acceleration factors R=4 and R=8 using SSIM and PSNR metrics. Results showed that the domain-adversarial augmented approach (Mixed-DAT) outperformed both baseline and augmented-only methods on neonatal data, achieving the highest SSIM at R=4 and superior SSIM and PSNR at R=8 depending on metric. Qualitative t-SNE analysis indicated that Mixed-DAT improved latent space alignment across adult and neonatal data distributions, suggesting enhanced domain robustness.
本研究旨在通过结合对比引导的数据增强与领域对抗训练，提升端到端VarNet模型在从欠采样成人数据重建新生儿MR图像时的泛化能力。实验比较了三种训练策略：仅使用未增强成人数据训练、使用新生儿对比引导增强的成人数据混合训练，以及加入领域对抗损失的混合训练。模型在成人与新生儿测试集（加速因子R=4和R=8）上通过SSIM和PSNR指标评估，并辅以t-SNE可视化分析潜在表示。结果显示，领域对抗训练显著提升新生儿重建效果，在R=4时SSIM最高达0.924，R=8时在SSIM指标上优于其他方法；同时在PSNR指标上，混合增强训练表现最佳。可视化分析表明，领域对抗训练增强了成人与新生儿数据在潜在空间中的重叠，说明其有效提升了跨域适应能力。
Causal Inference with Generative Artificial Intelligence: Application to Texts as Treatments
Authors: Kosuke Imai, Kentaro Nakamura
First: 2024-10-01T17:46:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:46:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this paper, we demonstrate how to enhance the validity of causal inference with unstructured high-dimensional treatments like texts, by leveraging the power of generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI). Specifically, we propose to use a deep generative model such as large language models (LLMs) to efficiently generate treatments and use their internal representation for subsequent causal effect estimation. We show that the knowledge of this true internal representation helps disentangle the treatment features of interest, such as specific sentiments and certain topics, from other possibly unknown confounding features. Unlike existing methods, the proposed GenAI-Powered Inference (GPI) methodology eliminates the need to learn causal representation from the data, and hence produces more accurate and efficient estimates. We formally establish the conditions required for the nonparametric identification of the average treatment effect, propose an estimation strategy that avoids the violation of the overlap assumption, and derive the asymptotic properties of the proposed estimator through the application of double machine learning. Finally, using an instrumental variables approach, we extend the proposed GPI methodology to the settings in which the treatment feature is based on human perception. The GPI is also applicable to text reuse where an LLM is used to regenerate existing texts. We conduct simulation and empirical studies, using the generated text data from an open-source LLM, Llama 3, to illustrate the advantages of our estimator over state-of-the-art causal representation learning algorithms.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用生成式人工智能进行因果推断：文本作为处理的应用
本文展示了如何利用生成式人工智能（GenAI）的力量，提升对非结构化高维处理（如文本）进行因果推断的有效性。具体而言，我们提出使用深度生成模型（如大语言模型，LLMs）高效生成处理，并利用其内部表征进行后续因果效应估计。我们证明，对这一真实内部表征的知识有助于分离出感兴趣的处理特征（如特定情感和某些主题），并将其与可能未知的混杂特征区分开来。与现有方法不同，所提出的GenAI赋能推断（GPI）方法无需从数据中学习因果表征，因而能产生更准确、高效的估计结果。我们正式确立了平均处理效应非参数识别所需的条件，提出了一种避免违反重叠假设的估计策略，并通过双重机器学习推导了所提估计量的渐近性质。最后，我们利用工具变量方法，将GPI方法扩展至处理特征基于人类感知的情形。GPI同样适用于文本重用场景，其中LLM用于重新生成现有文本。我们通过使用开源LLM Llama 3生成的文本数据进行模拟和实证研究，展示了我们的估计量相较于当前最先进的因果表征学习算法的优势。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of estimating causal effects from unstructured, high-dimensional text treatments, this paper introduces GenAI-Powered Inference (GPI), a method that uses large language models to generate treatments and exploit their internal representations for causal effect estimation. Unlike traditional approaches that require learning causal representations from data, GPI leverages the inherent structure of LLM-generated text to disentangle target treatment features—such as sentiment or topic—from confounding variables, thereby improving estimation accuracy and efficiency. The method formally establishes identification conditions, avoids overlap assumption violations via double machine learning, and extends to human-perceived treatments using instrumental variables. Empirical and simulation studies using Llama 3-generated text confirm GPI outperforms state-of-the-art causal representation learning methods.
为解决从非结构化高维文本处理中估计因果效应的难题，本文提出GenAI-Powered Inference（GPI）方法，利用大语言模型生成处理并借助其内部表征进行因果效应估计。与传统方法需从数据中学习因果表征不同，GPI直接利用模型的潜在结构，分离出目标处理特征（如情感或主题）与混杂变量，从而提升估计精度与效率。该方法形式化了识别条件，通过双重机器学习避免重叠假设的违反，并扩展至基于人类感知的处理场景。实验结果表明，使用Llama 3生成的文本数据，GPI在模拟与实证研究中均优于现有因果表征学习方法。
Appearance-Invariant Detection of Suggestive Motion via Laban Movement Descriptors
Authors: Jaehoon Ahn, Jeonghan Kong, Moon-Ryul Jung
Venue: SIGGRAPH 2026 poster
First: 2026-05-23T09:22:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:42:25+00:00
Comments: 5 pages, 2 figures, 3 tables. Extended version of a poster accepted to SIGGRAPH 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Content moderation in online multiplayer 3D virtual environments is increasingly automated, yet detection has focused on images, video, and audio, leaving suggestive motion a blind spot. We present a motion-only classification pipeline that detects suggestive and explicit movement from SMPL skeleton trajectories using Laban Movement Analysis (LMA) descriptors. On a dataset spanning everyday, artistic, suggestive, and explicit movement (17+ hours of video), a logistic regression trained on 61-feature LMA descriptors reaches 68% binary SFW/NSFW accuracy (70% random forest) under a leak-free evaluation protocol. At this level, our descriptor performs comparably to a learned video model trained on the same motion re-rendered as appearance-free video, a gray figure with no clothing, skin, or scene. The indirectness (tortuosity) of each joint's trajectory, measured as the ratio of the joint's path length to its net displacement, peaks at the suggestive tier, showing that the Direct-to-Indirect polarity of Laban's Space factor provides an interpretable marker of the shift from functional to suggestive motion. Ultimately, Laban-based kinematic descriptors offer a lightweight, interpretable approach to suggestive-motion detection: every decision decomposes into named, theory-grounded features. Because the classifier operates on pose trajectories alone, moderation can run directly on avatar poses in virtual environments, with no appearance data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于拉班运动描述符的暗示性动作外观不变检测
在线多人3D虚拟环境中的内容审核日益自动化，但现有检测主要针对图像、视频和音频，对暗示性动作存在盲区。本文提出一种仅基于运动的分类流程，利用拉班运动分析（LMA）描述符从SMPL骨架轨迹中检测暗示性和显性动作。在涵盖日常、艺术、暗示及显性动作（共17小时视频）的数据集上，基于61维LMA特征的逻辑回归模型在无泄漏评估协议下达到68%的SFW/NSFW二分类准确率（随机森林为70%）。在此水平下，该描述符表现与在相同运动重渲染为无外观视频（灰色人形，无服装、皮肤或场景）上训练的视频模型相当。每个关节轨迹的间接性（曲折度），即路径长度与净位移之比，在暗示性层级达到峰值，表明拉班空间因素的“直接-间接”极性可作为从功能性动作向暗示性动作转变的可解释性标记。最终，基于拉班的运动学描述符提供了一种轻量且可解释的暗示性动作检测方法：每个决策均可分解为命名明确、理论支撑的特征。由于分类器仅基于姿态轨迹运行，审核可直接在虚拟环境中的角色姿态上执行，无需外观数据。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap in automated content moderation for suggestive motion in 3D virtual environments, this work introduces a motion-only detection pipeline using Laban Movement Analysis descriptors applied to SMPL skeleton trajectories. The method extracts 61 kinematic features capturing movement qualities like tortuosity and directionality, then trains classifiers (logistic regression and random forest) on a 17+ hour dataset of everyday, artistic, suggestive, and explicit motion. Results show 68% binary SFW/NSFW accuracy under strict evaluation, matching performance of a video model trained on appearance-free re-rendered motion. Key findings reveal that joint trajectory tortuosity peaks in suggestive motion, validating Laban’s Space factor as an interpretable indicator of motion intent, enabling lightweight, appearance-free moderation directly on avatar poses.
为弥补3D虚拟环境中自动内容审核对暗示性动作的盲区，该研究提出一种仅基于运动轨迹的检测方法，利用Laban动作分析描述符对SMPL骨架轨迹进行建模。方法从运动中提取61个运动学特征，训练分类器区分暗示性与非暗示性动作。在17小时以上视频数据集上，严格评估下达到68%二分类准确率，与基于无外观视频的深度学习模型表现相当。实验发现，关节轨迹的曲折度（路径长度与位移比）在暗示性动作阶段达到峰值，验证了Laban空间因素中“直接-间接”极性作为运动意图转变的可解释性指标。该方法仅依赖姿态数据即可实现实时审核，无需视觉外观信息。
QueryOcc: Query-based Self-Supervision for 3D Semantic Occupancy
Authors: Adam Lilja, Ji Lan, Junsheng Fu, Lars Hammarstrand
First: 2025-11-21T13:05:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:41:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Learning 3D scene geometry and semantics from images is a core challenge in computer vision and a key capability for autonomous driving. Since large-scale 3D annotation is prohibitively expensive, recent work explores self-supervised learning directly from sensor data without manual labels. Existing approaches either rely on 2D rendering consistency, where 3D structure emerges only implicitly, or on discretized voxel grids from accumulated lidar point clouds, limiting spatial precision and scalability. We introduce QueryOcc, a query-based self-supervised framework that learns continuous 3D semantic occupancy directly through independent 4D spatio-temporal queries sampled across adjacent frames. The framework supports supervision from either pseudo-point clouds derived from vision foundation models or raw lidar data. To enable long-range supervision and reasoning under constant memory, we introduce a contractive scene representation that preserves near-field detail while smoothly compressing distant regions. QueryOcc surpasses previous camera-based methods by 26% in semantic RayIoU on the self-supervised Occ3D-nuScenes benchmark while running at 11.6 FPS, demonstrating that direct 4D query supervision enables strong self-supervised occupancy learning. https://research.zenseact.com/publications/queryocc/
中文标题/摘要
标题：QueryOcc：基于查询的自监督3D语义占据学习
从图像中学习3D场景几何结构与语义是计算机视觉的核心挑战，也是自动驾驶的关键能力。由于大规模3D标注成本过高，近期研究探索直接从传感器数据中进行自监督学习，无需人工标注。现有方法要么依赖2D渲染一致性（3D结构仅隐式出现），要么基于累积激光雷达点云的离散体素网格，限制了空间精度与可扩展性。我们提出QueryOcc，一种基于查询的自监督框架，通过在相邻帧间采样的独立4D时空查询，直接学习连续的3D语义占据。该框架支持来自视觉基础模型生成的伪点云或原始激光雷达数据的监督。为实现长程监督与常量内存下的推理，我们引入一种收缩场景表示，保留近场细节的同时平滑压缩远场区域。QueryOcc在自监督Occ3D-nuScenes基准测试中，语义RayIoU指标超越现有相机方法26%，运行速度达11.6 FPS，证明直接4D查询监督可实现强大的自监督占据学习。https://research.zenseact.com/publications/queryocc/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high cost of 3D annotations for autonomous driving, this paper proposes QueryOcc, a self-supervised framework that learns continuous 3D semantic occupancy directly from 4D spatio-temporal queries sampled across adjacent frames. It leverages either pseudo-point clouds from vision models or raw lidar data for supervision and employs a contractive scene representation to maintain detail near the camera while compressing distant regions under memory constraints. Experiments on the Occ3D-nuScenes benchmark show QueryOcc achieves a 26% improvement in semantic RayIoU over prior camera-based methods while operating at 11.6 FPS.
为降低3D标注成本并克服现有自监督方法的局限，QueryOcc提出一种基于查询的框架，通过跨相邻帧采样的4D时空查询直接学习连续的3D语义占据。该方法支持从视觉基础模型生成的伪点云或原始激光雷达数据中获取监督信号，并引入收缩场景表示以保留近场细节同时压缩远场区域，实现高效长程推理。在Occ3D-nuScenes自监督基准测试中，QueryOcc在语义RayIoU指标上比现有相机基自监督方法高出26%，同时运行速度达11.6 FPS。
Edit the Bits, Diff the Codes: Bitwise Residual Editing for Visual Autoregressive Models
Authors: Shengqiang Zhang, Ruotong Liao, Volker Tresp, Barbara Plank, Hinrich Schütze
First: 2026-06-11T16:41:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:41:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Text-guided image editing with visual autoregressive (VAR) generators requires controlling both what the model samples and where the sampled change is written back into the image code. Existing VAR editors mainly operate on token streams, features, or flat next-token logits, leaving two native structures of bitwise-residual VAR models underused: the per-bit Bernoulli prediction head and the additive multi-scale residual code field from which the image is assembled. We propose BitResEdit, a training-free editor for bitwise-residual VAR generators such as Infinity. BitEdit performs source-negative guidance by tilting the post-CFG per-bit log-odds along a source--target contrast computed on a shared edited prefix, then projects each update into a closed-form Bernoulli-KL trust region around the clean CFG sampler. ResEdit converts the sampled bits into per-scale continuous-code residuals, gates them with a localization mask, and re-injects them through the generator's native sum-of-scales. Together they couple decision-time bit guidance with combination-time code composition, so masked-out latent features are preserved exactly by code arithmetic while localized, scale-aware edits are applied inside the target region. On PIE-Bench with Infinity-2B, BitResEdit attains the strongest text alignment among same-backbone VAR editors, improving CLIP on the edited region by +1.07 over the strongest prior editor while keeping background preservation competitive with it. Ablations show BitEdit and ResEdit play complementary roles in target alignment and background preservation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：编辑比特，差异编码：面向视觉自回归模型的比特残差编辑
基于文本引导的图像编辑，使用视觉自回归（VAR）生成器时，需同时控制模型采样内容及采样结果写回图像编码的位置。现有VAR编辑器主要操作于标记流、特征或扁平的下一标记对数概率，而未充分利用比特残差VAR模型的两种原生结构：逐比特伯努利预测头与用于图像合成的加性多尺度残差码域。我们提出BitResEdit，一种面向比特残差VAR生成器（如Infinity）的无训练编辑器。BitEdit通过在共享编辑前缀上计算源-目标对比，倾斜后CFG阶段的逐比特对数似然比，并将每个更新投影至围绕干净CFG采样器的闭式伯努利-KL可信区域；ResEdit将采样比特转换为逐尺度连续码残差，通过定位掩码进行门控，再通过生成器原生的多尺度求和机制重新注入。二者协同实现决策时比特引导与组合时码域合成，使掩码外潜在特征通过码域运算精确保留，同时在目标区域内施加局部、尺度感知的编辑。在PIE-Bench上使用Infinity-2B时，BitResEdit在同架构VAR编辑器中实现最强文本对齐，编辑区域CLIP得分较最强前序编辑器提升+1.07，同时保持背景保留能力与之相当。消融实验表明BitEdit与ResEdit在目标对齐与背景保留中发挥互补作用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to precisely control both content and location in text-guided image editing using visual autoregressive models, the authors introduce BitResEdit, a training-free editor that leverages two underused native structures: the per-bit Bernoulli prediction head and the additive multi-scale residual code field. BitEdit guides bit-level decisions via source-target contrast on a shared prefix, projecting updates into a Bernoulli-KL trust region around the clean CFG sampler. ResEdit converts sampled bits into continuous residuals, gates them with a localization mask, and re-injects them via the generator’s native sum-of-scales. Together, they enable exact preservation of masked features while applying localized, scale-aware edits. On PIE-Bench with Infinity-2B, BitResEdit achieves the best text alignment among same-backbone editors, improving CLIP score by +1.07 over prior methods while maintaining competitive background preservation.
为提升文本引导图像编辑中内容与位置的精确控制，本文提出BitResEdit，一种无需训练的编辑器，利用视觉自回归模型中被忽视的两个原生结构：逐比特伯努利预测头和加性多尺度残差码场。BitEdit通过源-目标对比调整采样后CFG的比特对数似然，限制在伯努利-KL可信区域内；ResEdit将采样比特转化为尺度感知的连续残差，经局部掩码门控后通过生成器原生的多尺度求和机制重注入。在PIE-Bench上使用Infinity-2B实验表明，BitResEdit在同架构VAR编辑器中实现最佳文本对齐，CLIP得分较前序方法提升+1.07，同时保持与之相当的背景保留能力。
InnoEval: On Research Idea Evaluation as a Knowledge-Grounded, Multi-Perspective Reasoning Problem
Authors: Shuofei Qiao, Yunxiang Wei, Xuehai Wang, Bin Wu, Boyang Xue, Ningyu Zhang, Hossein A. Rahmani, Yanshan Wang, Qiang Zhang, Keyan Ding, Jeff Z. Pan, Huajun Chen, Emine Yilmaz
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-02-16T00:40:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:39:44+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
