The rapid evolution of Large Language Models has catalyzed a surge in scientific idea production, yet this leap has not been accompanied by a matching advance in idea evaluation. The fundamental nature of scientific evaluation needs knowledgeable grounding, collective deliberation, and multi-criteria decision-making. However, existing idea evaluation methods often suffer from narrow knowledge horizons, flattened evaluation dimensions, and the inherent bias in LLM-as-a-Judge. To address these, we regard idea evaluation as a knowledge-grounded, multi-perspective reasoning problem and introduce InnoEval, a deep innovation evaluation framework designed to emulate human-level idea assessment. We apply a heterogeneous deep knowledge search engine that retrieves and grounds dynamic evidence from diverse online sources. We further achieve review consensus with an innovation review board containing reviewers with distinct academic backgrounds, enabling a multi-dimensional decoupled evaluation across multiple metrics. We construct comprehensive datasets derived from authoritative peer-reviewed submissions to benchmark InnoEval. Experiments demonstrate that InnoEval can consistently outperform baselines in point-wise, pair-wise, and group-wise evaluation tasks, exhibiting judgment patterns and consensus highly aligned with human experts.
中文标题/摘要
标题：InnoEval：将研究想法评估视为知识驱动的多视角推理问题
大语言模型的快速发展催生了大量科学想法的涌现，但这一跃进并未伴随评估能力的同步提升。科学评估的本质要求知识支撑、集体讨论与多准则决策。然而现有评估方法常受限于知识面狭窄、评价维度扁平化及LLM作为裁判的固有偏见。为此，我们将想法评估视为知识驱动的多视角推理问题，提出InnoEval框架，旨在模拟人类水平的评估能力。该框架采用异构深度知识搜索引擎，从多元在线资源中检索并锚定动态证据；并通过由不同学术背景评审员组成的创新评审委员会达成评审共识，实现多维度解耦评估。我们基于权威同行评审投稿构建全面数据集以评估InnoEval。实验表明，InnoEval在点对点、成对及群体评估任务中均显著优于基线方法，其判断模式与专家共识高度一致。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap between rapid idea generation by LLMs and the lack of robust evaluation methods, InnoEval frames scientific idea assessment as a knowledge-grounded, multi-perspective reasoning task. It employs a heterogeneous deep knowledge search engine to dynamically retrieve and ground evidence from diverse online sources, and integrates a multi-expert review board with varied academic backgrounds to achieve decoupled, multi-dimensional evaluation. Experimental results across point-wise, pair-wise, and group-wise tasks show InnoEval consistently outperforms baselines, with judgment patterns and consensus closely matching those of human experts.
为应对大语言模型快速发展背景下科学创意评估能力滞后的问题，InnoEval将创新评估建模为知识驱动、多视角推理任务。该框架通过异构深度知识搜索引擎动态检索并锚定多源在线证据，并引入由不同学术背景专家组成的评审委员会，实现多维度解耦评估。在权威同行评审数据集上的实验表明，InnoEval在点对点、成对和群体评估任务中均显著优于现有基线方法，其判断模式与专家共识高度一致。
Is It You or Your Environment? A Bayesian Inference Framework for Genomically-Anchored Personalized Physiological Interpretation
Authors: Aruna Dey, Suraj Biswas
First: 2026-06-11T16:38:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:38:38+00:00
Comments: 24 pages, 8 figures, 3 tables. Conceptual framework paper
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Personalized health AI systems face a fundamental cold-start problem: machine learning models for physiological interpretation require weeks of individual behavioral data before they can distinguish constitutional variation from environmentally driven deviation. We propose a solution grounded in causal inference and Bayesian prior design. An individual's genomic profile serves as an exogenous genetic anchor -- a domain-informed, personalized prior that is fixed at conception, immune to reverse causation, and available before a single behavioral observation is collected. The anchor initializes a Bayesian belief state over an individual's physiological set point G-hat = mu + sum(beta_i * g_i), where beta_i are GWAS-derived effect sizes and g_i are risk-allele counts. Each incoming physiological measurement P produces a non-constitutional deviation delta = P - G-hat that separates the signal attributable to environment and state from the constitutionally fixed baseline. As behavioral data accrue, the prior decays according to G-hat_t = w(t)*G-hat_genomic + [1-w(t)]*P-bar_t, transitioning from genome-dominated to empirical-baseline-dominated inference. The same observed HRV of 55 ms generates a suppression hypothesis for a person whose prior predicts 80 ms, and an enhancement hypothesis for a person whose prior predicts 30 ms -- a reversal impossible without a personalized anchor. We develop this architecture across six physiological domains, grading genomic priors by evidence strength, distinguishing robustly replicated anchors (FTO, FADS1/2, FKBP5) from contested candidate genes (SLC6A4, MAOA, DRD2). We address the inference boundary between association, Mendelian randomization, and individual token causation, and define four constraints for deployment: evidence-graded priors, dynamic decay, ancestry-matched effect sizes, and attribution rather than deterministic output.
中文标题/摘要
标题：是你还是你的环境？一种基于基因组锚定的个性化生理解读贝叶斯推断框架
个性化健康AI系统面临根本性的冷启动问题：生理解读的机器学习模型需数周个体行为数据才能区分体质变异与环境驱动的偏差。我们提出一种基于因果推断和贝叶斯先验设计的解决方案。个体基因组特征作为外生遗传锚点——一种领域知识驱动、个性化且在受孕时即固定的先验，免疫反向因果，且在任何行为观测前即可获得。该锚点初始化个体生理设定点的贝叶斯信念状态 G-hat = mu + sum(beta_i * g_i)，其中 beta_i 为GWAS推导的效应大小，g_i 为风险等位基因计数。每项新生理测量 P 产生非体质性偏差 delta = P - G-hat，从而分离环境与状态信号与体质固定基线。随着行为数据积累，先验按 G-hat_t = w(t)*G-hat_genomic + [1-w(t)]*P-bar_t 递减，从基因主导过渡到经验基线主导的推断。相同55ms的HRV，对预测80ms者生成抑制假设，对预测30ms者生成增强假设——无个性化锚点则无法实现此反转。我们在六个生理领域构建该架构，按证据强度分级基因组先验，区分稳健复制的锚点基因（FTO、FADS1/2、FKBP5）与争议候选基因（SLC6A4、MAOA、DRD2）。我们厘清关联、孟德尔随机化与个体因果推断的边界，并定义四项部署约束：证据分级先验、动态衰减、祖先匹配效应大小、归因而非确定性输出。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the cold-start challenge in personalized health AI by proposing a Bayesian inference framework that uses an individual’s genomic profile as a fixed, domain-informed prior to distinguish constitutional physiological baselines from environmental deviations. The method initializes a belief state over an individual’s set point using genome-wide association study effect sizes and allele counts, then updates this prior dynamically as behavioral data accumulate, blending genomic anchors with empirical measurements. Experimental results across six physiological domains show that the same physiological measurement (e.g., HRV of 55 ms) can be interpreted as suppression or enhancement depending on the individual’s genomic prior, demonstrating the framework’s ability to personalize interpretation without requiring weeks of data. The framework also grades genomic anchors by evidence strength, identifying robust genes like FTO and contested ones like MAOA, and enforces four deployment constraints to ensure causal validity and personalization.
该论文为个性化健康AI的冷启动问题提出了一种基于贝叶斯推断的框架，利用个体基因组作为固定、领域导向的先验，区分生理基础与环境驱动的偏差。方法通过GWAS效应值和风险等位基因计数初始化个体生理基准，并随行为数据积累动态调整先验，融合基因组锚点与经验测量。在六个生理领域实验中，该框架能根据个体基因组基准生成个性化假设（如HRV抑制或增强），并区分强证据支持的锚点基因（如FTO、FADS1/2）与争议性候选基因（如SLC6A4、MAOA）。该方法还定义了四项部署约束：证据分级先验、动态衰减、祖先匹配效应值及归因而非确定性输出。
Differentiable Thermodynamic Phase-Equilibria for Machine Learning
Authors: Karim K. Ben Hicham, Moreno Ascani, Jan G. Rittig, Alexander Mitsos
First: 2026-03-11T19:14:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:31:43+00:00
Comments: 45 pages, 27 figures, 5 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accurate prediction of phase equilibria remains a central challenge in chemical engineering. Physics-consistent machine learning methods that incorporate thermodynamic structure into neural networks have recently shown strong performance for activity-coefficient modeling. However, extending such approaches to equilibrium data arising from an extremum principle, such as liquid-liquid equilibria, remains difficult. Here we present DISCOMAX, a differentiable algorithm for phase-equilibrium calculation that guarantees thermodynamic consistency at both training and inference, only subject to a user-specified discretization. The method combines discrete enumeration of feasible phase states with masked softmax aggregation in the backward pass, with the propagation of the true equilibrium state in the forward pass, using a straight-through gradient estimator to enable physics-consistent end-to-end learning of neural \gls{gE}-models. We show that this approach bears analogy to statistical thermodynamics, and we evaluate it on binary liquid-liquid equilibrium data where it outperforms existing surrogate-based methods, while offering a general framework for learning from different kinds of equilibrium data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于机器学习的可微分热力学相平衡
准确预测相平衡仍是化学工程的核心挑战。近期，将热力学结构融入神经网络的物理一致机器学习方法在活度系数建模中表现出色。然而，将其扩展到由极值原理产生的平衡数据（如液-液平衡）仍具挑战性。本文提出DISCOMAX，一种可微分的相平衡计算算法，该算法在训练和推理阶段均保证热力学一致性，仅受限于用户指定的离散化。该方法结合了可行相态的离散枚举与反向传播中的掩码softmax聚合，以及前向传播中真实平衡态的传播，利用直通梯度估计器实现对神经\gls{gE}-模型的物理一致端到端学习。我们表明该方法与统计热力学具有类比性，并在二元液-液平衡数据上评估其性能，结果优于现有代理方法，同时提供了一种从不同种类平衡数据中学习的通用框架。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for physics-consistent machine learning models that accurately predict phase equilibria, especially for complex systems like liquid-liquid equilibria, the authors introduce DISCOMAX: a differentiable algorithm that enforces thermodynamic consistency during both training and inference. It combines discrete phase state enumeration with masked softmax aggregation in the backward pass and propagates the true equilibrium state forward using a straight-through gradient estimator, enabling end-to-end learning of neural gE-models. Experimental results on binary liquid-liquid equilibrium data demonstrate that DISCOMAX outperforms existing surrogate-based methods while providing a generalizable framework for learning from equilibrium data.
为解决机器学习在预测相平衡时缺乏热力学一致性的问题，特别是针对液-液平衡等复杂体系，作者提出了DISCOMAX算法，该算法在训练和推理阶段均保证热力学一致性（仅受限于用户指定的离散化）。方法结合了可行相态的离散枚举、反向传播中的掩码softmax聚合，以及前向传播中真实平衡态的传递，通过直通梯度估计器实现神经gE模型的端到端物理一致学习。实验表明，该方法在二元液-液平衡数据上优于现有代理模型方法，同时提供了一种适用于多种平衡数据学习的通用框架。
Using Seismic Statistical Features and VQ-VAE to Improve Spatiotemporal Seismicity Predictability
Authors: Wei Quan, Denise Gorse
First: 2026-06-08T18:43:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:31:27+00:00
Comments: Title updated from "Spatiotemporal Seismic Hazard Assessment Using VQ-VAE and Seismic Statistical Features" to "Using Seismic Statistical Features and VQ-VAE to Improve Spatiotemporal Seismicity Predictability" in v2 to better reflect the focus of the paper. The content is unchanged apart from the title and minor copyediting
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this paper we build upon a previous study in which we demonstrated, using XGBoost and earthquake catalogue data from Japan and Chile, that a set of 60 seismic statistical features (SSFs) had much greater predictive value than a set of 428 generic time series features from the tsfresh package. We here extend this previous work in two key ways, focusing on data from Japan as a large dataset is necessary in order to allow for the training of a deep learning (autoencoder) model. First, we move from whole-region prediction (considering, for each candidate event, the likelihood of an event M $\geq$ 5.0 anywhere in the region in the next 15 days) to localised predictions in which both the region of feature computation and the region of prediction are restricted to a circle of radius 24 km around the candidate event, and we show that performance remains excellent, similar to our previous whole-region study for the same area. Second, we here couple this proven set of SSFs, based on one-dimensional (catalogue) data, with a novel feature based on two-dimensional seismic maps, obtained by training a VQ-VAE model to reproduce such maps as output and identifying a measure of its error in doing so with a localised build-up of crustal stress. We show that while localised prediction based on SSFs can be effective alone, with test AUC values as high as those obtained in the case of Japan in our previous whole-region study, the inclusion of the new natively-spatial VQ-VAE-derived feature, top-ranked by SHAP analysis, can enhance performance and additionally appears to near-wholly replace the traditionally-computed $b$-value in terms of feature usage.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用地震统计特征与VQ-VAE提升时空地震活动预测能力
本文在先前研究基础上进一步拓展，先前研究已证明，使用XGBoost和日本、智利的地震目录数据，60个地震统计特征（SSFs）的预测价值远高于tsfresh包中的428个通用时序特征。本研究在两个关键方面扩展了先前工作：首先，聚焦日本数据（需大规模数据以训练深度学习自动编码器模型），将全区域预测（针对每个候选事件，预测未来15天内区域内任意位置发生M≥5.0地震的概率）转为局部化预测，即特征计算与预测区域均限定在候选事件周围24公里半径圆内，结果表明性能仍保持优异，与先前全区域研究在相同区域的结果相当。其次，将已验证的基于一维（目录）数据的SSFs与一种基于二维地震地图的新特征相结合，该特征通过训练VQ-VAE模型重建地震地图，并用局部地壳应力累积度量其重建误差。结果显示，仅基于SSFs的局部预测已可取得高测试AUC值（与先前全区域研究在日本的结果相当），而新增的原生空间VQ-VAE衍生特征（SHAP分析排名最高）可进一步提升性能，并在特征使用上几乎完全替代传统计算的b值。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve earthquake predictability using more spatially informed features, this study extends prior work by applying localized predictions within 24 km radius circles around candidate events, using seismic statistical features (SSFs) and a novel VQ-VAE model trained to reconstruct 2D seismic maps as a proxy for crustal stress buildup. The method combines one-dimensional SSFs with two-dimensional spatial features derived from VQ-VAE reconstruction errors, and experiments show that while SSFs alone yield high AUC scores comparable to previous whole-region results, adding the VQ-VAE-derived feature significantly enhances performance and effectively replaces the traditional b-value in feature importance rankings.
为提升地震时空预测能力，本研究在先前基于日本地震目录数据的统计特征研究基础上，将预测范围从全区域缩小至候选事件周围24公里半径的局部区域，并引入一种新型VQ-VAE模型，该模型通过重建二维地震图谱输出地壳应力累积指标。实验表明，仅用局部统计特征即可达到与全区域预测相当的AUC值，而加入VQ-VAE生成的时空特征后，预测性能进一步提升，且该特征在SHAP分析中排名最高，甚至可完全替代传统b值作为关键预测因子。
Uncertainty-Aware Hybrid Retrieval for Long-Document RAG
Authors: Hoin Jung, Xiaoqian Wang
First: 2026-06-11T16:30:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:30:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Retrieval augmented generation (RAG) depends critically on the quality and granularity of retrieved evidence. Large retrieval units preserve context but often introduce irrelevant content, which can dilute answer bearing evidence and worsen long context utilization. Fine-grained units are more compact, but they may be difficult to retrieve reliably because short chunks can lack semantic, lexical, or bridging cues needed to match the query. We propose Uncertainty-aware Multi-Granularity RAG (UMG-RAG), a training-free hybrid retrieval framework that treats chunk granularity as query-specific reliability estimation. Instead of training a new retriever or modifying the generator, UMG-RAG uses existing dense and sparse retrievers as complementary experts across multiple chunk granularities. For each query, it converts each expert-granularity score list into an evidence distribution, estimates reliability from distribution entropy, and fuses candidates according to query-specific semantic, lexical, and granularity confidence. We further introduce UMGP-RAG, a parent promotion variant that uses fine-grained hits to locate relevant evidence while returning broader non-redundant parent chunks for local coherence. Experiments on question answering benchmarks show that uncertainty-aware fusion and parent promotion improve generation quality while maintaining a lightweight, plug-and-play retrieval pipeline.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向长文档RAG的不确定性感知混合检索
检索增强生成（RAG）高度依赖检索证据的质量与粒度。大粒度检索单元虽能保留上下文，但常引入无关内容，稀释答案相关证据并损害长上下文利用效果；细粒度单元更紧凑，但因短片段缺乏语义、词汇或衔接线索，难以可靠匹配查询。本文提出不确定性感知多粒度RAG（UMG-RAG），一种无需训练的混合检索框架，将片段粒度视为查询特定的可靠性估计。无需训练新检索器或修改生成器，UMG-RAG利用现有稠密与稀疏检索器作为多粒度互补专家。对每个查询，将各专家粒度得分列表转化为证据分布，从分布熵估计可靠性，并依据查询特定的语义、词汇及粒度置信度融合候选结果。进一步提出UMGP-RAG变体，利用细粒度命中定位相关证据，同时返回更宽泛的非冗余父级片段以保障局部连贯性。在问答基准测试中，不确定性感知融合与父级提升显著提升生成质量，同时保持轻量、即插即用的检索管道。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the trade-off between context preservation and relevance in long-document retrieval, this paper proposes UMG-RAG, a training-free hybrid retrieval framework that dynamically adjusts chunk granularity based on query-specific uncertainty. It leverages existing dense and sparse retrievers across multiple granularities, converts their scores into evidence distributions, and fuses results using reliability estimates derived from entropy and query-specific confidence metrics. Experiments demonstrate that this uncertainty-aware fusion, along with the parent promotion variant UMGP-RAG, improves generation quality on QA benchmarks without requiring retraining or modifying the generator, while maintaining a lightweight, plug-and-play architecture.
为解决长文档检索中上下文保留与相关性之间的权衡问题，本文提出UMG-RAG，一种无需训练的混合检索框架，根据查询特定不确定性动态调整分块粒度。该框架利用现有密集和稀疏检索器在不同粒度下的检索结果，将其评分转化为证据分布，并通过熵值和查询特定置信度指标融合结果。实验表明，该不确定性感知融合机制及其父级推广变体UMGP-RAG在问答基准上显著提升生成质量，同时保持轻量级、即插即用的架构，无需重新训练或修改生成器。
Lyapunov-Based PI-Like Control for Robust Trajectory Tracking of a Four-Wheel Independently Driven and Steered Robot: Design and Experimental Validation
Authors: Branimir Ćaran, Vladimir Milić, Marko Švaco, Bojan Jerbić
First: 2026-02-17T08:36:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:29:10+00:00
Comments: This work has been submitted to the IEEE for possible publication
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this paper, a Lyapunov-based synthesis of a PI-like controller is proposed for robust trajectory tracking of an independently driven and steered four-wheel mobile robot. For the robot considered in this work, an explicit structurally verified mathematical model is used to enable systematic controller design with rigorous stability guarantees suitable for real time implementation. An augmented Lyapunov-based practical stability analysis is developed for the combined velocity-error and integral-error dynamics of the inner loop, yielding explicit bounds and sufficient conditions for practical stability and uniform ultimate boundedness of the combined velocity-error and integral-error state. The resulting control law retains a PI-like structure with model-based feedforward compensation, making it suitable for implementation on standard embedded platforms while improving robustness against configuration dependent residual dynamics and unmodelled effects. The effectiveness and robustness of the proposed design are demonstrated experimentally on a four-wheel independently steered and independently driven mobile robot platform, under both horizontal and vertical operating conditions and benchmarked against a PI controller and a sliding-mode controller.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于李雅普诺夫的PI类控制用于四轮独立驱动与转向机器人鲁棒轨迹跟踪：设计与实验验证
本文提出一种基于李雅普诺夫的PI类控制器综合方法，用于独立驱动与转向四轮移动机器人的鲁棒轨迹跟踪。针对本文所考虑的机器人，采用显式结构验证的数学模型，以实现系统化控制器设计并提供严格的稳定性保证，适用于实时实现。针对内环的组合速度误差与积分误差动力学，开发了一种扩展的李雅普诺夫实用稳定性分析，得出组合误差状态的实用稳定性和一致最终有界性的显式界限与充分条件。所得到的控制律保留PI类结构并结合基于模型的前馈补偿，使其适用于标准嵌入式平台，同时增强对配置依赖残余动力学和未建模效应的鲁棒性。该设计的有效性与鲁棒性已在四轮独立转向与驱动移动机器人平台上通过实验验证，涵盖水平与垂直操作条件，并与PI控制器和滑模控制器进行对比。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robust trajectory tracking in four-wheel independently driven and steered robots, this paper proposes a Lyapunov-based PI-like controller with model-based feedforward compensation. The controller is designed using an explicitly verified mathematical model to ensure stability and real-time feasibility, with an augmented Lyapunov analysis providing explicit bounds for practical stability and uniform ultimate boundedness of velocity and integral errors. Experimental validation on a physical robot platform under both horizontal and vertical conditions demonstrates superior performance compared to both a standard PI controller and a sliding-mode controller, confirming enhanced robustness against unmodeled dynamics and configuration-dependent residuals.
为实现四轮独立驱动与转向机器人在复杂环境下的鲁棒轨迹跟踪，本文提出一种基于李雅普诺夫的PI型控制器，结合模型前馈补偿。控制器基于明确验证的数学模型设计，通过扩展的李雅普诺夫稳定性分析，确保速度误差与积分误差的实用稳定性和一致最终有界性。实验在物理平台上验证，涵盖水平与垂直工况，结果表明该控制器相比标准PI控制器与滑模控制器具有更强鲁棒性，能有效抑制未建模动态与配置相关残差。
Adaptive Turn-Taking for Real-time Multi-Party Voice Agents
Authors: Soumyajit Mitra, Prabhat Pandey, Abhinav Jain, Shanmukha Sahith, K V Vijay Girish
First: 2026-06-11T16:27:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:27:45+00:00
Comments: Accepted for publication at Interspeech 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Turn-taking in multi-party spoken conversations remains a fundamental challenge for voice-based agents, particularly under dynamic floor competition and varying user expectations. We propose ModeratorLM, a role-playing voice agent that conditions turn-taking behavior on an explicitly assigned role in multi-party settings. The system is built on a speech large language model operating in chunk-wise streaming manner. We further introduce a reasoning-augmented variant that incorporates chain-of-thought reasoning over conversational context and the assigned role. We construct RolePlayConv, a large-scale synthetic dataset of spoken multi-party conversations with diverse assistant roles. Experiments on real-world meeting data and RolePlayConv show improved turn-taking precision by over 40% and recall by more than 70%, while substantially reducing false-positive interruptions compared to non-role-conditioned baselines.
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of turn-taking in dynamic multi-party voice conversations, the paper introduces ModeratorLM, a voice agent that adapts its turn-taking behavior based on an explicitly assigned role, using a speech large language model in chunk-wise streaming mode. A reasoning-augmented version enhances performance by applying chain-of-thought reasoning over context and role. Evaluated on real meeting data and a synthetic dataset RolePlayConv, the system achieves over 40% higher turn-taking precision and more than 70% higher recall, while significantly reducing false-positive interruptions compared to non-role-conditioned baselines.
为解决多用户语音对话中动态轮流发言的难题，本文提出ModeratorLM，一种根据明确分配角色调整发言行为的语音代理。该系统基于分块流式语音大语言模型，并引入结合链式推理的角色增强版本，以对话上下文和角色信息指导发言决策。在真实会议数据和合成数据集RolePlayConv上的实验表明，该系统轮流发言精度提升超40%，召回率提升超70%，同时大幅降低误打断率，优于未角色条件化的基线模型。
Emergence of Hierarchical Emotion Organization in Large Language Models
Authors: Maya Okawa, Bo Zhao, Eric J. Bigelow, Rose Yu, Tomer Ullman, Ekdeep Singh Lubana, Hidenori Tanaka
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2025-07-12T15:12:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:27:44+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As large language models (LLMs) increasingly power conversational agents, understanding how they model users' emotional states is critical for ethical deployment. Inspired by emotion wheels, i.e., a psychological framework that argues emotions organize hierarchically, we analyze probabilistic dependencies between emotional states in model outputs. We find that LLMs naturally form hierarchical emotion trees that align with human psychological models, and larger models develop more complex hierarchies. We also uncover systematic biases in emotion recognition across socioeconomic personas, with compounding misclassifications for intersectional, underrepresented groups. Human studies reveal striking parallels, suggesting that LLMs internalize aspects of social perception. Beyond highlighting emergent emotional reasoning in LLMs, our results hint at the potential of using cognitively-grounded theories for developing better model evaluations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大型语言模型中层级情绪组织的涌现
随着大型语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地驱动对话代理，理解它们如何建模用户的情绪状态对于伦理部署至关重要。受情绪轮（一种心理学框架，主张情绪具有层级组织）启发，我们分析模型输出中情绪状态间的概率依赖关系。我们发现LLMs自然形成与人类心理学模型一致的层级情绪树，且模型规模越大，层级越复杂。我们还发现情绪识别在社会经济人格间存在系统性偏差，对交叉性、代表性不足群体的误分类叠加加剧。人类研究揭示显著相似性，表明LLMs内化了部分社会认知。除凸显LLMs中涌现的情绪推理外，我们的结果暗示可借助认知基础理论开发更优模型评估方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how large language models represent human emotions for ethical deployment, this study analyzes probabilistic dependencies in model outputs using a psychological framework inspired by emotion wheels. The method identifies hierarchical emotion trees that emerge naturally in LLMs, with larger models exhibiting more complex structures aligned with human emotion organization. Experimental results show that these hierarchies mirror human psychological models, while also revealing systematic biases in emotion recognition that disproportionately affect intersectional and underrepresented groups, suggesting LLMs internalize social perception patterns.
为理解大型语言模型如何表征情绪状态以实现伦理部署，本研究探讨LLM是否展现出类似人类心理学模型的情绪层级结构。通过对模型输出中情绪状态概率依赖关系的分析，研究人员发现LLM自然形成与情绪轮一致的层级情绪树，且模型规模越大结构越复杂。实验表明，情绪识别在不同社会经济身份群体中存在系统性偏差，尤其影响交叉性边缘群体，人类研究也发现相似现象，暗示LLM内化了社会感知模式。
NetCause: Counterfactual Learning for Root Cause Analysis in Large-Scale Networks
Authors: Fabien Chraim, Jian Zhang, Dominik Janzing, Xiang Song, Christos Faloutsos, John Evans
First: 2026-06-11T16:26:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:26:33+00:00
Comments: 9 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Can a learned model capture how faults propagate through a large-scale network and use this knowledge to causally attribute customer impact to its underlying root cause? Existing root cause analysis techniques often rely on static rules, correlation heuristics, or topology-local reasoning, which struggle to generalize in dynamic environments where faults propagate across complex physical and logical dependencies. We present NetCause, a self-supervised learning-based framework that models network incidents as graph-temporal processes and uses counterfactual simulation to rank candidate root causes. This approach produces an interpretable ranking of root cause hypotheses and integrates naturally with operator-defined mitigation and remediation actions. We train the model on over 1,500 incidents collected over six months from a leading cloud provider's production network and evaluate it on 31 expert-labeled incidents. NetCause consistently improves root cause ranking quality in the regime most relevant to operational decision-making, achieving a 16.1% accuracy improvement over a rule-based heuristic baseline. While training is computationally intensive, inference is lightweight, requiring only seconds of GPU runtime per incident (well below typical telemetry collection latencies).
中文标题/摘要
标题：NetCause：用于大规模网络根因分析的反事实学习
学习的模型能否捕捉故障在大规模网络中的传播方式，并利用该知识因果地将客户影响归因于其根本原因？现有根因分析技术通常依赖静态规则、相关性启发式或拓扑局部推理，在动态环境中难以泛化，因为故障会跨越复杂的物理和逻辑依赖关系传播。 我们提出了NetCause，一种基于自监督学习的框架，将网络事件建模为图时序过程，并利用反事实模拟对候选根因进行排序。该方法生成可解释的根因假设排序，并自然地与操作员定义的缓解和修复措施集成。 我们在一家领先云服务提供商的生产网络中收集的1500多个事件上训练模型，并在31个专家标注的事件上进行评估。NetCause在最贴近运营决策的场景中持续提升根因排序质量，相比基于规则的启发式基线准确率提升16.1%。虽然训练计算开销大，但推理轻量，每个事件仅需数秒GPU运行时间（远低于典型遥测收集延迟）。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of static and heuristic-based root cause analysis in dynamic, large-scale networks, NetCause introduces a self-supervised learning framework that models network incidents as graph-temporal processes and employs counterfactual simulation to rank root causes. Trained on over 1,500 real-world incidents from a cloud provider’s production network, the model evaluates candidate causes by simulating their absence and measuring the resulting impact on network behavior. Evaluated on 31 expert-labeled incidents, NetCause consistently outperforms a rule-based baseline by 16.1% in root cause ranking accuracy, while maintaining lightweight inference requiring only seconds per incident.
为了解决现有静态规则和启发式方法在动态大规模网络中泛化能力差的问题，NetCause提出一种基于自监督学习的框架，将网络事件建模为图时序过程，并通过反事实模拟对根因候选进行排序。该模型在一家云服务商的生产网络中训练了1500多个真实事件，评估31个专家标注案例，根因排序准确率比规则基线提升16.1%，且推理仅需数秒，远低于典型遥测采集延迟。
When Does Mixing Help? Analyzing Query Embedding Interpolation in Multilingual Dense Retrieval
Authors: Tongyao Zhu, Chao-Ming Huang, Min-Yen Kan
Venue: ACL 2026 Oral
First: 2026-06-11T16:23:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:23:16+00:00
Comments: ACL 2026 Main (Oral)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While mixed-language querying is ubiquitous in multilingual communities, the sensitivity of dense retrievers to such queries remains poorly understood. We present a ratio-controlled study on mMARCO that systematically evaluates retrieval performance by varying the mixing proportion of parallel query translations via embedding-level mixing -- constructing mixed queries as an interpolation of monolingual embeddings. Experiments with BGE-M3 demonstrate that an optimal mixing ratio outperforms the best monolingual endpoint in 88/105 cases. We uncover a distinct asymmetry driven by English dominance: mixing is uniformly beneficial when retrieving from non-English document indices, whereas indices containing English are best served by pure English queries. Furthermore, English acts as the strongest mixing partner for every non-English document language. Finally, when controlling for English dominance, mixing gains correlate negatively with typological distance. We conclude that language-mix sensitivity is structured and predictable, and we validate the robustness of these patterns across model families and scales.
中文标题/摘要
标题：何时混合查询有助于多语言密集检索？分析查询嵌入插值
尽管混合语言查询在多语言社区中普遍存在，但密集检索器对此类查询的敏感性仍不甚明了。我们在mMARCO上开展了一项比例控制研究，通过嵌入层混合系统性评估检索性能——将混合查询构建为单语嵌入的插值。实验表明，BGE-M3在88/105个案例中，最优混合比例优于最佳单语查询。我们发现英语主导导致的显著不对称性：从非英语文档索引检索时，混合查询始终有益；而含英语索引则更适合纯英语查询。此外，英语是所有非英语文档语言最强的混合伙伴。最后，在控制英语主导因素后，混合收益与类型学距离呈负相关。我们得出结论：语言混合敏感性具有结构化和可预测性，并验证了这些模式在不同模型家族和规模中的稳健性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the widespread use of mixed-language queries in multilingual settings but limited understanding of how dense retrievers respond to them, this study systematically evaluates query embedding interpolation on mMARCO by varying the mixing ratio of parallel translations. Using BGE-M3, experiments reveal that an optimal mixing ratio outperforms the best monolingual query in 88 out of 105 cases, with a clear asymmetry driven by English dominance: mixing benefits non-English document indices while English-only queries perform best for English indices. English consistently acts as the strongest mixing partner for all non-English languages, and mixing gains correlate negatively with typological distance when controlling for English influence, indicating that language-mix sensitivity is structured and predictable across model families and scales.
为理解多语言密集检索系统对混合语言查询的敏感性，该研究在mMARCO上通过插值单语查询嵌入并调节混合比例，系统评估检索性能。实验表明，BGE-M3在88/105个案例中，最优混合比例优于最佳单语查询，揭示出不对称性：非英语文档索引普遍受益于混合，而含英语索引则更适合纯英语查询；英语对所有非英语语言均为最强混合伙伴，且在控制英语主导性后，混合收益随语言类型距离增大而降低，表明语言混合敏感性具有结构化、可预测的规律。
RoboNaldo: Accurate, Stable and Powerful Humanoid Soccer Shooting via Motion-Guided Curriculum Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Yichao Zhong, Yidan Lu, Yuhang Lu, Tianyang Tang, Haoguang Mai, Yixuan Pan, Tianyu Li, Li Chen, Jingbo Wang, Zhongyu Li, Peng Lu, Hongyang Li
First: 2026-06-09T16:51:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:22:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Elite humanoid soccer shooting requires whole-body stability, high-impulse whole-body interactions, and accuracy to targets. Motion tracking-driven reinforcement learning (RL) provides stability in whole-body movement coordination, but a fixed reference makes it hard to adapt to varied ball positions and strike timings; in contrast, task reward-driven RL struggles to explore and discover valid kicks from scratch. We therefore introduce RoboNaldo, a three-stage motion-guided curriculum RL framework for high-impulse humanoid interaction. A single human-kick reference is used as a scaffold and progressively shifts optimization towards shooting performance. The curriculum first learns a stable whole-body kicking prior, then adapts the kick to free-kick settings where the ball is stationary at random positions, and finally extends it to moving-ball shooting through a locomotion-command and kick-trigger interface. A high-level heuristic planner controls this interface during training, while alternative high-level controllers can drive the same low-level policy at inference. In simulation, RoboNaldo demonstrates free-kick shot error 48.6% lower and shoot velocity 2.96x than prior work baselines. In real world on a Unitree G1 with onboard perception, RoboNaldo attains 0.73 m and 0.86 m average target shooting error from 3 m away in free-kick and moving-ball cases, accordingly. And the post-contact ball velocity reaches 13.10 m/s, which is 59-71% of reported professional open-play shot speed. Project page: https://opendrivelab.com/RoboNaldo.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RoboNaldo：通过运动引导的课程强化学习实现精准、稳定且强大的人形机器人足球射门
精英级人形机器人足球射门需要全身稳定性、高冲击力的全身交互以及精准命中目标。运动跟踪驱动的强化学习（RL）可提供全身运动协调的稳定性，但固定参考点使其难以适应球的不同位置和击球时机；相比之下，任务奖励驱动的RL难以从零开始探索并发现有效的射门动作。为此，我们提出了RoboNaldo，一种面向高冲击力人形交互的三阶段运动引导课程RL框架。以单个真人踢球动作作为基础参考，逐步将优化目标转向射门性能。课程首先学习稳定的全身踢球先验，然后适应定点任意位置静止球的任意球场景，最后通过运动指令与踢球触发接口扩展至移动球射门。训练期间，高层启发式规划器控制该接口，而在推理阶段，其他高层控制器可驱动相同的低层策略。仿真结果显示，RoboNaldo的任意球射门误差比现有基线方法低48.6%，射门速度提升2.96倍。在搭载视觉感知的Unitree G1机器人真实环境中，RoboNaldo在3米距离下任意球和移动球场景的平均目标误差分别为0.73米和0.86米，触球后球速达13.10米/秒，相当于职业球员开放进攻射门速度的59-71%。项目页面：https://opendrivelab.com/RoboNaldo。
Summary / 总结
RoboNaldo addresses the challenge of accurate and stable humanoid soccer shooting by combining motion-guided curriculum reinforcement learning with task-adaptive training stages. Motivated by the need for whole-body stability and high-impulse interaction under varying ball conditions, the framework uses a single human-kick motion as a scaffold to progressively shift learning toward shooting performance: first stabilizing whole-body kicking, then adapting to stationary free-kick scenarios, and finally handling moving-ball shots via a locomotion-command interface. A heuristic planner guides training, while alternative controllers can operate the same policy at inference. In simulation, RoboNaldo achieved 48.6% lower shot error and 2.96x higher velocity than baselines. On a real Unitree G1 robot, it hit targets with 0.73m and 0.86m average error in free-kick and moving-ball modes, respectively, and generated post-contact ball velocities of 13.10 m/s — 59–71% of professional open-play shot speeds.
为实现稳定、精准且有力的人形机器人足球射门，RoboNaldo提出一种基于运动引导的课程强化学习框架，通过从人类踢球动作参考逐步演化至适应自由球和移动球场景。该框架首先学习稳定的全身踢球先验，再适应静止球自由球场景，最终通过运动指令与踢球触发接口扩展至移动球射门，训练时由启发式规划器控制接口，推理时可替换高层控制器。实验表明，仿真中射门误差降低48.6%，速度提升2.96倍；在Unitree G1机器人实测中，3米外自由球与移动球平均射门误差分别为0.73米和0.86米，触球后球速达13.10米/秒，相当于职业球员开放进攻射门速度的59–71%。
AgentRivet: an automated system for producing Rivet routines from journal publications
Authors: Antonio J. Costa, Caterina Doglioni, Christian Gütschow, Andrew D. Pilkington, Sukanya Sinha
First: 2026-06-11T16:22:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:22:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Particle physics collider experiments provide Rivet routines as part of the analysis preservation strategy for model-independent measurements. Rivet is a C++ toolkit that allow new theoretical models to be compared to the measurements, thus aiding the development and tuning of Monte Carlo event generators as well as searches for physics beyond the Standard Model. However, analysis coverage is known to be incomplete, with only 39% of measurements having documented and publicly available Rivet routines. In this article, we design and implement an automated workflow based on Large Language Models with the goal of providing the missing routines. This multi-step workflow, referred to as AgentRivet, extracts the physics analysis information from published papers and writes the missing Rivet routines, with intermediate code- and physics- reviews as part of an autonomous quality control. We report the results obtained using commercial Large Language Models, provided by OpenAI, Anthropic, and Google, for two recent measurements from the ATLAS and CMS experiments. We find that AgentRivet produces competent Rivet routines with few syntax errors. The physics fidelity of the routines is reasonable and follows the explanations given in the relevant publications. Nevertheless, physics-implementation issues do arise and are investigated using the artefacts produced by AgentRivet. The majority of physics implementation issues arise from subtle-but-ambiguous definitions in the given publication, although some models struggle to implement complex observables even when clear definitions are given.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AgentRivet：一种从期刊论文自动生成Rivet程序的自动化系统
粒子物理对撞机实验作为模型无关测量分析保存策略的一部分，提供Rivet程序。Rivet是一个C++工具包，允许将新理论模型与测量结果进行比较，从而辅助蒙特卡洛事件生成器的开发与调优，以及对标准模型外物理的搜寻。然而，分析覆盖范围已知不完整，仅有39%的测量结果具有文档化且公开可用的Rivet程序。本文设计并实现了一种基于大语言模型的自动化工作流，旨在提供缺失的程序。该多步骤工作流称为AgentRivet，从已发表论文中提取物理分析信息并编写缺失的Rivet程序，中间包含代码和物理层面的审查，作为自主质量控制的一部分。我们使用OpenAI、Anthropic和Google提供的商业大语言模型，对ATLAS和CMS实验的两项近期测量结果进行了测试。结果表明，AgentRivet生成的Rivet程序能力较强，语法错误较少，物理保真度合理，符合相关论文的说明。然而，仍存在物理实现问题，我们通过分析AgentRivet生成的产物进行研究。大部分物理实现问题源于论文中微妙但含糊的定义，尽管部分模型即使在定义明确的情况下，仍难以实现复杂可观测量。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the incomplete coverage of Rivet routines—only 39% of collider measurements have documented ones—this work introduces AgentRivet, an automated system using Large Language Models to generate missing Rivet routines from journal publications. The method involves extracting physics analysis details from papers and synthesizing corresponding C++ code, with intermediate reviews for quality control. Experiments using commercial LLMs from OpenAI, Anthropic, and Google on two recent ATLAS and CMS measurements show that AgentRivet produces syntactically correct routines with reasonable physics fidelity, matching published explanations. However, physics implementation errors occur, often due to ambiguous definitions in the source papers, even when definitions are clear, indicating limitations in handling complex observables.
鉴于仅有39%的强子对撞机测量结果配有文档化的Rivet程序，本研究提出AgentRivet系统，利用大语言模型自动从学术论文中提取物理分析信息并生成缺失的Rivet程序，辅以中间代码与物理审查实现质量控制。在对ATLAS和CMS两个近期测量结果的实验中，使用OpenAI、Anthropic和Google的商用大模型，系统生成的程序语法错误较少，物理实现基本符合论文描述。但仍有物理实现问题，主要源于原文中隐含模糊的定义，即使定义明确，部分模型仍难以处理复杂可观测量，表明系统在复杂物理实现方面仍有局限。
Graphical Causal Reasoning for Root Cause Analysis in Cloud Networks
Authors: Fabien Chraim, Dominik Janzing, John Evans
First: 2026-06-11T16:20:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:20:51+00:00
Comments: 6 pages, 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Cloud-computing relies on large-scale networks which are inherently complex systems. In this paper, we present a novel approach to root cause analysis (RCA) of cloud network incidents, leveraging graph-based causal discovery techniques. Our method addresses the limitations of rule-based automation by introducing a spatiotemporal grouping strategy and an automation ontology to reduce the dimensionality of the problem. We construct a causal graph from binary time series data using bivariate Granger causality and conditional independence tests. For inference, we introduce a probabilistic method that assigns edge-specific conditional probabilities as a function of time lag, allowing for interpretable, time-aware root cause scoring via causal graph traversal. We evaluated the system using a labeled dataset of 35 production incidents from a major cloud provider. The model successfully recalled the correct root cause in 85.7% of incidents and produced an exact match in 74.3%. In production, the deployed system has been used in over 800 real-world incidents, with positive qualitative feedback from network engineers. These results highlight the practicality of a data-driven, causal approach to RCA in dynamic and large-scale operational environments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：云网络中基于图的因果推理根因分析方法
云计算依赖于大规模网络，这些网络本质上是复杂系统。本文提出了一种新颖的云网络事件根因分析（RCA）方法，利用基于图的因果发现技术。该方法通过引入时空分组策略和自动化本体，克服了基于规则的自动化方法的局限性，降低了问题维度。我们利用双变量格兰杰因果关系和条件独立性检验，从二进制时间序列数据构建因果图。在推理阶段，我们引入一种概率方法，将边特定的条件概率作为时间滞后的函数，从而通过因果图遍历实现可解释且时间感知的根因评分。我们使用某大型云服务商提供的35个标注生产事件数据集对系统进行评估，模型在85.7%的事件中成功召回正确根因，在74.3%的事件中实现精确匹配。在生产环境中，该系统已应用于800多个真实事件，获得网络工程师的积极反馈。这些结果突显了数据驱动、因果导向方法在动态、大规模运营环境中的实用性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the complexity of cloud networks and the limitations of rule-based root cause analysis, this paper introduces a graph-based causal reasoning method that uses spatiotemporal grouping and an automation ontology to reduce problem dimensionality. It constructs causal graphs from binary time series using Granger causality and conditional independence tests, then applies a probabilistic inference model that assigns time-lagged conditional probabilities to edges for interpretable root cause scoring. Evaluated on 35 labeled production incidents, the system recalled the correct root cause in 85.7% of cases and achieved exact matches in 74.3%, with real-world deployment in over 800 incidents receiving positive feedback from engineers.
为应对云网络固有的复杂性，本文提出一种基于图的因果推理方法用于根因分析，通过引入时空分组策略和自动化本体，克服规则系统局限并降低问题维度。该方法利用格兰杰因果和条件独立性检验从二进制时序数据构建因果图，并采用概率推理模型，为图中边分配依赖时滞的条件概率，实现可解释的、时间感知的根因评分。在35个标注的生产事故数据集上，系统正确召回根因比例达85.7%，精确匹配率达74.3%，并在800多个真实事故中部署，获得网络工程师正面反馈。
Ride, Track, and Recover: Pilot Randomized Trial of a Wearable Digital Self-Management Intervention During a Veteran Endurance-Cycling Program
Authors: Alan Ta, Nilsu Salgin, Caleb Armstrong, Kala Phillips Reindel, Farzan Sasangohar
First: 2026-06-11T16:18:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:18:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Post-traumatic stress disorder (PTSD) in veterans is characterized by persistent hyperarousal and comorbid anxiety and depressive symptoms that are difficult to monitor and manage outside clinical settings. Thirteen veterans participating in a Project Hero cycling event in Texas were randomized by computer-generated sequence in a naturalistic setting to two arms: (1) digital intervention plus physical activity, or (2) physical activity only, plus a third at-home monitoring control cohort consisting of 7 veterans selected from the broader Project Hero veteran community. Continuous smartwatch sensing combined heart rate and accelerometer features to detect hyperarousal events, which were confirmed in real time by participants. Weekly self-report measures of anxiety, depression, and PTSD severity were collected. Generalized additive mixed models characterized nonlinear trajectories over time. Baseline-normalized hyperarousal trajectories differed significantly across conditions, with the digital intervention group (n=7) showing structured stabilization compared to late-study escalation in the physical-only group (n=3). Both cycling groups exhibited acute symptom improvements during the endurance event; however, the digital intervention group demonstrated a higher overall maintenance of gains. The at-home control group (n=4) showed gradual symptom declines. Perceived precision of ML detections varied substantially across individuals and was positively associated with symptom severity, with higher-severity participants confirming a greater proportion of detected events. These results suggest that coupling wearable detection with digital self-management tools may support stabilization of hyperarousal and symptom improvement while emphasizing the importance of personalization and human-centered design in wearable mental health systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：骑行、追踪与恢复：一项针对退伍军人耐力骑行项目的可穿戴数字自我管理干预的随机对照试验
退伍军人创伤后应激障碍（PTSD）表现为持续性高唤醒状态及伴随焦虑和抑郁症状，难以在临床环境外监测和管理。十三名参与德克萨斯州“英雄项目”骑行活动的退伍军人在自然环境中通过计算机生成序列随机分配至两组：（1）数字干预加体育活动，或（2）仅体育活动，另设一组七名退伍军人作为居家监测对照组，从更广泛的“英雄项目”退伍军人社区中选取。智能手表持续监测结合心率与加速度计功能，用于检测高唤醒事件，参与者实时确认。每周收集焦虑、抑郁及PTSD严重程度的自我报告数据。广义加性混合模型刻画随时间变化的非线性轨迹。基线标准化的高唤醒轨迹在各组间显著不同，数字干预组（n=7）表现出结构化稳定，而仅体育活动组（n=3）在研究后期症状加剧。两组骑行者在耐力骑行期间均出现急性症状改善，但数字干预组整体维持效果更佳。居家对照组（n=4）症状呈渐进性下降。机器学习检测的精确度因个体差异显著，且与症状严重程度正相关，症状更严重者更频繁确认检测到的事件。结果表明，将可穿戴设备检测与数字自我管理工具结合，可能有助于稳定高唤醒状态和改善症状，同时强调可穿戴心理健康系统中个性化与以人为本设计的重要性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of monitoring and managing PTSD-related hyperarousal and comorbid symptoms outside clinical settings, the study randomized 13 veterans into two intervention arms during a cycling event: one received a digital self-management intervention paired with physical activity, while the other received physical activity alone; a third control group received at-home monitoring. Using smartwatch sensors to detect hyperarousal events confirmed by participants, and collecting weekly self-reports, the study applied generalized additive mixed models to track symptom trajectories. Results showed that the digital intervention group exhibited structured stabilization of hyperarousal over time, contrasting with late-study symptom escalation in the physical-only group and gradual decline in the control group. The digital group also maintained symptom improvements better than the others, and participant confidence in machine learning detections correlated positively with symptom severity, underscoring the need for personalized, human-centered wearable mental health design.
本研究旨在评估可穿戴设备结合数字自我管理干预是否有助于退伍军人在耐力骑行活动中管理创伤后应激障碍相关的过度警觉。13名退伍军人被随机分配至两组——一组接受数字干预加骑行，另一组仅骑行——及一组居家监测对照组。智能手表持续监测心率与运动数据以识别过度警觉，由参与者实时确认，同时每周收集自我报告症状评分。广义加性混合模型分析显示，数字干预组在过度警觉轨迹上呈现结构化稳定，而仅骑行组症状在后期恶化。两组活动组在活动期间均出现急性症状改善，但仅数字干预组能维持疗效。对照组症状呈渐进性下降。值得注意的是，症状严重者更常确认检测到的过度警觉事件，凸显个性化与以人为本设计在可穿戴心理健康工具中的重要性。
What's Old is New Again: Classical Dimensionality Reduction for Efficient Saliency-Guided Biometric Attack Detection
Authors: Samuel Webster, Walter Scheirer
First: 2026-06-11T16:18:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:18:06+00:00
Comments: 16 pages (8 main, 2 references, 6 appendix), 4 figures (3 main, 1 appendix), 13 tables (3 main, 10 appendix)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Saliency-guided training is a paradigm in visual recognition that encourages models to focus on the most relevant image regions during learning. While its application in biometric presentation attack detection (PAD) has shown strong benefits in robustness and generalization, adoption is often limited by the high cost, domain specificity, and limited scalability of existing saliency acquisition methods, such as human annotations over a limited dataset. We present a novel, cost-efficient, and highly-scalable approach to saliency acquisition using maps inspired by classical dimensionality reduction techniques: PCA and LDA. Our proposed methods generate saliency maps directly from raw training data, requiring no human annotation nor domain knowledge. We contextualize the effectiveness of these saliency sources in three saliency-explored domains (iris PAD, synthetic face detection, fingerprint PAD) and demonstrate its scalability in two saliency-novel domains (fingerprint vein PAD and ID card PAD). Across all domains tested, models trained using dimensionality reduction-sourced saliency maps exceed baseline and sometimes SOTA saliency methods without any resource investment or domain-specific tooling. Our findings overcome an important yet unaddressed barrier to saliency-guided training for biometric attack detection and beyond.
中文标题/摘要
标题：旧瓶新酒：经典降维技术用于高效显著性引导的生物特征攻击检测
显著性引导训练是视觉识别中的一个范式，旨在鼓励模型在学习过程中聚焦于图像中最相关的区域。尽管其在生物特征呈现攻击检测（PAD）中的应用已显示出在鲁棒性和泛化能力方面的显著优势，但其采用常受限于现有显著性获取方法（如有限数据集上的人工标注）的高成本、领域特异性及有限可扩展性。本文提出了一种新颖、低成本且高度可扩展的显著性获取方法，该方法借鉴经典降维技术（PCA和LDA）生成显著性图。所提方法可直接从原始训练数据生成显著性图，无需人工标注或领域知识。我们在三个已探索显著性的领域（虹膜PAD、合成人脸检测、指纹PAD）中评估了这些显著性源的有效性，并在两个新领域（指纹静脉PAD、身份证PAD）中验证其可扩展性。在所有测试领域中，使用降维源显著性图训练的模型均超越基线方法，有时甚至优于当前最先进（SOTA）的显著性方法，且无需任何资源投入或领域特定工具。我们的研究结果克服了生物特征攻击检测中显著性引导训练领域一个重要而未被解决的障碍。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high cost and limited scalability of traditional saliency acquisition methods in biometric attack detection, this work proposes using classical dimensionality reduction techniques—PCA and LDA—to generate saliency maps directly from raw training data without human annotation or domain expertise. The method is evaluated across five biometric domains, including iris, face, fingerprint, fingerprint vein, and ID card PAD, demonstrating that models trained with these automatically derived saliency maps consistently outperform baseline and state-of-the-art saliency methods while requiring no additional resources or specialized tools. The results highlight the scalability and effectiveness of classical dimensionality reduction for saliency-guided training in biometric security applications.
为解决传统生物特征攻击检测中获取显著性图成本高、可扩展性差的问题，本文提出利用经典降维方法PCA和LDA直接从原始训练数据生成显著性图，无需人工标注或领域知识。该方法在三个成熟领域（虹膜PAD、合成人脸检测、指纹PAD）和两个新领域（指纹静脉PAD、身份证PAD）中验证，结果表明使用这些自动生成显著性图训练的模型在性能上持续优于基线方法，有时甚至超越当前最先进方法，且无需额外资源或专用工具。
Improving Pre-trained Adult Glioma Segmentation Models Using only Post-processing Techniques
Authors: Abhijeet Parida, Daniel Capellán-Martín, Zhifan Jiang, Nishad Kulkarni, Krithika Iyer, Austin Tapp, Syed Muhammad Anwar, María J. Ledesma-Carbayo, Marius George Linguraru
First: 2025-12-16T22:01:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:10:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Gliomas are the most common malignant brain tumors in adults and are among the most lethal. Despite aggressive treatment, the median survival rate is less than 15 months. Accurate multiparametric MRI (mpMRI) tumor segmentation is critical for surgical planning, radiotherapy, and disease monitoring. While deep learning models have improved the accuracy of automated segmentation, large-scale pre-trained models generalize poorly and often underperform, producing systematic errors such as false positives, label swaps, and slice discontinuities in slices. These limitations are further compounded by unequal access to GPU resources and the growing environmental cost of large-scale model training. In this work, we propose adaptive post-processing techniques to refine the quality of glioma segmentations produced by large-scale pretrained models developed for various types of tumors. We demonstrated the techniques in multiple BraTS 2025 segmentation challenge tasks, with the ranking metric improving by 14.9 % for the sub-Saharan Africa challenge and 0.9% for the adult glioma challenge. This approach promotes a shift in brain tumor segmentation research from increasingly complex model architectures to efficient, clinically aligned post-processing strategies that are precise, computationally fair, and sustainable.

标题：仅使用后处理技术改进预训练成人胶质瘤分割模型
胶质瘤是成人中最常见的恶性脑肿瘤，也是最致命的肿瘤之一。尽管治疗积极，中位生存期仍不足15个月。准确的多参数MRI（mpMRI）肿瘤分割对术前规划、放疗和疾病监测至关重要。虽然深度学习模型提升了自动化分割的准确性，但大规模预训练模型泛化能力差，常表现不佳，产生系统性错误，如假阳性、标签互换和切片不连续。这些局限性进一步因GPU资源获取不均及大规模模型训练的环境成本而加剧。本文提出自适应后处理技术，以优化各类肿瘤预训练模型生成的胶质瘤分割质量。我们在多个BraTS 2025分割挑战任务中验证了该方法，其中撒哈拉以南非洲挑战赛排名指标提升14.9%，成人胶质瘤挑战赛提升0.9%。该方法推动脑肿瘤分割研究从复杂模型架构转向高效、临床契合的后处理策略，实现精准、计算公平且可持续。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for accurate glioma segmentation in resource-limited settings and the inefficiency of large-scale pre-trained models that often produce systematic errors, this study introduces adaptive post-processing techniques to refine outputs from existing deep learning models. Applied to BraTS 2025 challenges, these methods significantly improved segmentation quality without retraining, boosting ranking metrics by 14.9% in the sub-Saharan Africa challenge and 0.9% in the adult glioma challenge, demonstrating that effective clinical outcomes can be achieved through post-processing rather than model scaling.
鉴于大规模预训练模型在成人胶质瘤分割中泛化能力差且存在系统性错误，本文提出自适应后处理技术以优化分割结果，无需重新训练模型。在BraTS 2025挑战赛中，该方法有效修正了假阳性、标签互换和切片不连续等问题，使排名指标在撒哈拉以南非洲挑战赛中提升14.9%，在成人胶质瘤挑战赛中提升0.9%，表明通过后处理而非模型复杂化即可实现临床适用的精准分割。
Adaptable Segmentation Pipeline for Diverse Brain Tumors with Radiomic-Guided Subtyping and Lesion-Wise Model Ensemble
Authors: Daniel Capellán-Martín, Abhijeet Parida, Zhifan Jiang, Nishad Kulkarni, Krithika Iyer, Austin Tapp, Syed Muhammad Anwar, María J. Ledesma-Carbayo, Marius George Linguraru
Venue: MICCAI
First: 2025-12-16T18:09:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:06:05+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 5 figures, 3 tables. Algorithm presented at MICCAI BraTS 2025
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Robust and generalizable segmentation of brain tumors on multi-parametric magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) remains difficult because tumor types differ widely. The BraTS 2025 Lighthouse Challenge benchmarks segmentation methods on diverse high-quality datasets of adult and pediatric tumors: multi-consortium international pediatric brain tumor segmentation (PED), preoperative meningioma tumor segmentation (MEN), meningioma radiotherapy segmentation (MEN-RT), and segmentation of pre- and post-treatment brain metastases (MET). We present a flexible, modular, and adaptable pipeline that improves segmentation performance by selecting and combining state-of-the-art models and applying tumor- and lesion-specific processing before and after training. Radiomic features extracted from MRI help detect tumor subtype, ensuring a more balanced training. Custom lesion-level performance metrics determine the influence of each model in the ensemble and optimize post-processing that further refines the predictions, enabling the workflow to tailor every step to each case. On the BraTS testing sets, our pipeline achieved performance comparable to top-ranked algorithms across multiple challenges. These findings confirm that custom lesion-aware processing and model selection yield robust segmentations yet without locking the method to a specific network architecture. Our method has the potential for quantitative tumor measurement in clinical practice, supporting diagnosis and prognosis.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于影像组学引导亚型划分与病灶级模型集成的适应性脑肿瘤分割流程
由于肿瘤类型差异显著，多参数磁共振成像（MRI）上脑肿瘤的稳健且可泛化分割仍具挑战。BraTS 2025灯塔挑战赛在成人及儿童肿瘤的多样化高质量数据集上评估分割方法，包括多联盟国际儿童脑肿瘤分割（PED）、术前脑膜瘤分割（MEN）、脑膜瘤放疗分割（MEN-RT）及术前术后脑转移瘤分割（MET）。我们提出一种灵活、模块化、可适应的流程，通过选择并组合前沿模型，结合肿瘤与病灶特异性预处理与后处理，提升分割性能。从MRI中提取的影像组学特征有助于识别肿瘤亚型，确保训练更均衡。自定义病灶级性能指标决定各模型在集成中的权重，并优化后处理以进一步精炼预测，使流程能针对每个病例自适应调整每一步。在BraTS测试集上，本流程性能与多个挑战赛中排名前列的算法相当。结果证实，自定义病灶感知处理与模型选择可实现稳健分割，且不依赖特定网络架构。该方法有望在临床实践中实现肿瘤定量测量，支持诊断与预后。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of segmenting diverse brain tumors across multi-parametric MRI datasets, this study introduces a flexible, modular pipeline that adapts to tumor type and lesion characteristics by combining state-of-the-art models and applying tumor-specific preprocessing and postprocessing. Radiomic features guide tumor subtyping to balance training data, while lesion-level metrics dynamically weight model contributions in an ensemble. Experimental results on BraTS 2025 challenge datasets—including pediatric, meningioma, and metastatic tumors—show the pipeline achieves performance comparable to top-ranked methods, demonstrating robust segmentation without reliance on a fixed architecture, with potential for clinical quantitative tumor measurement.
为应对多参数MRI中不同类型脑肿瘤分割的挑战，该研究提出一种模块化可调管道，利用影像组学特征识别肿瘤亚型，并通过病变级模型集成选择最优模型组合。该方法对每个病变进行独立预处理与后处理，根据自定义性能指标动态调整模型权重并优化预测结果。在BraTS 2025多个挑战数据集（含儿童、脑膜瘤及转移瘤）上，该方法分割性能与顶尖算法相当，验证了其在不依赖特定网络架构前提下对多种肿瘤类型具有鲁棒性。
MaskWAM: Unifying Mask Prompting and Prediction for World-Action Models
Authors: Hanyang Yu, Haitao Lin, Jingbo Zhang, Wenyao Zhang, Chenghao Gu, Heng Li, Ping Tan
First: 2026-06-11T16:02:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:02:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
World Action Models (WAMs) present a promising paradigm for robotic control via video prediction. However, current WAMs suffer from fundamental spatial bottlenecks: standard text inputs introduce referential ambiguity in cluttered scenes, while unstructured RGB predictions lack semantic grounding and remain biased by task-irrelevant backgrounds. To overcome these limitations, we introduce MaskWAM, an object-centric world-action model. By jointly integrating masks as both explicit inputs and predictions via a unified Mixture of Transformers (MoT), MaskWAM unlocks robust policy generalization. This design provides two key benefits: (1) predicting future masks yields object-centric semantic supervision that suppresses visual noise, significantly enhancing even standard text-conditioned WAMs; and (2) coupling this predictive supervision with first-frame visual prompts, such as target object masks, establishes a precise spatial anchor that substantially reduces language ambiguity. Crucially, as WAMs are inherently vision-driven architectures, direct mask conditioning yields substantially stronger guidance than text alone, establishing a precise and robust paradigm for manipulating unseen objects. Evaluations on LIBERO, RoboTwin, and real-world tasks demonstrate that MaskWAM significantly outperforms baselines in both language-clear and language-ambiguous tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MaskWAM：统一掩码提示与预测的世界动作模型
世界动作模型（WAMs）为通过视频预测实现机器人控制提供了一种有前景的范式。然而，当前WAMs存在根本性的空间瓶颈：标准文本输入在杂乱场景中引入指代歧义，而无结构化的RGB预测缺乏语义锚定，且受任务无关背景干扰。为克服这些限制，我们提出MaskWAM，一种以物体为中心的世界动作模型。通过统一的混合Transformer（MoT）将掩码同时作为显式输入和预测，MaskWAM实现了稳健的策略泛化。该设计带来两大优势：（1）预测未来掩码可提供以物体为中心的语义监督，抑制视觉噪声，显著增强甚至标准文本条件下的WAMs；（2）将该预测监督与首帧视觉提示（如目标物体掩码）结合，建立精确的空间锚点，大幅降低语言歧义。关键在于，WAMs本质上是视觉驱动架构，直接掩码条件化比纯文本提供更强的引导，建立了一种精确且稳健的操作未知物体的范式。在LIBERO、RoboTwin及真实世界任务上的评估表明，MaskWAM在语言清晰与语言模糊任务中均显著优于基线方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the spatial ambiguities and semantic lack in current World Action Models, MaskWAM introduces a unified object-centric framework that jointly uses masks as both input prompts and output predictions via a Mixture of Transformers. This design enhances policy generalization by providing semantic grounding through mask prediction, which suppresses visual noise and improves even text-conditioned models, while first-frame masks act as spatial anchors to resolve language ambiguity. Experimental results across LIBERO, RoboTwin, and real-world tasks show MaskWAM consistently outperforms baselines in both clear and ambiguous language settings, demonstrating that direct mask conditioning yields stronger and more robust control than text alone.
为解决当前世界动作模型在复杂场景中因文本输入导致的空间歧义和RGB预测缺乏语义引导的问题，MaskWAM提出一种以对象为中心的统一框架，通过混合Transformer结构将掩码同时作为输入与预测。该设计通过掩码预测提供语义监督，有效抑制视觉噪声并提升标准文本条件模型性能，同时结合首帧视觉提示（如目标对象掩码）建立精确空间锚点，显著降低语言歧义。在LIBERO、RoboTwin及真实任务中的实验表明，MaskWAM在语言清晰与模糊场景下均显著优于基线方法，证明直接掩码引导比纯文本更有效、更稳健。
Bernstein-Schur Kernels: Random Features by Sketched Modulation and Radial Randomization
Authors: Taha Bouhsine
First: 2026-06-08T21:59:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:01:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Bernstein--Schur kernels are products of a finite-feature kernel and a completely monotone shift-invariant kernel: nonstationary kernels falling between the shift-invariant and dot-product templates random features exploit, so neither Bochner sampling nor polynomial sketching applies to the full kernel directly. We give one random-feature construction for the whole class that randomizes both factors: it sketches the finite modulation and samples the radial factor's one-dimensional Bernstein--Widder scale before applying Gaussian random Fourier features, giving feature dimension $Dm$, free of the $O(d^2)$ size of the exact modulation feature. With the modulation kept exact (the $m\to\infty$ limit), we prove unbiasedness, an exact variance, and a matrix-Bernstein operator-norm bound controlled by the top kernel and modulation eigenvalues and an intrinsic dimension rather than the crude $N\max_{ij}$ route. Whitening this argument at the ridge makes the effective dimension $d_{\mathrm{eff}}(λ)$ the \emph{exact} intrinsic dimension of the matrix variance, so $O((1+\|P\|_{\mathrm{op}}/λ)\log(d_{\mathrm{eff}}/δ))$ radial draws preserve the kernel-ridge solution; tilting the draw by a closed-form whitened leverage improves this to the effective-dimension count $O((1+d_{\mathrm{eff}})\log(d_{\mathrm{eff}}/δ))$. Conditioning on the sketch carries every guarantee to the deployed doubly-randomized estimator up to one additive sketch term, and all hold for the whole class with the modulation Gram in place of the polynomial one. The flagship instance is the biased $yat$-kernel $k_{yat,b}(w,x)=(w^\top x+b)^2/(\|w-x\|^2+\varepsilon)$, whose family span contains the inverse-multiquadric kernel by finite differences in $b$.
中文标题/摘要
标题：伯恩斯坦-舒尔核：基于草图调制与径向随机化的随机特征
伯恩斯坦-舒尔核是有限特征核与完全单调平移不变核的乘积：非平稳核介于平移不变与点积模板随机特征之间，因此直接对完整核无法应用博赫纳采样或多项式草图化。我们提出一种随机特征构造方法，对两个因子均进行随机化：先对有限调制进行草图化，再对径向因子的一维伯恩斯坦-威德德尺度进行采样，最后应用高斯随机傅里叶特征，得到特征维度为Dm，摆脱了精确调制特征的O(d²)规模。当调制保持精确（即m→∞极限）时，我们证明了无偏性、精确方差及由核与调制特征值和内在维度控制的矩阵伯恩斯坦算子范数界，而非粗略的Nmax_{ij}路径。在岭估计中对论证进行白化，使有效维度d_eff(λ)成为矩阵方差的精确内在维度，因此O((1+||P||_{op}/λ)log(d_eff/δ))个径向采样即可保留核岭解；通过闭式白化杠杆倾斜采样，可进一步优化至有效维度计数O((1+d_eff)log(d_eff/δ))。对草图进行条件化后，所有保证均传递至部署的双重随机化估计量，仅增加一个附加草图项，且对整个类均成立，只需用调制格拉姆矩阵替代多项式格拉姆矩阵。典型实例为偏置yat核k_{yat,b}(w,x)=(w^⊤x+b)²/(||w-x||²+ε)，其函数空间包含通过b的有限差分得到的逆多二次核。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to approximate nonstationary Bernstein-Schur kernels that cannot be handled by standard random feature methods, the paper introduces a novel random feature construction that simultaneously sketches the finite modulation component and samples the radial Bernstein-Widder scale before applying Gaussian random Fourier features. This yields a feature dimension of Dm, avoiding the O(d²) complexity of exact modulation. Theoretical guarantees include unbiasedness, exact variance, and a matrix-Bernstein operator-norm bound dependent on kernel and modulation eigenvalues and intrinsic dimension. Whitening via ridge regularization reveals the effective dimension d_eff(λ) as the exact intrinsic dimension, enabling efficient radial sampling with O((1+||P||_op/λ)log(d_eff/δ)) draws to preserve kernel-ridge solutions, and further improvement via whitened leverage to O((1+d_eff)log(d_eff/δ)). All guarantees extend to the full kernel class using the modulation Gram matrix, with the biased yat-kernel serving as a key example whose family spans the inverse-multiquadric kernel via finite-difference modulation.
为逼近介于移不变与点积核之间的非平稳Bernstein-Schur核，该文提出一种随机特征方法，先对有限调制进行草图采样，再对径向Bernstein-Widder尺度进行一维采样，最后应用高斯随机傅里叶特征，实现Dm维特征空间而避免O(d²)的精确调制开销。该方法证明了无偏性，并提供依赖核与调制特征值及内在维度的矩阵Bernstein算子范数界，而非粗略的Nmax路径。通过岭正则化白化，有效维度d_eff(λ)成为矩阵方差的精确内在维度，使径向采样量降至O((1+||P||_op/λ)log(d_eff/δ))以保留核岭解，而白化杠杆倾斜可进一步降至O((1+d_eff)log(d_eff/δ))。所有保证可扩展至部署的双重随机化估计器，仅增加一个草图项，且适用于整个类，以调制Gram矩阵替代多项式形式。典型实例为偏置yat核k_yat,b(w,x)=(w^Tx+b)²/(||w-x||²+ε)，其家族通过b的有限差分可涵盖逆多二次核。
CloudCons: A Comprehensive End-to-End Benchmark for Cloud Resource Consolidation
Authors: Xiaobin Zhang, Lefei Shen, Mouxiang Chen, Zhuo Li, Hongkai Li, Han Fu, Jianling Sun, Xiaoxue Ren, Chenghao Liu
Venue: KDD 2026
First: 2026-06-11T16:00:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:00:51+00:00
Comments: Accepted to KDD 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Driven by conservative over-provisioning to guarantee service reliability, resource utilization in cloud data centers remains at low levels. To mitigate this, the forecast-then-optimize paradigm has emerged to optimize consolidation by anticipating future demands. While emerging time series foundation models promise to enhance this paradigm through zero-shot generalization, existing benchmarks focus solely on prediction error metrics. The actual decision utility of these advanced models remains unverified, rendering their practical value for downstream tasks uncertain. To bridge this gap, we propose CloudCons, a comprehensive end-to-end benchmark designed to evaluate forecasting models within the specific context of cloud resource consolidation. We build high-quality datasets that cover diverse workloads from Huawei Cloud, Microsoft Azure, and Google Borg, capturing distinct service characteristics ranging from synchronized diurnal rhythms to stochastic, pulse-like bursts and high-frequency noise. We conduct an extensive evaluation of statistical, deep learning, and foundation models. Our experiments reveal a pivotal finding: while foundation models demonstrate superior zero-shot forecasting accuracy, this advantage does not inherently translate into better decision utility. Of practical significance, we systematically analyze how the selection of predictive quantiles acts as a critical lever. We provide actionable guidelines for calibrating these selections to balance the trade-off between resource efficiency and service reliability, offering vital insights for real-world deployment decisions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CloudCons：云资源整合的端到端综合基准
为保障服务可靠性，云数据中心普遍采用保守的资源过量配置，导致资源利用率偏低。为缓解此问题，预测-优化范式应运而生，通过预判未来需求来优化资源整合。尽管新兴时间序列基础模型有望通过零样本泛化能力提升该范式，但现有基准仅关注预测误差指标，未能验证这些先进模型的实际决策效用，其对下游任务的实际价值尚不明确。为弥合这一差距，我们提出CloudCons，一个专为评估云资源整合场景下预测模型而设计的端到端综合基准。我们构建了覆盖华为云、微软Azure和谷歌Borg等多样化工作负载的高质量数据集，涵盖从同步日周期到随机脉冲式突发及高频噪声等不同服务特征。我们对统计模型、深度学习模型和基础模型进行了广泛评估。实验揭示关键发现：虽然基础模型在零样本预测精度上表现更优，但这一优势并不必然转化为更好的决策效用。具有实际意义的是，我们系统分析了预测分位数选择作为关键调节杠杆的作用，并提供可操作的校准指南，以平衡资源效率与服务可靠性之间的权衡，为实际部署决策提供重要参考。

It leverages high-quality, diverse datasets from Huawei Cloud, Azure, and Google Borg to simulate real-world workload patterns. The authors demonstrate that predictive quantile selection is a critical factor influencing resource efficiency versus service reliability, providing practical guidelines for deployment.
为解决云数据中心因保守过量配置导致资源利用率低的问题，本文提出CloudCons，一个端到端基准，用于评估预测模型在资源合并决策中的实际效用。该基准基于华为云、Azure和Google Borg的真实工作负载构建，涵盖从同步日周期到脉冲式突发和高频噪声等多种服务模式。实验覆盖统计、深度学习及基础模型，发现虽然基础模型在零样本预测精度上表现优异，但其在资源合并决策中的实际效用并不必然更高。研究进一步指出，预测分位数的选择是关键杠杆，可有效平衡资源效率与服务可靠性，为实际部署提供可操作的指导。
Measurement-Calibrated Multi-Camera Fusion for Vision-Based Indoor Localization
Authors: Mateo Toro Diz, Jonathan Hoss, Noah Klarmann
First: 2026-06-11T15:56:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T15:56:02+00:00
Comments: This paper has been accepted for presentation at the IEEE 22st International Conference on Automation Science and Engineering (CASE 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Indoor vision-based localization systems are affected by detection noise, occlusions, and limited camera coverage, leading to uncertainty at multiple stages of the pipeline. While multi-camera data fusion is widely used to mitigate these issues, it is typically treated as a black-box component and evaluated solely end-to-end, obscuring its mechanistic contributions. To address this gap, this work investigates whether explicitly characterizing single-camera localization errors can be leveraged to calibrate and optimize multi-camera data fusion. We introduce a measurement-calibrated fusion approach that integrates component-wise error quantification, specifically isolating homography calibration, human detection, and motion tracking. A component-wise evaluation is conducted to quantify error contributions from homography calibration, human detection, and motion tracking. Experimental results show that data fusion improves localization accuracy compared to single-camera baselines. While measurement-calibrated fusion provides only limited improvement in absolute accuracy over standard fusion, it substantially reduces trajectory variance and improves motion smoothness, which are critical for applications requiring stable and continuous motion estimates. These results highlight the value of explicit error characterization when designing data fusion strategies for vision-based indoor positioning systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于测量校准的多相机融合用于视觉室内定位
基于视觉的室内定位系统受检测噪声、遮挡和相机覆盖范围有限的影响，在管道的多个阶段产生不确定性。虽然多相机数据融合广泛用于缓解这些问题，但通常被视为黑盒组件，仅以端到端方式评估，掩盖了其机制性贡献。为解决这一问题，本研究探讨是否可通过显式表征单相机定位误差，来校准和优化多相机数据融合。 我们提出了一种测量校准融合方法，整合了分量级误差量化，具体隔离了单应性校准、人体检测和运动跟踪。通过分量级评估量化了单应性校准、人体检测和运动跟踪的误差贡献。 实验结果表明，数据融合相比单相机基线提升了定位精度。尽管测量校准融合在绝对精度上仅提供有限提升，但显著降低了轨迹方差并改善了运动平滑性，这对需要稳定连续运动估计的应用至关重要。这些结果突显了在设计视觉室内定位系统数据融合策略时，显式误差表征的价值。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the challenge of uncertainty in indoor vision-based localization due to detection noise, occlusions, and limited camera coverage. It proposes a measurement-calibrated multi-camera fusion method that explicitly characterizes and isolates errors from homography calibration, human detection, and motion tracking to guide fusion optimization. Experimental results demonstrate that while standard fusion outperforms single-camera methods in absolute accuracy, the measurement-calibrated approach significantly reduces trajectory variance and improves motion smoothness, offering critical stability benefits for applications requiring continuous motion estimates.
该论文针对室内视觉定位中因检测噪声、遮挡和相机覆盖不足导致的不确定性问题，提出一种测量校准的多相机融合方法，通过量化并分离相机标定、人体检测与运动跟踪三个模块的误差。实验表明，标准融合虽优于单相机方法，但测量校准融合显著降低了轨迹方差并提升了运动平滑性，对需要连续稳定运动估计的应用具有关键价值。
Leveraging Audio-LLMs to Filter Speech-to-Speech Training Data
Authors: Qixu Chen, Satoshi Nakamura
First: 2026-06-11T15:55:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T15:55:23+00:00
Comments: Accepted to INTERSPEECH 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large-scale mined corpora provide abundant training data for end-to-end speech-to-speech translation (S2ST) but may contain noise, misalignment, and semantic errors. Filtering noisy data is crucial to maintain robust speech translation performance. We study how to train an audio-language model to make keep/drop decisions on paired speech directly from audio. To obtain reliable supervision without manual labels, we adopt a scalable two-stage Rank-to-Distill strategy. A lightweight ranker generates keep/drop pseudo-labels from noisy speech pairs, then trains an audio large language model to predict keep/drop directly from raw paired speech. The resulting model jointly captures acoustic fidelity and cross-lingual semantic consistency for the selection of speech-conditioned data. Experiments on CVSS-C and SpeechMatrix show consistent improvements over unfiltered training, yielding up to +1.4 ASR-BLEU for end-to-end S2ST.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用音频大语言模型过滤语音到语音训练数据
大规模挖掘语料为端到端语音到语音翻译（S2ST）提供了丰富的训练数据，但可能包含噪声、对齐错误和语义错误。过滤噪声数据对保持稳健的语音翻译性能至关重要。我们研究如何训练一个音频语言模型，直接从音频中对配对语音做出保留或丢弃的决策。为在无需人工标注的情况下获得可靠监督，我们采用可扩展的两阶段Rank-to-Distill策略：轻量级排序器从噪声语音对中生成保留/丢弃伪标签，然后训练音频大语言模型直接从原始配对语音预测保留/丢弃。所得模型联合捕捉声学保真度和跨语言语义一致性，用于选择语音条件数据。在CVSS-C和SpeechMatrix上的实验表明，相比未过滤训练，性能持续提升，端到端S2ST最高提升+1.4 ASR-BLEU。
Summary / 总结
To address the issue of noisy, misaligned, and semantically flawed data in large-scale mined corpora used for end-to-end speech-to-speech translation, this work trains an audio-language model to autonomously decide whether to keep or drop speech pairs based on raw audio. Using a two-stage Rank-to-Distill strategy, a lightweight ranker first generates pseudo-labels from noisy data, which are then used to train an audio large language model to predict keep/drop decisions directly from paired audio inputs. The model effectively captures both acoustic quality and cross-lingual semantic consistency, leading to improved training data selection. Experiments on CVSS-C and SpeechMatrix demonstrate consistent performance gains, with up to +1.4 ASR-BLEU improvement over unfiltered training data.
为解决大规模挖掘语料库中存在噪声、对齐错误和语义错误的问题，该研究提出训练一个音频-语言模型，根据原始音频自主判断是否保留或丢弃语音对。采用两阶段Rank-to-Distill策略，首先由轻量级排序器从噪声数据中生成保留/丢弃的伪标签，再用这些标签训练音频大语言模型直接从配对语音中预测保留或丢弃决策。该模型同时捕捉声学保真度与跨语言语义一致性，以提升语音条件数据的选择质量。在CVSS-C和SpeechMatrix上的实验表明，相比未过滤训练，该方法持续提升性能，最高可提升1.4 ASR-BLEU。
Heterogeneous LiDAR Early Fusion and Learned Re-Ranking Strategy for Robust Long-Term Place Recognition in Unstructured Environments
Authors: Judith Vilella-Cantos, Juan José Cabrera, Mónica Ballesta, David Valiente, Luis Payá
First: 2026-06-11T15:52:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T15:52:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Robust localization in unstructured environments, such as agricultural fields, is a critical challenge for autonomous systems. LiDAR sensors provide detailed 3D information about the environment and are invariant to lighting conditions. For this reason, LiDAR-based place recognition methods have gained significant attention. In this paper, we propose MinkUNeXt-VINE++, a novel approach that combines early fusion of heterogeneous LiDAR data from two sensors (Livox Mid-360 and Velodyne VLP-16) and a learned re-ranking strategy in inference time. This fusion leverages the strengths of each sensor to provide a more comprehensive representation of the environment. Additionally, the re-ranking approach is particularly important in repetitive environments, such as vineyards, as finding true positives is a major challenge. We evaluated our approach using the TEMPO-VINE dataset, which provides heterogeneous LiDAR data in vineyard environments across different phenological stages. Our results demonstrate that MinkUNeXt-VINE++ significantly improves place recognition performance compared to single-sensor approaches and state-of-the-art methods. MinkUNeXt-VINE++ achieves a 20% improvement in the Recall@1 metric compared to single-sensor approaches, and +30% including re-ranking. The code of our method is publicly available for reproduction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：异构激光雷达早期融合与学习重排序策略用于非结构化环境中鲁棒的长期定位
在非结构化环境（如农田）中实现鲁棒定位是自主系统的关键挑战。激光雷达传感器可提供环境的详细3D信息，且不受光照条件影响，因此基于激光雷达的定位方法备受关注。本文提出MinkUNeXt-VINE++，一种结合双传感器（Livox Mid-360与Velodyne VLP-16）异构激光雷达数据早期融合与推理时学习重排序策略的新方法。该融合充分利用各传感器优势，提供更全面的环境表征。此外，重排序策略在重复性环境（如葡萄园）中尤为重要，因真阳性识别是主要挑战。我们在TEMPO-VINE数据集上评估该方法，该数据集提供不同物候期葡萄园中的异构激光雷达数据。结果表明，MinkUNeXt-VINE++相比单传感器方法及当前最先进方法显著提升定位性能。与单传感器方法相比，其Recall@1指标提升20%，结合重排序后提升30%。本方法代码已公开，便于复现。
Summary / 总结
To enhance long-term place recognition in unstructured environments like vineyards, this work introduces MinkUNeXt-VINE++, which fuses early-stage data from two heterogeneous LiDAR sensors—Livox Mid-360 and Velodyne VLP-16—and applies a learned re-ranking strategy during inference. The fusion combines complementary sensor strengths to create richer environmental representations, while the re-ranking addresses the challenge of false positives in repetitive scenes. Evaluated on the TEMPO-VINE dataset across multiple vineyard phenological stages, the method outperforms single-sensor baselines and state-of-the-art approaches, achieving a 20% gain in Recall@1 without re-ranking and a 30% gain with it.
为提升在如葡萄园等非结构化环境中长期定位的鲁棒性，本文提出MinkUNeXt-VINE++方法，通过早期融合Livox Mid-360与Velodyne VLP-16两种异构激光雷达数据，并在推理阶段引入学习型重排序策略。该方法结合两种传感器的优势，构建更全面的环境表征，有效应对重复场景中真阳性检测困难的问题。在涵盖多个物候期的TEMPO-VINE数据集上评估，该方法显著优于单传感器基线及现有最先进方法，无重排序时Recall@1提升20%，启用重排序后提升达30%。
GF-DiT: Scheduling Parallelism for Diffusion Transformer Serving
Authors: Xinwei Qiang, Yifan Hu, Shixuan Sun, Jing Yang, Han Zhao, Chen Chen, Yu Feng, Jingwen Leng, Minyi Guo
First: 2026-06-11T15:51:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T15:51:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) have become the dominant architecture for image and video generation, creating growing demand for efficient DiT serving. Existing systems assign each request a fixed parallel configuration throughout its lifetime. However, DiT workloads exhibit substantial heterogeneity across requests, execution stages, and system conditions, making static parallelism inefficient and often leading to poor GPU utilization and degraded service quality. This paper argues that DiT serving should treat GPU parallelism as a first-class schedulable resource. We present GF-DiT, a policy-programmable runtime for elastic DiT serving that dynamically adapts the parallelism of running requests according to workload demands and service objectives. GF-DiT introduces an asynchronous execution abstraction that decomposes requests into independently schedulable trajectory tasks and enables online GPU reallocation. To make elastic parallelism practical, GF-DiT further proposes group-free collectives, a lightweight communication abstraction that supports low-overhead online formation and reconfiguration of arbitrary execution groups. We implement GF-DiT in vLLM-Omni and evaluate it on representative image and video diffusion workloads. Compared with fixed-pipeline execution with static parallelism, GF-DiT improves throughput by up to 6.01$\times$, reduces mean latency by up to 95%, lowers SLO violation rates by up to 90%, and reduces communication-group setup overhead from 778 ms to approximately 60 $μ$s.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GF-DiT：为扩散Transformer服务调度并行性
扩散Transformer（DiT）已成为图像和视频生成的主流架构，催生了对高效DiT服务日益增长的需求。现有系统在请求生命周期内为其分配固定并行配置。然而，DiT工作负载在请求、执行阶段和系统条件上表现出显著异构性，静态并行性效率低下，常导致GPU利用率低下和服务质量下降。 本文主张DiT服务应将GPU并行性视为可调度的首要资源。我们提出了GF-DiT，一种面向弹性DiT服务的策略可编程运行时，可根据工作负载需求和服务目标动态调整运行请求的并行性。GF-DiT引入异步执行抽象，将请求分解为可独立调度的轨迹任务，并支持在线GPU重新分配。为使弹性并行性实用，GF-DiT进一步提出组无关集体通信，一种轻量级通信抽象，支持任意执行组的低开销在线形成与重构。 我们在vLLM-Omni中实现GF-DiT，并在代表性图像和视频扩散工作负载上进行评估。与固定流水线和静态并行性相比，GF-DiT吞吐量提升最高达6.01倍，平均延迟降低最高达95%，SLO违规率降低最高达90%，通信组设置开销从778毫秒降至约60微秒。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the heterogeneity of DiT workloads and inefficiencies of static GPU parallelism, GF-DiT introduces a policy-programmable runtime that dynamically adjusts parallelism per request based on workload and service goals. It employs asynchronous execution to break requests into schedulable trajectory tasks and enables online GPU reallocation, complemented by group-free collectives for low-overhead communication group management. Evaluated on image and video diffusion tasks, GF-DiT achieves up to 6.01x higher throughput, 95% lower mean latency, 90% fewer SLO violations, and reduces communication setup overhead from 778 ms to 60 μs.
为应对DiT工作负载的异构性与静态并行配置的低效问题，GF-DiT提出一种可编程运行时，动态调整运行请求的GPU并行度。其通过异步执行模型将请求拆分为可独立调度的轨迹任务，并支持在线GPU重分配，结合轻量级通信抽象“组无关集体通信”实现低开销的执行组动态重组。在图像与视频扩散任务中，GF-DiT实现最高6.01倍吞吐量提升、95%平均延迟降低、90% SLO违规率下降，且通信组设置开销从778毫秒降至约60微秒。
SPARC: Reliable Spatial Annotations from Robot Demonstrations at Scale
Authors: Nils Blank, Paul Mattes, Maximilian Xiling Li, Jakub Suliga, Thomas Roth, Moritz Reuss, Pankhuri Vanjani, Rudolf Lioutikov
First: 2026-06-11T15:46:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T15:46:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This work introduces Spatial Annotations from Robot Demonstrations with Reliability Calibration (SPARC), a risk-aware framework that automatically labels robot demonstrations with structured spatial annotations and assigns each annotation a reliability score. Structured spatial annotations, such as bounding boxes, object trajectories, and manipulation phase labels, benefit a broad range of robotics applications from training grounded robot policies and embodied foundation models to motion planning and hierarchical task composition. Existing automated pipelines generate such annotations at scale but provide no reliable quality signal: detector confidence is poorly calibrated for annotation correctness, forcing a choice between accepting noisy labels or discarding useful samples. In contrast to existing automated pipelines, SPARC leverages the spatio-temporal structure inherent to robot tasks to generate a reliability signal, reducing noisy labels and retaining more useful samples. We further introduce Interaction-Aware Bench (IA-Bench), a benchmark that measures model accuracy in grounding the locations of interacted objects in robot demonstrations. On 1.7k human-annotated demonstrations spanning diverse embodiments and scenarios, SPARC significantly outperforms detection-only baselines in localization accuracy while retaining three times more samples at high-precision operating points. Our experiments demonstrate that models finetuned on our annotations achieve state-of-the-art results on object-grounding and pointing benchmarks among similarly sized models, while remaining competitive on broader spatial-reasoning suites without manually verified or annotated training data. Furthermore, policies trained on SPARC-generated annotations outperform baselines in cluttered, visually ambiguous real-world scenes. Code, data, and models are available at intuitive-robots.github.io/sparc-labeling.
中文标题/摘要

Unlike existing pipelines that lack quality signals, SPARC leverages spatio-temporal task structure to calibrate annotation confidence, reducing noise while preserving useful samples.
