为解决机器人领域大规模空间标注缺乏可靠质量评估的问题，SPARC提出一种风险感知框架，自动从机器人演示中生成结构化空间标注（如边界框和操作阶段标签），并为每个标注赋予可靠性评分。与现有仅依赖检测置信度的自动化流程不同，SPARC利用机器人任务固有的时空结构生成可信可靠性信号，有效减少噪声标注同时保留更多有用样本。在1.7k人工标注演示数据上，通过Interaction-Aware Bench评估，SPARC在定位精度上显著优于仅检测的基线方法，并在高精度操作点保留三倍样本量。在标注数据上微调的模型在物体定位和指针任务上优于同类规模模型，且在无需人工验证标注的广泛空间推理任务中仍保持竞争力；基于SPARC标注训练的策略在真实复杂场景中表现优于基线方法。
Fourier Multi-Component and Multi-Layer Neural Networks: Unlocking High-Frequency Potential
Authors: Shijun Zhang, Hongkai Zhao, Yimin Zhong, Haomin Zhou
First: 2025-02-26T09:12:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T15:45:06+00:00
Comments: Our code and implementation details are available at https://github.com/ShijunZhangMath/FMMNN
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
The architecture of a neural network and the choice of its activation function are both fundamental to its performance. Equally important is ensuring that these two elements are well matched, as their alignment is key to effective representation and learning. In this paper, we introduce the Fourier Multi-Component and Multi-Layer Neural Network (FMMNN), a model that combines sine-type activations with the multi-component and multi-layer structure of MMNNs. In an FMMNN, each component is represented as a trainable linear combination of fixed random sine-type basis functions, while multi-layer composition generates more complex and adaptive high-frequency features. We establish that FMMNNs retain exponential expressive power for function approximation even under a low-rank architectural structure. We also analyze the optimization landscape of FMMNNs and find it to be substantially more favorable than that of standard fully connected neural networks, especially for high-frequency targets. In addition, we propose a scaled random initialization method for the first-layer weights in FMMNNs, which accelerates training and improves final performance when sufficient samples are available. Extensive numerical experiments support our theoretical insights, showing that FMMNNs achieve strong accuracy and favorable convergence behavior on oscillatory function-approximation benchmarks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：傅里叶多分量多层神经网络：释放高频潜力
神经网络的架构及其激活函数的选择对其性能至关重要，二者需良好匹配，以实现有效的表示与学习。本文提出傅里叶多分量多层神经网络（FMMNN），结合正弦型激活函数与多分量多层结构。在FMMNN中，每个分量表示为固定随机正弦基函数的可训练线性组合，多层组合生成更复杂且自适应的高频特征。我们证明FMMNN在低秩架构下仍保持函数逼近的指数表达能力。同时分析其优化景观，发现其显著优于标准全连接网络，尤其对高频目标。此外，我们提出FMMNN第一层权重的缩放随机初始化方法，可加速训练并提升充足样本下的最终性能。大量数值实验验证理论见解，表明FMMNN在振荡函数逼近基准上表现优异。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to better capture high-frequency patterns in function approximation, this paper proposes the Fourier Multi-Component and Multi-Layer Neural Network (FMMNN), which combines sine-type activations with a multi-component, multi-layer architecture. Each component is modeled as a trainable linear combination of fixed random sine basis functions, enabling the network to generate complex, adaptive high-frequency features through layered composition. Theoretical analysis confirms that FMMNNs maintain exponential expressive power even under low-rank constraints, and their optimization landscape is significantly more favorable than standard fully connected networks for high-frequency tasks. Empirical results demonstrate that FMMNNs achieve superior accuracy and faster convergence on oscillatory benchmarks, especially when initialized with a scaled random method for the first layer.
为更有效地逼近高频函数，本文提出傅里叶多分量多层神经网络（FMMNN），结合正弦型激活函数与多分量多层结构。每个分量由固定随机正弦基函数的可训练线性组合表示，多层堆叠可生成自适应高频特征。理论分析表明，FMMNN在低秩结构下仍保持指数级表达能力，其优化景观对高频目标比标准全连接网络更优。实验结果显示，FMMNN在振荡函数逼近任务中精度更高、收敛更快，尤其在采用第一层缩放随机初始化时表现更佳。
Budget-Constrained Step-Level Diffusion Caching
Authors: Mingkun Lei, Tong Zhao, Liangyu Yuan, Chi Zhang
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-11T15:45:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T15:45:05+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Step-level caching accelerates diffusion models by exploiting temporal redundancy across denoising steps. Existing methods make per-step cache decisions using threshold-based heuristics, without directly optimizing for final output quality. As a result, their inference latency varies across inputs and is difficult to control at deployment. In this work, we propose BudCache, which inverts this formulation: rather than letting per-step error thresholds dictate the runtime cost, we fix the compute budget in advance and search for the cache policy that best preserves the final output. To tackle the combinatorial complexity of step selection, we combine Simulated Annealing with deterministic Hill Climbing. This offline search identifies high-quality cache policies within minutes and introduces no online search or thresholding overhead during inference. When the compute budget is very tight, we further introduce cache-aware schedule alignment, which adapts the time discretization to the selected cache policy to reduce cache-induced trajectory mismatch. Experiments on FLUX.1-dev and Wan2.1 show that BudCache achieves better generation quality than heuristic caching baselines under the same inference budgets. Code is available at https://github.com/Westlake-AGI-Lab/BudCache
中文标题/摘要
标题：预算受限的步级扩散缓存
步级缓存通过利用去噪步骤间的时序冗余加速扩散模型。现有方法使用基于阈值的启发式规则进行每步缓存决策，未直接优化最终输出质量。因此，其推理延迟因输入而异，难以在部署时控制。本文提出BudCache，反转该范式：不再由每步误差阈值决定运行时成本，而是预先固定计算预算，搜索能最好保留最终输出的缓存策略。为应对步选择的组合复杂性，我们结合模拟退火与确定性爬山算法。该离线搜索可在数分钟内识别高质量缓存策略，且推理时无需在线搜索或阈值开销。当计算预算非常紧张时，我们进一步引入缓存感知的调度对齐，通过调整时间离散化以适应所选缓存策略，减少缓存引起的轨迹不匹配。在FLUX.1-dev和Wan2.1上的实验表明，BudCache在相同推理预算下比启发式缓存基线获得更优生成质量。代码见https://github.com/Westlake-AGI-Lab/BudCache
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to control inference latency while preserving output quality in diffusion models, this work introduces BudCache, a budget-constrained caching method that optimizes cache policies offline to maximize final generation quality under fixed compute budgets, rather than using heuristic thresholds per denoising step. It employs a combination of Simulated Annealing and deterministic Hill Climbing to efficiently search the combinatorial space of step selections, enabling high-quality cache policies to be found within minutes without any online overhead. Additionally, it introduces cache-aware schedule alignment to reduce trajectory mismatch under tight budgets. Experiments on FLUX.1-dev and Wan2.1 demonstrate that BudCache outperforms heuristic baselines in generation quality while maintaining the same inference budget.
为在保持生成质量的同时控制推理延迟，本文提出BudCache方法，通过在固定计算预算下优化步级缓存策略以最大化最终输出质量。该方法采用模拟退火与确定性爬山算法组合进行离线搜索，避免在线阈值判断和开销。在FLUX.1-dev和Wan2.1上的实验表明，BudCache在相同推理预算下优于启发式缓存基线，并在计算资源紧张时通过缓存感知的时间对齐进一步提升效率。
NavWAM: A Navigation World Action Model for Goal-Conditioned Visual Navigation
Authors: Daichi Azuma, Taiki Miyanishi, Koya Sakamoto, Shuhei Kurita, Yaonan Zhu, Petr Khrapchenkov, Motoaki Kawanabe, Yusuke Iwasawa, Yutaka Matsuo
First: 2026-06-11T15:44:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T15:44:36+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://dachii-azm.github.io/navwam/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Goal-conditioned visual navigation requires a robot to act under partial observability by anticipating how its motion will change the future egocentric view and whether that change brings it closer to the goal. Navigation world models provide such visual foresight, but they remain prediction modules that require an external planner to convert predicted futures into closed-loop control. We propose Navigation World Action Model (NavWAM), a diffusion-transformer policy that turns navigation world-model prediction into executable action by representing future observations, goal-progress values, and action chunks in a shared latent sequence. By learning future prediction jointly with the action and value targets that determine closed-loop behavior, NavWAM makes visual foresight directly usable for robot control. We build NavWAM through simulation pretraining and real-robot adaptation, and evaluate it on image-goal navigation against planning-based world models and a representative direct navigation policy. Across offline benchmarks and closed-loop real-robot deployment, NavWAM improves over planning-based world-model baselines in our evaluations while using the default policy mode without CEM-style action search. Project page: https://dachii-azm.github.io/navwam/
中文标题/摘要
标题：NavWAM：一种面向目标条件视觉导航的导航世界动作模型
目标条件视觉导航要求机器人在部分可观测性下行动，需预判自身运动如何改变未来的第一人称视角，以及该变化是否使其更接近目标。导航世界模型可提供此类视觉前瞻，但它们仍为预测模块，需外部规划器将预测未来转化为闭环控制。我们提出导航世界动作模型（NavWAM），一种扩散-Transformer策略，通过共享潜在序列表示未来观测、目标进度值和动作块，将导航世界模型预测转化为可执行动作。通过联合学习未来预测与决定闭环行为的动作和价值目标，NavWAM使视觉前瞻可直接用于机器人控制。我们通过仿真预训练和真实机器人适应构建NavWAM，并在图像目标导航任务中与基于规划的世界模型及代表性直接导航策略进行评估。在离线基准测试和闭环真实机器人部署中，NavWAM在无需CEM式动作搜索的默认策略模式下，优于基于规划的世界模型基线。项目页面：https://dachii-azm.github.io/navwam/

Motivated by the need for robots to navigate visually under partial observability while anticipating future visual changes toward goals, this paper introduces NavWAM, a diffusion-transformer policy that directly converts world-model predictions into executable actions. NavWAM jointly learns to predict future observations, goal-progress values, and action chunks within a shared latent sequence, enabling closed-loop control without external planners. Evaluated against planning-based world models and direct navigation policies in both simulation and real-world deployment, NavWAM consistently outperforms baselines without requiring CEM-style action search.
为解决机器人在部分可观测环境下基于视觉导航时需直接利用预测信息进行控制的问题，本文提出NavWAM，一种将未来视觉预测、目标进度与动作块统一编码于共享潜在序列的扩散-Transformer策略。通过联合学习预测与控制目标，NavWAM无需外部规划器或CEM式动作搜索即可实现闭环导航。在仿真与真实机器人部署的图像目标导航任务中，NavWAM在多个基准测试中优于基于规划的世界模型和直接导航基线。
Point-Wise Geometry-Aware Transformer for Partial-to-Full Point Cloud Registration in Computer-Assisted Surgery
Authors: Siyu Zhou, Zhongliang Jiang
First: 2026-06-11T15:37:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T15:37:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Partial-to-full registration remains challenging due to varying overlap ratios, fluctuating point densities, and the presence of noise. While transformers have shown strong potential for point cloud processing, prior methods typically confine them to global context aggregation, overlooking fine-grained local geometry crucial for accurate correspondence. We propose \emph{GAPR-Net}, a learning-based point cloud registration framework with a coarse-to-fine architecture that combines convolution and transformer modules, in which local and global information is fused between the partial and full point clouds using a cross-attention mechanism. To achieve this, a transformation-invariant point-wise geometric feature representation is proposed, which can robustly capture relative geometric features for individual points with respect to their neighboring points. To evaluate the effectiveness of the proposed approach, experiments are conducted on four geometrically distinct bones, including the tibia, femur, pelvis, and thoracic cartilage. The overall registration recall reaches 94.2\%, the method results in a low RMSE of 1.992 mm and $R^2$ values of 0.908 and 0.974 for rotation and translation, respectively. The results demonstrate that the proposed method effectively addresses the partial-to-full point cloud registration problem. The proposed method enables highly accurate 3D point cloud registration using partial observation, providing a critical foundation for precise surgical navigation and robotic interventions in computer-assisted surgery. The code will be accessed after the double-blind review process.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于计算机辅助手术中部分到完整点云配准的点感知几何感知变换器
由于重叠比例变化、点密度波动及噪声存在，部分到完整配准仍具挑战性。尽管变换器在点云处理中展现出强大潜力，但现有方法通常仅限于全局上下文聚合，忽视了对精确对应至关重要的细粒度局部几何信息。我们提出GAPR-Net，一种基于学习的点云配准框架，采用粗到精架构，结合卷积与变换器模块，通过交叉注意力机制在部分与完整点云间融合局部与全局信息。为此，我们提出一种变换不变的点级几何特征表示，可稳健地捕捉每个点相对于其邻近点的相对几何特征。为评估所提方法的有效性，我们在四类几何结构各异的骨骼（胫骨、股骨、骨盆、胸肋软骨）上进行实验。整体配准召回率达94.2%，方法获得低RMSE（1.992 mm）及旋转与平移的R²值分别为0.908和0.974。结果表明，所提方法有效解决了部分到完整点云配准问题，实现了基于部分观测的高精度三维点云配准，为计算机辅助手术中的精准手术导航与机器人干预提供了关键基础。代码将在双盲评审后开放。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of partial-to-full point cloud registration due to variable overlap, density, and noise, the authors propose GAPR-Net, a coarse-to-fine learning framework that integrates convolution and transformer modules with a cross-attention mechanism to fuse local and global geometry between partial and full point clouds. A transformation-invariant point-wise geometric feature representation enables robust local feature capture. Evaluated on four bone types, the method achieves 94.2% registration recall, 1.992 mm RMSE, and R² values of 0.908 and 0.974 for rotation and translation, demonstrating high accuracy and reliability for surgical navigation applications.
为解决计算机辅助手术中因重叠率、点密度波动和噪声导致的局部到全局点云配准难题，本文提出GAPR-Net框架，结合卷积与Transformer模块，采用跨注意力机制融合局部与全局几何信息，并引入变换不变的点级几何特征表示以捕捉点与其邻域的相对几何关系。在胫骨、股骨、骨盆和胸软骨四个几何结构各异的骨骼上实验，该方法实现94.2%的配准召回率，RMSE为1.992毫米，旋转与平移的R²值分别为0.908和0.974，表明其在真实手术场景下具备高精度、鲁棒的配准能力。
DecompSR: A dataset for decomposed analyses of compositional multihop spatial reasoning
Authors: Lachlan McPheat, Navdeep Kaur, Robert Blackwell, Alessandra Russo, Anthony G. Cohn, Pranava Madhyastha
First: 2025-11-04T14:57:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T15:36:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce DecompSR, decomposed spatial reasoning, a large benchmark dataset (over 5m datapoints) and generation framework designed to analyse compositional spatial reasoning ability. The generation of DecompSR allows users to independently vary several aspects of compositionality, namely: productivity (reasoning depth), substitutivity (entity and linguistic variability), overgeneralisation (input order, distractors) and systematicity (novel linguistic elements). DecompSR is built procedurally in a manner which makes it is correct by construction, which is independently verified using a symbolic solver to guarantee the correctness of the dataset. DecompSR is comprehensively benchmarked across a host of Large Language Models (LLMs) where we show that LLMs struggle with productive and systematic generalisation in spatial reasoning tasks whereas they are more robust to linguistic variation. DecompSR provides a provably correct and rigorous benchmarking dataset with a novel ability to independently vary the degrees of several key aspects of compositionality, allowing for robust and fine-grained probing of the compositional reasoning abilities of LLMs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DecompSR：用于分解分析组合式多跳空间推理的数据集
我们引入DecompSR，一种分解式空间推理的大规模基准数据集（超过500万个数据点）及生成框架，旨在分析组合式空间推理能力。DecompSR的生成允许用户独立调整组合性的多个方面：生产力（推理深度）、可替换性（实体与语言变体）、过度泛化（输入顺序、干扰项）和系统性（新颖语言元素）。DecompSR以程序化方式构建，确保其构造正确，并通过符号求解器独立验证，以保证数据集的正确性。DecompSR在多种大型语言模型（LLMs）上进行了全面基准测试，结果显示LLMs在空间推理任务中难以实现生产力和系统性的泛化，但对语言变体更具鲁棒性。DecompSR提供了一个可证明正确的严谨基准数据集，具备独立调节组合性若干关键方面程度的新型能力，从而实现对LLMs组合推理能力的稳健且精细的探测。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to rigorously evaluate how large language models handle compositional spatial reasoning, the authors introduce DecompSR, a large-scale benchmark dataset with over 5 million datapoints. The dataset is procedurally generated to allow independent manipulation of four key compositional dimensions: productivity (reasoning depth), substitutivity (entity and linguistic variability), overgeneralisation (input order and distractors), and systematicity (novel linguistic elements). Each instance is guaranteed correct by construction via symbolic verification. Experimental results across multiple LLMs reveal that models struggle with productive and systematic generalisation but perform more robustly under linguistic variation, highlighting a gap in their compositional reasoning capabilities.
为深入探究大语言模型在组合式空间推理中的能力，作者构建了DecompSR数据集，包含超过500万条数据，可独立调节组合性关键维度，包括生成深度、实体与语言变体、输入顺序干扰及新颖语言元素。该数据集通过程序化生成并经符号求解器验证正确性，确保无误。实验表明，大语言模型在需要生成深度和系统性泛化的问题上表现不佳，但在语言变体方面更具鲁棒性，揭示其组合推理能力的关键缺陷。
